### [1] [Hubble tension: the shape wall](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21244)
*Miguel A. Sabogal,Luis A. Escamilla,Davide Pedrotti,Edvig Selimaj,Sunny Vagnozzi*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文针对哈勃常数张力（Hubble tension）的晚期宇宙学解决方案，指出除了已知的“归一化墙”（normalization wall）外，还存在一个约百分之几量级的“形状墙”（shape wall）。利用高斯过程（Gaussian Processes）对无量纲膨胀率E(z)进行非参数重建，结合PantheonPlus Ia型超新星和DESI DR2重子声学振荡（BAO）数据，发现晚期物理对H₀的最大提升仅约≲2%，远不足以解决需要≳8%提升的哈勃张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 哈勃张力是当前宇宙学中最突出的危机之一，标准的“no-go定理”认为晚期解必须面对BAO对H₀r_d乘积约束形成的归一化墙。然而晚期解不仅受归一化约束，还受膨胀历史形状，即无量纲膨胀率E(z)的约束。作者旨在量化这一由E(z)形状约束形成的“形状墙”，以评估晚期物理（无论单独使用还是与早期新物理结合）解决哈勃张力的可行性上限。

Method: 作者证明了在固定r_d和声学角尺度θ_s的条件下，H₀的增加必须匹配无量纲距离积分I的同等分数增加（δH₀/H₀≈δI/I）。基于这一关系，利用高斯过程对E(z)进行非参数重建，使用最新的无锚定Ia型超新星（PantheonPlus SNeIa）和BAO（DESI DR2）数据，对晚期E(z)变体所能提供的H₀最大提升进行量化。

Result: 在最保守的分析中（使用PantheonPlus SNeIa与DESI DR2 BAO数据），发现H₀的最大分数增加仅为≲2%，远低于解决哈勃张力所需的≳8%增幅。该形状墙即使在存在早期新物理的情况下依然成立，限制了固定θ_s时晚期E(z)修改所能贡献的H₀最大增加量。

Conclusion: 晚期宇宙学修改，无论单独采用还是与早期新物理结合，不仅要面对著名的归一化墙，还要面对严格的百分数级别的形状墙。这为晚期解解决哈勃张力设置了几乎不可逾越的额外障碍。

Abstract: The standard "no-go theorem" against late-time solutions to the Hubble tension is essentially a normalization wall, since Baryon Acoustic Oscillation (BAO) measurements constrain the product $H_0r_d$, with $r_d$ the sound horizon at baryon drag. However, late-time solutions (which keep $r_d$ fixed) are tightly constrained not only by the BAO normalization $H_0r_d$, but also by the shape of the expansion history, i.e. the dimensionless expansion rate $E(z) \equiv H(z)/H_0$. We show that, if $r_d$ and the acoustic angular scale $θ_s$ are fixed, an increase in $H_0$ needs to be matched by an equal fractional increase in the dimensionless distance integral $I \equiv \int dz/E(z)$: $δH_0/H_0 \simeq δI/I$. We use this to quantify the "shape wall" set by relative distance constraints on $E(z)$, which we reconstruct nonparametrically, using Gaussian Processes and the latest unanchored Type Ia Supernovae (SNeIa) and BAO data. In our most conservative analysis using PantheonPlus SNeIa and DESI DR2 BAO data, we find a maximum fractional increase in $H_0$ of $\lesssim 2\%$, falling well short of the $\gtrsim 8\%$ required to solve the tension. This shape wall holds even in the presence of early-time new physics, and limits the maximum increase in $H_0$ which can be contributed by late-time modifications to $E(z)$ at fixed $θ_s$. Therefore, late-time modifications, whether invoked alone or alongside early-time new physics, face not only the well-known normalization wall, but also a stringent percent-level shape wall.

</details>


### [2] [Enhancing dark siren cosmology via Gaussian process reconstruction of incomplete galaxy catalogs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24992)
*Matteo Tagliazucchi,Jonathan Gair,Riccardo Barbieri,Michele Moresco*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新颖框架，通过将高斯过程（Gaussian process, GP）应用于不完整星系目录的视线方向（line-of-sight, LOS）重建，来改进暗汽笛（dark siren）宇宙学。该方法在模拟数据上相比标准均匀补齐方法将哈勃常数 $H_0$ 约束精度平均提高23%至37%，在最优配置下可达66%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在利用引力波（gravitational-wave, GW）暗汽笛推断哈勃常数 $H_0$ 的标准星系目录方法中，缺失星系通常被假设为在共动体积中均匀分布，这一假设丢弃了星系红移聚簇（redshift clustering）信息，而该信息对宇宙学推断至关重要。

Method: 将视线方向星系红移分布建模为从高斯过程实现中抽取的非参数函数，并通过显式考虑巡天选择函数（selection function）的分层贝叶斯似然（hierarchical Bayesian likelihood）将其拟合到观测到的不完整星系目录上。

Result: 应用于延伸至红移 $z\leq0.4$ 的模拟引力波与星系目录时，对24%完整度星系目录，$H_0$ 约束精度平均比标准均匀补齐方法提高23%；对8%完整度目录提高37%；在GP最有效重建红移过密与欠密特征的配置中，最大改进可达66%。

Conclusion: 通过非参数高斯过程对不完整星系目录的视线方向红移分布进行重建，能够有效捕捉标准均匀补齐方法无法识别的红移过密与欠密特征，从而显著提升暗汽笛 $H_0$ 推断精度，尤其在星系目录完整度较低的条件下改进更为明显。

Abstract: We present a novel framework for improving dark siren cosmology by applying a Gaussian process (GP) to the line-of-sight (LOS) reconstruction of incomplete galaxy catalogs. In the standard galaxy catalog method for inferring the Hubble constant $H_0$ from gravitational-wave (GW) dark sirens, missing galaxies are typically assumed to follow a uniform distribution in comoving volume, an assumption that discards galaxy redshift clustering information crucial for cosmological inference. We propose instead to model the LOS galaxy redshift distribution as a non-parametric function drawn from a GP realization, which is fitted to the observed incomplete catalog via a hierarchical Bayesian likelihood that explicitly accounts for the survey selection function. Applied to mock GW and galaxy catalogs extending up to redshift $z\leq0.4$, our method yields $H_0$ constraints that are on average 23% more precise than the standard homogeneous completion when using a 24%-complete galaxy catalog, and 37% more precise for an 8%-complete catalog. The largest improvement, reaching 66%, is obtained in configurations where the GP most effectively reconstructs the redshift over- and under-density features that the homogeneous completion fails to capture.

</details>


### [3] [Slowly Rolling on a Quantum Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25004)
*Ido Ben-Dayan,Ayushi Srivastava,Amresh Verma*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文针对DESI和ACT等最新宇宙学观测显示谱指数（spectral tilt）ns的偏好值增大、从而对希格斯暴胀和Starobinski暴胀等流行模型不利的现象，提出这实际上表明数据已足够灵敏，足以探测超越树级（tree-level）模型的量子修正。作者重新引入Coleman-Weinberg有效势这一经典框架，分析此类模型的预言并进行似然分析（likelihood analysis），得到一个更稳健且更有趣的预测性框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DESI和ACT等最新宇宙学测量显示谱指数ns的偏好值有所增大，使得希格斯暴胀（Higgs inflation）和Starobinski暴胀等广受欢迎的模型受到排斥。作者认为这并非模型的失败，而是表明数据已具备足够的灵敏度去探测超越简单树级模型的量子效应。

Method: 重新引入Coleman-Weinberg有效势这一经典方法，分析包含量子修正的暴胀模型的预言，并综合最新宇宙学数据进行似然分析，以检验模型与数据的相容性。

Result: 研究表明，在Coleman-Weinberg有效势框架下构建的暴胀模型能够自然适应增大的ns偏好值，给出与DESI和ACT等最新观测数据相容的预言，从而建立起一个更稳健且具有预测性的理论框架。

Conclusion: 最新宇宙学数据对ns的敏感度提升意味着需要超越树级近似的量子修正，Coleman-Weinberg有效势框架为此提供了合适的理论基础，并使相关模型重新成为一个更有说服力和预测力的候选方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in cosmological measurements such as DESI and ACT may indicate an increase in the preferred value of the spectral tilt $n_s$ such that it disfavors rather popular models such as the Higgs or Starobinski inflation models. We argue that it actually means that the data is now sensitive enough to quantum corrections beyond simple tree-level models. Resurrecting the old theme of Coleman-Weinberg effective potential, we analyze the predictions of such models, as well as likelihood analysis, leading to a more established and interesting predictive framework.

</details>

### [4] [Oscillator-Based Processing Unit for Formant Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25191)
*Tamás Rudner-Halász,Wolfgang Porod,Gyorgy Csaba*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了如何利用振荡神经网络（oscillatory neural networks）直接处理时变波形（time-dependent waveforms），将其从复杂的信号预处理步骤中解放出来，有望为低功耗边缘人工智能（edge-AI）设备开辟新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在传统神经网络中，预处理（preprocessing）和第一层处理往往是最耗能的步骤。作者希望通过振荡神经网络减少或消除对预处理的需求，从而降低整体能耗，推动简单且高效的低功耗边缘AI设备的实现。

Method: 研究所采用的方法是展示振荡神经网络如何以最少甚至无需预处理的方式直接处理时变波形。作者利用振荡器的固有动态特性来替代传统神经网络中的信号预处理和首层特征提取。

Result: 实验结果表明，振荡神经网络能够以最小化甚至完全不需要预处理的方式直接处理时变波形信号，这一特性有望显著降低边缘AI设备的功耗。

Conclusion: 该发现为简单且高效的低功耗边缘AI设备提供了新的可能性，因为减少了最耗能的预处理和首层处理环节，可能大幅降低整体系统能耗。

Abstract: Oscillatory neural networks have been successfully applied to a number of computing problems, such as associative memories and computationally hard optimization tasks. In this paper, we show how to use oscillators to process time-dependent waveforms with minimal or no preprocessing. Since preprocessing and first-layer processing are often the most power-hungry steps in neural networks, our findings may open new doors to simple and power-efficient edge-AI devices.

</details>

### [5] [Persistence length of short homopolymeric single-stranded DNA sequences in polyvalent cations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25046)
*Balaka Mondal,D. Thirumalai*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究基于序列依赖的三位点（Three Interaction Site, TIS）模型模拟短单链DNA（single-stranded DNA, ssDNA）均聚物，计算了多价阳离子中的持续长度（persistence length, $l_p$）。在二价阳离子（Mg2+ 和 Ca2+）中，$l_p$ 与 $l_p = l_p^0 + λκ^{-1}$ 公式定量吻合；但在四价精胺（spermine）中，$l_p$ 几乎不受其浓度影响，这可能与精胺分子各向异性结构导致的磷酸基团空间位阻有关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续长度是表征聚合物链刚性的关键物理量，理解多价阳离子如何影响单链DNA的构象灵活性对于阐明DNA在细胞内（富含多价离子）的真实行为至关重要。Debye-Hückel 理论下的标准标度关系在多价阳离子体系中是否仍然适用，是亟需澄清的问题。

Method: 采用序列依赖的三位点（TIS）模型对短ssDNA均聚物（dT30 和 dA30）进行模拟，模型中通过库仑势（Coulomb potential）处理静电相互作用，同时考虑碱基堆叠（stacking）相互作用。模拟中分别考察二价阳离子（Mg2+、Ca2+）和四价阳离子（Spm4+）对持续长度的影响。

Result: 在Mg2+和Ca2+中，dT30 和 dA30 的 $l_p$ 均可用 $l_p = l_p^0 + λκ^{-1}$ 定量拟合；polyT 和 polyA 的裸持续长度 $l_p^0$ 分别为0.4 nm 和 1.1 nm。令人惊讶的是，$l_p$ 几乎不依赖于四价精胺的浓度；模拟显示结合到ssDNA上的精胺分子数量在较小值处即饱和。研究人员提出，精胺分子的各向异性结构造成体积排斥，限制了其对磷酸基团的可及性，从而定性解释了这一现象。

Conclusion: 在二价阳离子中观察到的 $l_p \propto κ^{-1}$ 关系出乎意料，因为这一关系原本是针对长柔性聚电解质（基于Debye-Hückel相互作用）推导的，却在短ssDNA体系中依然成立。精胺浓度下持续长度的近乎不变缺乏明确的理论解释，但体积排阻的定性图景可以合理化模拟结果，该预测的$l_p$对精胺的依赖性有待实验检验。

Abstract: We used simulations of short single stranded DNA (ssDNA) homopolymers, based on the sequence dependent Three Interaction Site (TIS) model, to calculate the persistence length ($l_p$) in polyvalent cations. The TIS model accounts for stacking interactions and electrostatic interactions are treated using the Coulomb potential. We find that $l_p$ for $\mathrm{dT_{30}}$ (T is thymine) and $\mathrm{dA_{30}}$ (A is adenine) is quantitatively fit using $l_p = l_p^0 + λκ^{-1}$ ($l_p^0$ is the bare persistence length, $λ$ is a dimensionless constant, and $κ$ is the inverse Debye length) in the divalent cations $\mathrm{Mg^{2+}}$ and $\mathrm{Ca^{2+}}$. The dependence of $l_p$ on $κ$ is surprising because it was derived for long flexible polyelectrolytes in which the charges interact via the Debye-Hückel potential. The $l_p^0$ values are 0.4 nm and 1.1 nm for polyT and polyA, respectively. Strikingly, $l_p$ is almost independent of the tetravalent spermine concentration. There is no clear theoretical explanation although simulations suggest that the number of spermine molecules that bind to the ssDNA saturates at a small value. A qualitative picture, based on the restrictions of access to the phosphate groups due to volume exclusion of the anisotropic structure of $\mathrm{Spm^{4+}}$, rationalizes the simulation results. The predicted dependence of $l_p$ in spermine awaits experimental test.

</details>


### [6] [Micromechanical statistical model links induced nematic order to mechanical response in fiber networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25125)
*Ehud Haimov,Yoni Koren,Ayelet Lesman,Jonathan Selinger,Yair Shokef*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文发展了一种连续介质微观力学理论，将纤维细胞外基质中的载荷诱导取向序与力学响应在空间各点联系起来，并应用于单轴拉伸与收缩细胞两种情形，揭示了取向序与纤维致密化的高度正相关关系，以及屈曲效应对向列序传播距离的控制作用，且通过非仿射离散纤维网络模拟验证了理论的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可收缩细胞和外部载荷会远距离重构纤维细胞外基质，使纤维取向排列并致密化，显著影响伤口愈合、血管生成和肿瘤侵袭等生物过程。现有研究缺乏能将载荷诱导的纤维取向序与网络随后表现出的力学响应在材料空间各点相互联系的理论框架。

Method: 该理论将网络用纤维取向的概率密度函数进行统计描述，假设网络仿射变形（affine deformation），从而将单纤维的本构关系带入网络应力，并通过力学平衡自洽地确定变形场。理论同时允许几何非线性（geometrical nonlinearity）和材料非线性（material nonlinearity），以贴近生物学真实条件。理论应用于二维网络单轴拉伸，归结为一个单一的各向异性参数，控制取向分布、向列序（nematic order）、泊松比和纤维致密化。

Result: 理论揭示诱导序与致密化高度正相关，在单轴拉伸情形下二者收敛到一条几乎普适的曲线，与单纤维刚度行为无关。对于收缩细胞情形，屈曲（buckling）控制向列取向序和致密化的传播距离，变形随距离呈代数衰减，并求解了幂律指数对屈曲后单纤维刚度降低的依赖关系。理论通过与非仿射离散纤维网络模拟对比获得验证。

Conclusion: 该连续介质微观力学理论提供了一个在生物相关条件下连接纤维取向序与力学响应的统一框架，能够解释实验中观察到的远距离纤维重构现象，其对屈曲控制传播机制的揭示有助于理解伤口愈合、血管生成和肿瘤侵袭等过程中的组织力学调控。

Abstract: Contractile cells and external loads reorganize the fibrous extracellular matrix, aligning and compacting fibers over distances far exceeding a cell's size, strongly affecting bioprocesses such as wound healing, angiogenesis and tumor invasion. We develop a continuum micromechanical theory that links, at every material point, the load-induced orientational order to the mechanical response that the reoriented network then exhibits. The network is described statistically, by the probability density of fiber orientations, and deforms affinely, so that a single-fiber stress-strain law is carried into the network stress, with the deformation set self-consistently by mechanical equilibrium. Critical to realistic biological relevant conditions, this theory allows both geometrical and material nonlinearities. Applied to a two-dimensional network under uniaxial stretch, the theory collapses onto a single anisotropy parameter that governs the orientation distribution, the nematic order, the Poisson ratio, and the densification of fibers. Our theory reveals that induced order and densification are highly positively correlated, and in the case of uniaxial stretch they collapse onto a nearly universal curve, independent of the single-fiber stiffness behavior. For a contracting cell, we find that buckling controls how far nematic orientational order and densification propagate. We find an algebraic decay of deformations with distance and solve for the dependence of the power-law exponent on the buckled-reduced stiffness of a single fiber. We validate our theory by comparison with non-affine discrete fiber-network simulations.

</details>


### [7] [Dynamical and conformational behavior of a polymer in a crowded solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25242)
*Setarehalsadat Changizrezaei,Colin Denniston*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文通过朗之万动力学（Langevin dynamics, LD）和格子-玻尔兹曼分子动力学（lattice-Boltzmann molecular dynamics, LBMD）模拟，研究了含球形胶体拥挤剂（colloidal crowder）的流体中聚合物的结构与动力学。研究发现胶体尺寸比 R/r 决定压实机制，而扩散行为在 LD 与 LBMD 中呈现截然不同的标度规律，并通过 Rouse 模分析识别出三种动力学区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细胞等生物环境中充满高浓度的可移动大分子（胶体拥挤剂），理解拥挤效应对聚合物构象和动力学的影响对生物物理研究具有重要意义；以往研究多集中于静态或固定拥挤剂，缺乏对流体动力学相互作用（hydrodynamic interactions）的系统考察。

Method: 采用两种模拟方法对比研究：不含长程流体动力相互作用的朗之万动力学（LD）和纳入长程流体动力相互作用的格子-玻尔兹曼分子动力学（LBMD）。系统改变胶体与单体的尺寸比 R/r 和体积分数 φ，分析聚合物压实机制、扩散系数及 Rouse 模动力学。

Result: 体积分数的增加导致聚合物压实，其机制取决于 R/r：小胶体主要改变短波长构象并维持自回避行走（self-avoiding walk）标度，而大胶体降低有效长波长 Flory 指数，符合限制团（confinement-blob）图像。LD 中扩散随拥挤度快速下降并依赖 R/r，可用含 ln(1+R/r) 的标度描述；LBMD 中扩散呈依赖浓度的指数衰减，受流体动力学耦合主导。Rouse 模分析揭示低体积分数标度崩坏、中等密度呈现 Zimm 行为、高密度下 LB 出现流体动力学屏蔽而 LD 呈限制主导动力学三种区域。

Conclusion: 拥挤剂尺寸比 R/r 与流体动力学相互作用的耦合共同决定聚合物行为；LD 与 LBMD 在扩散和动力学上的显著差异表明，长程流体动力学在拥挤环境中的聚合物研究中不可忽略。

Abstract: We investigate the structure and dynamics of a polymer in a fluid containing mobile spherical colloidal crowders of radius $R$. We compare and contrast the behavior with Langevin dynamics (LD) and lattice--Boltzmann molecular dynamics (LBMD), the latter incorporating long-range hydrodynamic interactions. Both the colloid size relative to the monomer radius $r$ and the volume fraction $φ$ are varied to determine how crowding modifies polymer behavior. Increasing volume fraction induces polymer compaction, with the mechanism strongly dependent on the size ratio $R/r$. Small colloids primarily modify the short-wavelength polymer conformation, causing self-avoiding-walk-like behavior to persist to shorter length scales, whereas large colloids reduce the effective long-wavelength Flory exponent, indicating degraded solvent quality consistent with a confinement-blob picture. Polymer diffusion exhibits distinct behavior in LD and LBMD. In LD, diffusion decreases rapidly and depends strongly on $R/r$; a phenomenological scaling involving $\ln(1+R/r)$ captures this size dependence, and additional scaling with $R_g$ reduces scatter, indicating polymer-scale correlations induced by crowding. In contrast, LBMD diffusion follows an effective-medium-like exponential dependence on concentration, governed by hydrodynamic coupling. Rouse-mode analysis identifies three regimes: scaling breakdown at low volume fraction, Zimm-like behavior at intermediate density in both LD and LB, and at high density hydrodynamic screening in LB with confinement-dominated dynamics in LD.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 铁电液滴在超疏水表面的带电可理解为一种弯曲诱导接触起电（splay-induced contact electrification），其目的是最小化积累电荷；该现象依赖于多种难以量化的实验条件，起电与极化效应在不同条件下互为补充地主导液滴行为。

Abstract: Ferroelectric sessile droplets on a superhydrophobic surface can be put into motion by highly non-uniform electric fields, in a direction that depends on the field sign. This indicates that droplets carry a non-vanishing electrostatic charge, which we interpret as acquired through contact electrification at the interface with the superhydrophobic substrate. This phenomenon is not observed in the presence of a uniform or quasi-uniform electric field, suggesting that droplets charging is favored in highly nonuniform fields able to induce localized distortions of the ferroelectric polarization, in turn producing localized charge accumulation sites. Electrostatic charging is thus understood as a splay-induced contact electrification and interpreted as a strategy to minimize the accumulated charge. Electrophoretic-like motion is observed in about 30% of the experiments, meaning that splay-induced charging requires the proper combination of conditions such as droplets size, distance from the field source, freshness of the substrate coating and other experimental details that are not easily quantified. When charging does not occur, effects related to droplet polarization such as dielectrophoresis, ejection of polarized fluid jets and electrostriction-like deformations are observed.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 该研究为密度增强运动性规则下非平衡系统的相分离和形态选择提供了系统表征，结果对受类似群居感应机制调控的生物系统及表现出相关空间组织形式的生物系统具有借鉴意义，并提出了未来研究方向。

Abstract: Using Langevin dynamics simulations, I investigate nonequilibrium systems of particles following a density-enhanced motility (DEM) rule: particles are passive below a critical local density and active above it. This mechanism represents an inverse of the conventional quorum-sensing rule. I explore specifically quorums much larger than particle size and at two levels of activity. Above critical values of the density, quorum size, and activity, the system undergoes phase separation into low- and high-energy regions. The passive particles organize into distinct spatial patterns, including holes, stripes, and labyrinthine structures, while the active particles form a gas. I characterize the resulting steady states and identify the qualitative mechanisms governing the selection of different morphological regimes. I further discuss the relevance of these results to biological systems governed by analogous quorum-sensing mechanisms and to biological systems exhibiting related forms of spatial organization. Finally, I present directions for future investigation.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬球状态方程在纳米受限体系中被普遍认为失效，但其失效的根本原因尚未被清晰阐明。作者旨在揭示这一失效的物理本质，并提供一个通用修正方案以恢复解析热力学框架的适用范围。

Method: 提出一种封闭形式、参数自由的映射关系，将表观堆积分数（apparent packing fraction）映射为有效堆积分数（effective packing fraction），以此校正Carnahan-Starling状态方程在纳米受限条件下的预测。将该映射应用于重叠锚定硬球液滴（overlapping anchored hard-sphere droplets）体系中计算热力学量。

Result: 该映射成功恢复了Carnahan-Starling状态方程在纳米受限硬球体系中的准确性，包括自由能、壁面压力、化学势及大空腔表面响应等全部解析热力学量。在生物分子力生成相关的重叠锚定硬球液滴体系中，计算得到的去混自由能（unmixing free energy）和完整距离依赖力分布（distance-dependent force profiles）在从稀溶液到高于体相冻结转变温度的范围内均与模拟定量吻合。

Conclusion: 纳米受限下硬球状态方程的失效本质是几何效应而非热力学效应，通过简单的堆积分数重映射即可恢复整个解析热力学框架的适用性，扩展了硬球状态方程在受限体系和软物质问题中的预测能力。

Abstract: Equations of state accurately describe hard-sphere thermodynamics but are generally considered to fail under nanoscale confinement. We show that this failure is geometric rather than thermodynamic: a closed-form, parameter-free mapping from apparent to effective packing fraction restores the accuracy of the Carnahan-Starling equation of state and, with it, the entire analytical thermodynamic framework, including free energies, wall pressures, chemical potentials, and the large-cavity surface response. Applied to overlapping anchored hard-sphere droplets, a geometry inherent to biomolecular force generation, it yields unmixing free energies and full distance-dependent force profiles in quantitative agreement with simulations from the dilute regime to above the bulk freezing transition.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: DLPC产生较规则的纹理，在高覆盖率下更容易促进螺旋解缠绕（helix unwinding），表明其具有更空间均匀的集体锚定；DOPC界面则展现更大的形态异质性，源于较长尾链的强局部锚定与顺式不饱和引起的组织不均匀性。高脂质浓度下两种体系的荧光探针迁移率均受到强烈限制。

Conclusion: 脂质酰基链结构通过影响局部相互作用、集体界面组织和分子迁移率来调控胆甾相锚定行为，为设计响应型液晶界面提供了设计原则。

Abstract: Liquid crystal-based biosensors exploit the sensitivity of interfacial anchoring to molecular adsorption. Cholesteric liquid crystals are especially useful because their helical structure supports multiple optically distinct textures that evolve with anchoring strength. Here, we compare saturated 1,2-dilauroyl-sn-glycero-3-phosphocholine (DLPC) and unsaturated 1,2-dioleoyl-sn-glycero-3-phosphocholine (DOPC) monolayers to determine how lipid acyl chain structure governs interfacial organization and director alignment. Mapping stripe spacing across lipid concentration, mixing ratio, cholesteric pitch, and confinement shows that the transition from fingerprint textures toward homeotropic alignment depends on both lipid structure and collective interfacial organization. DLPC produces comparatively regular textures and more readily promotes helix unwinding at high coverage, consistent with more spatially uniform collective anchoring. DOPC-containing interfaces show greater morphological heterogeneity, consistent with strong local anchoring from longer tails together with less uniform organization arising from cis-unsaturation. FRAP measurements provide complementary information on fluorescent-probe mobility, which becomes strongly restricted at high lipid concentration for both systems. Variations in pitch and film thickness further modulate the response, producing coexisting regular, distorted, and lipid-enriched textures. Together, these results show that lipid acyl chain structure regulates cholesteric anchoring through its effects on local interactions, collective interfacial organization, and mobility, providing design principles for responsive liquid-crystal interfaces.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 塑料的酶法回收受到半晶球晶的制约，这类球晶对酶解聚合具有抗性。由于废弃物质量和处理历史不同，大量材料实际以这类球晶的连通团簇形式存在，而此前缺乏有效预测这些团簇行为的定量工具。

Method: 作者建立在近期已发表的数值方法基础上，利用该方法预测酶解降解过程中球晶团簇的数量、连通性（connectivity）和形貌（morphology）作为降解结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解酶解过程中微粒的产生提供了定量框架，有助于选择合适的下游处理方案（如过滤或絮凝），从而推动酶解聚合（enzymatic depolymerization）技术的发展。

Abstract: Enzymatic recycling of plastics is limited by the presence of semi-crystalline spherulites that are recalcitrant to enzymatic depolymerization. Depending on the quality of the waste stream and its treatment history, a large volume fraction of the material actually remains in form of connected clusters of such spherulites. We build on a recently published numeric method to predict the number, the connectivity, and the morphology of these clusters as an outcome of enzymatic degradation. When applied to PET waste, our method predicts that the resulting aggregates are loosely connected, "fluffy" structures with a high surface-to-volume ratio, accompanied by smaller clusters following a continuous size distribution. By providing a quantitative framework for understanding the microparticle production during the depolymerization, these findings should assist choosing a suitable downstream treatment, such as filtering or flocculation. This work could thus help to advance the enzymatic depolymerization technologies.

</details>

### [13] [Rotated semicircle laws for permanental roots of Gaussian random matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26038)
*Renjie Feng,Dong Yao*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文证明了从标准高斯正交系综（GOE）和高斯幺正系综（GUE）中抽取的随机矩阵，其永久型特征多项式（permanental characteristic polynomial）的归一化零点计数测度几乎必然收敛到旋转π/2到虚轴上的标准维格纳半圆律，从而验证了Fyodorov提出的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fyodorov在2006年提出了关于随机矩阵永久型特征多项式零点分布的猜想，该问题涉及随机矩阵理论与组合数学（尤其是永久型）的交叉领域，此前尚未得到严格证明。

Method: 针对标准高斯正交系综和高斯幺正系综中的随机矩阵，证明了永久型特征多项式Per(zI_N−H_N)的归一化零点计数测度几乎必然收敛到标准维格纳半圆律，并将其旋转π/2映射到虚轴。

Result: 对于GOE和GUE两类系综，永久型特征多项式的零点计数测度几乎必然收敛，极限分布为旋转后的标准维格纳半圆律（Wigner semicircle law）。

Conclusion: 结果为Fyodorov于2006年提出的猜想提供了严格证明，确立了永久型特征多项式零点分布在随机矩阵框架下的极限行为。

Abstract: For matrices drawn from the standard Gaussian orthogonal ensemble (GOE) and Gaussian unitary ensemble (GUE), we prove that the normalized zero counting measure of the permanental characteristic polynomial $Per(zI_N-H_N)$ converges almost surely to the standard Wigner semicircle law on $[-2,2]$, rotated by $π/2$ onto the imaginary axis. This proves the conjecture proposed by Fyodorov in \cite{Fyodorov2006}.

</details>

### [14] [Lab earthquakes confirm the theory of frictional slip pulses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26065)
*Alina Shafir,Tom Gabrieli,Yuval Tal,Eran Bouchbinder*

Main category: physics.geo-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实验室地震（laboratory earthquake）实验，对近期建立的摩擦滑动脉冲（frictional slip pulse）二维理论进行系统验证。实验在广泛的预应力水平、破裂成核条件和小尺度断层粗糙度幅度下，证实了脉冲破裂的内在慢非定常演化特性以及衰减脉冲向增长脉冲转变的理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型天然地震通常由摩擦脉冲型破裂（frictional pulse-like rupture）介导，其特征是有限的滑动区。近期发展的二维理论预测脉冲是类别性不稳定破裂模态，其演化本质上是缓慢的，且存在从衰减到缓慢增长脉冲的转变。这一理论预测亟需实验验证。

Method: 作者开展大规模实验室地震实验，在由演化脉冲尺寸和峰值滑动速率定义的平面中检验了预测的时间依赖动力学，并测量了脉冲的无量纲增长率，以验证其内在缓慢非定常（unsteady）特性。

Result: 实验结果在多种预应力水平、破裂成核条件和断层粗糙度幅度下均证实了理论预测，包括脉冲缓慢非定常演化的动力学特征，以及衰减脉冲向增长脉冲转变的预测。

Conclusion: 这些实验结果构成了理解这一主导性地震破裂模式的重要进展，为摩擦滑动脉冲的理论框架提供了有力的实验支撑。

Abstract: Large natural earthquakes are typically mediated by frictional pulse-like rupture, which features a finite slipping zone. Recently, a comprehensive two-dimensional theory of frictional slip pulses has been developed. It predicts that pulses are categorically unstable rupture modes, whose evolution is intrinsically slow. Unsteady pulses satisfy an equation of motion expressed in terms of discrete observables, which is inherently related to their steady-state counterparts. The theory also predicts a transition from decaying to slowly growing pulses. Here, we perform extensive lab earthquake experiments to test the theory. The experiments confirm the theoretical predictions for pulse-like lab earthquakes over a range of prestress levels, rupture nucleation conditions and small-scale fault roughness amplitudes. Specifically, the predicted time-dependent dynamics are tested in a plane defined by the evolving pulse size and peak slip rate, along with measurements of the dimensionless growth rate of pulses, demonstrating their intrinsically slow unsteady nature. The predicted transition between decaying and growing pulses is also experimentally demonstrated. These results constitute major progress in understanding a dominant earthquake rupture mode.

</details>

*Daozhi Han,Nan Jiang,Jonah H. Nissan,Sayantan Sarkar*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一族用于Cahn-Hilliard-Navier-Stokes（CHNS）方程的二阶、线性、无条件稳定的隐式-显式（IMEX）方法。该方案结合了非线性项的外推、辅助变量形式的非线性自由能项以及由参数ε控制的时间曲率正则化，建立了离散能量估计以证明对θ∈(1/2,1]且ε≥0时方法的无条件长时间稳定性，数值实验验证了约二阶的时间收敛性及多种代表性界面流动问题的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等密度两相流问题由CHNS方程描述，其数值求解通常面临非线性自由能项和时间步长稳定性约束的挑战。需要一个既具有二阶精度又无条件稳定的线性数值格式，以兼顾计算效率和长时间模拟的可靠性。

Method: 提出一族半离散的隐式-显式（IMEX）方法：对非线性项采用外推处理，非线性自由能项采用辅助变量（auxiliary-variable）公式化，并引入由参数ε控制的时间曲率正则化（temporal-curvature regularization）。每个时间步仅需线性求解。

Result: 建立了离散能量估计，表明当θ∈(1/2,1]且ε≥0时方法具有无条件长时间稳定性。数值实验显示约二阶的时间收敛性，并检验了质量守恒、能量耗散与数值稳健性，成功应用于旋节线分解、液滴形状弛豫、两相顶盖驱动腔流及瑞利-泰勒不稳定性等代表性界面流动问题。

Conclusion: 该方法兼顾二阶精度、线性求解与无条件稳定性，为CHNS方程的高效数值模拟提供了一族实用且稳健的求解方案，适用于长时间演化与复杂界面流动的模拟。

Abstract: We present a family of second-order, linear, unconditionally stable implicit-explicit (IMEX) methods for the Cahn-Hilliard-Navier-Stokes (CHNS) equations modeling matched-density two-phase flows. The proposed semi-discrete scheme combines extrapolation of the nonlinear terms with an auxiliary-variable formulation of the nonlinear free-energy term and a temporal-curvature regularization controlled by a parameter $ε$. We establish a discrete energy estimate showing unconditional long-time stability of the method for $θ\in(1/2,1]$ and $ε\geq0$. The resulting scheme requires only linear solves at each time step. Numerical experiments demonstrate approximately second-order temporal convergence and examine mass conservation, energy dissipation, numerical robustness, and several representative interfacial-flow problems, including spinodal decomposition, droplet shape relaxation, two-phase lid-driven cavity flow, and Rayleigh-Taylor instability.

</details>

Abstract: Competing and coupled spin and charge interactions in quantum materials lead to a variety of ordered states where local configurations preferentially influence the long-range order. When these interactions are finely balanced in energy, disorder and fluctuations play an outsized role. Raman scattering is particularly well-suited to revealing the underlying physics in such situations because of its sensitivity to local environments and ability to reveal overall symmetries. We perform angle-resolved Raman polarization measurements on single crystals of the correlated, layered magnet, \ce{Sr2IrO4}, where the Mott insulating ground state arises from strong spin-orbit coupling. We characterize the symmetries of both the phonon and magnon modes through comprehensive measurements in both the $ab$-plane and out-of-plane geometries from 10 to 700 cm$^{-1}$, and trace the evolution of these modes in both configurations to 12 GPa in a diamond anvil cell with perforated diamonds. Pressure does not significantly alter the lattice as the phonon modes shift linearly under compression, but at the same time the magnon modes become position dependent and spread over a range of wavenumbers. We attribute this magnetic heterogeneity to pressure-enhanced variations in the weak interlayer interactions, which may locally favor competing magnetic stacking configurations, and compare our experimental results to the predictions of linear spin wave calculations. Our results demonstrate that Raman-active magnons amplify $μ$eV-scale interactions responsible for ground-state selection into easily measurable spectral changes.

Abstract: Integration of magnetic material with scalable microfluidic platforms can significantly improve the throughput and precision in biotechnology processes. In this work we have developed a new magnetic platform based on printed circuit board (PCB) technology. Cobalt-Platinum (CoPt) micromagnets are electroplated on copper pads with an arbitrary footprint on a Kapton substrate to enable generation of different magnetic field gradients. The magnets are characterized by XRD and VSM, before and after thermal annealing. The ordered L10 phase appear in XRD results after annealing at 600 °C, and VSM results show around six times increase for in-plane magnetic remanence from 0.24T to 1.4T. The near equiatomic ratios of Co:Pt is confirmed by EDX observations. The performance of these magnets is experimentally validated by trapping magnetic nanoparticles in microfluidic channels. These results are in excellent agreement with FEA models presented in COMSOL Multiphysics.

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于变形晶格上电荷密度波（charge-density wave, CDW）的最小朗道理论（minimal Landau theory）。该理论以均匀拉伸应变改变晶格倒易波矢为前提，构建了振幅-应变自由能，描述CDW通过诱导有限均匀应变来缓解其公度波数与不相称涨落波数之间的失配，并指出该失配由CDW与晶格共同分担，分担比例取决于二者刚度之比。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期在孤立单链NbS₃上的观测发现，一个公度CDW态伴随着6%的晶格常数收缩。这一现象促使作者重新审视将公度性定义在变形晶格上的CDW理论，以解释晶格与CDW之间的相互作用。

Method: 作者构建了一个振幅-应变自由能（amplitude-strain free energy），分别针对N=3和N=4两种公度情形。该理论中，均匀拉伸a₀→a₀(1+ε)将倒易晶格波矢变为G(ε)=G₀/(1+ε)，从而公度CDW波数为Q_C(ε)=G(ε)/N，而由不相称不稳定性（incommensurate instability）所偏好的波数Q_IC保持不变。

Result: 理论分析表明，CDW通过诱导有限均匀应变来缓解Q_C(ε)与Q_IC之间的失配。该失配被CDW和晶格按刚度比分担；由于CDW的刚度随其振幅增大而增加，随着CDW发展，晶格承担的失配份额越来越大。

Conclusion: 该结果提示需要对锁相（lock-in）理论以及对密度波系统的应变调控（strain-tuning）实验进行重新审视。

Abstract: We formulate a minimal Landau theory for a charge-density wave (CDW) whose commensurability is defined with respect to a deformed lattice. The motivation is provided by recent observations on an isolated single NbS$_3$ chain, which exhibits a commensurate CDW state accompanied by a $6\%$ shrinkage of the lattice constant. A uniform stretch $a_0\to a_0(1+\varepsilon)$ changes the reciprocal lattice wave number to $G(\varepsilon)=G_0/(1+\varepsilon)$, so that an $N$-fold commensurate CDW has the wave number $Q_\mathrm{C}(\varepsilon)=G(\varepsilon)/N$, whereas the wave number $Q_\mathrm{IC}$ favored by the incommensurate instability remains fixed. We propose an amplitude-strain free energy for both $N=3$ and $N=4$, in which the CDW induces a finite uniform strain by relieving the mismatch between $Q_\mathrm{C}(\varepsilon)$ and $Q_\mathrm{IC}$. The mismatch is shared between the CDW and the lattice in a proportion set by their stiffness ratio; since the CDW stiffness grows with the CDW amplitude, the lattice takes up an increasing share of the mismatch as the CDW develops. Our results suggest a reexamination of lock-in theories and of strain-tuning experiments on density-wave systems.

</details>

### [19] [Heat Transfer and Torque in Enclosing Cylindrical Configurations with Nonreciprocal Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24967)
*Dhruv Shah,Kiryl Asheichyk,David Gelbwaser-Klimovsky,Noah Graham,Mehran Kardar,Matthias Krüger*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了电磁涨落（electromagnetic fluctuations）在非平衡构型中引起的角动量传递与热传递之间的内在联系。研究证明，当两个物体中至少一个具有旋转对称性时，力矩（torque）被热传递所约束；在同心圆柱构型中，利用Rytov涨落电动力学（Rytov fluctuational electrodynamics）推导了角动量分辨通量密度表达式，并分析了稳定悬浮、摩擦以及接触式引擎效率的上界（Carnot极限）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电磁涨落不仅能传递能量，还能传递角动量，从而在非平衡体系（out-of-equilibrium setups）中产生力、力矩和摩擦。理解这些效应之间的内在约束关系对于量子涨落热力学、非接触热机和纳米级机械系统具有重要意义。

Method: 本文首先从角动量守恒的一般性物理论证出发，分析在对称性条件下力矩与热传递的约束关系。随后针对非互易介质（nonreciprocal media）构成的同心圆柱构型，采用Rytov涨落电动力学，将热传递和力矩表达为角动量分辨的通量密度Φ_n(ω)的形式；利用临近力近似（proximity force approximation）分析内圆柱的稳定悬浮条件，并推导了不同温度下稀薄平板间正常Casimir力的新解析公式；最后计算慢旋转内圆柱的涨落诱导摩擦（fluctuation-induced friction），建立力矩、摩擦与热传递之间的界限。

Result: 研究得出以下关键结果：（1）当至少一个物体具有旋转对称性时，力矩受热传递的上界约束；若两个物体均具旋转对称性，则需非互易介质才能打破n与−n之间的对称性以产生非零力矩。（2）在同心圆柱构型中，获得了基于角动量分辨通量密度的显式表达。（3）推导出稀薄平板间不同温度下正常Casimir力的新解析公式。（4）建立了力矩、摩擦与热传递之间的界限，由此证明非接触热机效率受限于Carnot极限。

Conclusion: 这项工作揭示了非平衡涨落电动力学中角动量传递与热传递之间的基本约束关系，为量子涨落热机（fluctuation heat engine）的效率和稳定性提供了理论边界，同时也展示了非互易介质在实现非零力矩中的关键作用。

Abstract: Electromagnetic fluctuations can transfer not only energy but also angular momentum, leading to forces, torques, heat currents, and friction in out-of-equilibrium setups. In enclosing configurations, we show that if at least one of two objects is rotationally symmetric, the torque is bounded by heat transfer, since both arise from photon transfers with angular momentum $\hbar n$ and energy $\hbarω$. With only one object assumed to be rotationally symmetric, it may be possible to obtain a nonzero torque with reciprocal media, but nonreciprocal media are required to break the symmetry between $n$ and $-n$ and produce a nonzero torque if both objects are rotationally symmetric. We then specialize to concentric cylinders with a nonreciprocal dielectric response and use Rytov fluctuational electrodynamics to express heat transfer and torque in terms of an angular-momentum-resolved flux density, $Φ_n(ω)$. We also analyze the conditions for stable levitation of the inner cylinder using the proximity force approximation, in the process obtaining a new analytic formula for the normal Casimir force between dilute plates at different temperatures. Finally, to find the extracted work in a contactless engine setup, we compute the fluctuation-induced friction for a slowly rotating inner cylinder, and we find a bound between torque, friction, and heat transfer. Due to this bound, the efficiency of the heat engine remains bounded by the Carnot limit.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Johnson-Nyquist理论通常将导体表示为独立局部热噪声源的集合，其强度由局部温度决定。但该局部噪声表示对处于不同温度的电容耦合导体是否仍然有效，此前鲜有研究关注。

Result: 当两根导线处于相同温度时，模型复现欧姆定律、平衡Johnson噪声以及分离导线段间无关的关联特性；但当导线处于不同温度时，远端线段产生的电动势之间存在有限关联，导致从局部噪声图像推断的Johnson温度出现系统性偏差。该效应在粒子相互作用为短程且导线仅通过电容耦合互作用的情况下仍然持续存在。

Conclusion: 结果表明，独立局部源表示热噪声的方法在电耦合的非平衡导体中可能不再有效，这对传统Johnson-Nyquist噪声理论在非平衡电耦合系统中的应用提出了重要质疑。

Abstract: The Johnson-Nyquist theory is commonly implemented by representing a conductor as a collection of independent local thermal-noise sources whose strength is determined by the local temperature. Whether this local-noise representation remains valid for electrically coupled conductors maintained at different temperatures has received comparatively little attention. We investigate this question by means of microscopic two-dimensional simulations of interacting charge carriers in conducting wires capacitively coupled. The model reproduces Ohm's law, the equilibrium Johnson noise, and vanishing correlations between detached wire segments when both wires are at the same temperature. However, when the wires are held at different temperatures, finite correlations develop between the electromotive forces generated in distant segments, leading to systematic deviations of the Johnson temperature inferred from the local-noise picture. The effect persists although the microscopic particle interactions are short-ranged and the two wires interact only through the capacitive coupling. These results suggest that the independent-local-source representation of thermal noise may not remain valid in electrically coupled nonequilibrium conductors.

</details>


### [21] [Directed walks shape a universal square-root law of entropy production rate in nonreciprocal systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25030)
*Thiparat Chotibut,Ewa Gudowska-Nowak,Maciej A. Nowak*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文针对多变量 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck 动力学，将熵产率（entropy production rate, EPR）在复杂相互作用网络上表述为反对称矩阵的二次型形式，等价于有向游走（directed walks）的数量量，并证明平均每节点 EPR 在各种随机相互作用下遵循普适的平方根律 φ_*(g)=1-√(1-g²)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准熵产率公式不能清晰揭示复杂相互作用网络如何产生不可逆性，需要建立 EPR 与网络结构之间更直接的数学联系。

Method: 对多变量 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck 动力学，作者将 EPR 表达为测量各长度聚合有向游走非互易性的反对称矩阵的二次型；等价的两种有向游走权重形式分别对应共享两端点的游走对与有向闭游走。对可对角化相互作用，建立了游走量与特征值及双正交特征向量重叠之间的精确对应。随后在满足匹配游走条件（matched-walk conditions）的稠密、稀疏及深层无环随机相互作用下分析平均每节点 EPR。

Result: 平均每节点 EPR 在所有三类随机相互作用下统一遵循平方根律 φ_*(g)=1-√(1-g²)，其中 g∈[0,1) 参数化相互作用强度。深层无环相互作用矩阵是幂零的（nilpotent），所有特征值恒为零，但随着深度增加其平均每节点 EPR 趋近于 φ_*(g)。

Conclusion: 平方根律源于有向游走特性而非共享谱密度或特定网络拓扑，表明 EPR 的网络起源可以脱离特征谱结构而独立理解。

Abstract: The entropy production rate (EPR) quantifies irreversibility of a nonequilibrium steady state, yet standard formulas obscure how a complex interaction network generates it. For multivariate Ornstein-Uhlenbeck dynamics on such networks, we express the EPR as a quadratic form in antisymmetric matrices measuring the nonreciprocity of aggregate directed walks at every length, and, equivalently, as two weighted-walk quantities: pairs of directed walks sharing both endpoints, and directed closed walks. For diagonalizable interactions, an exact correspondence translates these walk quantities into eigenvalues and biorthogonal eigenvector overlaps. Across dense, sparse, and deep acyclic random interactions satisfying matched-walk conditions, the mean EPR per node universally follows the square-root law $φ_*(g)=1-\sqrt{1-g^2}$, where $g \in [0,1)$ parametrizes the interaction strength. Deep acyclic interaction matrices are nilpotent, with all eigenvalues fixed at zero for every $g$, yet, as their depth increases, their mean EPR per node approaches $φ_*(g)$. Thus, the square-root law arises from directed walk properties, rather than from a shared spectral density or specific network topology.

</details>


### [22] [Delta-Function Kicks are Optimal for Rapidly Driven Inertial Stochastic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25070)

Abstract: Relaxation toward equilibrium is usually accelerated by modifying the environment or by cooling a system further from equilibrium. This replacement turns a continuous problem over unitary matrices into a discrete combinatorial problem over zero--one permutation matrices, and thereby opens a path toward derandomization.

Thus the random construction can be derandomized in an asymptotic algorithmic sense, although finding a simple closed-form or practically computable counterexample remains open.
  Finally, a quantitative random permutation estimate by Chen, Garza-Vargas, Tropp and van Handel gives a fully numerical estimate: there exists a tuple of 57,836,025 permutations acting on a set of size \[ N \le 5.422\times 10^{116216}\] such that the associated finite dimensional channel exhibits nonadditivity. This enormous value remains an obstacle to a practical construction.

</details>

We then show that prediction precision grows as a power law $\sim N^α$ in the reservoir size, superextensively $(α>1)$ at the onset of scrambling and only sublinearly $(α<1)$ in either neighbouring regime. At the onset, the total memory capacity grows superextensively, provided that the necessary ``forgetting'' mechanism is supplied by a collective relaxation channel. Abstract: The interaction between a qubit and its environment can be engineered such that one error channel dominates over all others, resulting in noise bias. This property enables error correction codes to focus on the dominant error type, thereby significantly reducing the number of physical systems required for fault-tolerant quantum computation. We encode the qubit in the transmon $|\mathrm{g}\rangle$- and $|\mathrm{f}\rangle$-states, and engineer a frequency-selective gain channel that counteracts single-photon loss errors between the computational states. We demonstrate an order-of-magnitude enhancement in relaxation time compared to the $|\mathrm{g}\rangle-|\mathrm{e}\rangle$ encoding, conceding only a factor-of-two decrease in the echo-coherence time. Furthermore, we show that this qubit is compatible with fast, high-fidelity operations. Our results open a path towards using this system as a simple building-block for hardware-efficient quantum error detection and correction schemes.

Abstract: Building on the first-order circuit quantization method [arXiv:2304.12252, arXiv:2401.09120], we provide simple formulas to construct exact Hamiltonians for Josephson-junction-based superconducting qudits capacitively, inductively, or galvanically coupled to passive linear environments. These environments may be multiport, multimode, discrete or continuous, reciprocal or nonreciprocal, and are characterized directly by their impedance or admittance matrices. In the weak-coupling regime, we further derive \emph{divergence-free} dispersive Hamiltonians for mode-resolved environments and transition-resolved weak-coupling master equations for dissipative continua. Mode structure, frequency renormalizations, environment-mediated interactions, decay rates, and directional cross couplings then follow from the same causal immittance response, while spurious Lamb-shift divergences arising from uncontrolled approximations in previous treatments are made explicit and avoided. We apply the theory to a set of illustrative circuits comprising a discrete resonator filter, finite-band metamaterial environments, nonreciprocal waveguide-QED systems, and superconducting giant atoms, for which analytical response matrices can be obtained, although the method is particularly well suited to numerical responses from electromagnetic solvers or experimental characterization. We thereby extend the black-box quantization framework to multiport, dissipative, and nonreciprocal settings, establishing a simple and scalable route toward optimized and automated electromagnetic design of large-scale superconducting quantum hardware.

Abstract: We show that the $d+0$-dimensional Yang-Lee theory describing classical Ising spins in an imaginary magnetic field can be realized, without post-selection, within a $(d-1)+1$ open quantum system whose dynamics consist of local unitaries and engineered dissipation. Competition between the coherent unitary and dissipative dynamics drives a transition wherein the time-dependence of a particular class of linear observables changes from damped oscillatory "underdamped" to purely exponential "overdamped" decay. Our construction relies on an extensive number of weak-symmetries of the Lindbladian fixed by the choice of observable but is otherwise exact. Consequently, we show that the dynamics are described by the non-Hermitian generator of the Yang-Lee transfer matrix, leading to an effective Yang-Lee theory defined on the spacetime history of the open system. By locally modifying the dynamics, we directly measure spin correlation functions of the Yang-Lee theory as well as a related "Loschmidt Echo" correlator which we detail. We explicitly show that the required dissipation channels can be obtained through local $2$-qubit gates and discuss potential realization on near-term quantum simulator devices. Finally, we generalize our construction to embed arbitrary non-Hermitian Hamiltonians within an open quantum system under Lindbladian time-evolution without post-selection, drawing connections between unconditional open quantum dynamics, exceptional point physics, and non-unitary statistical mechanics.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate the stability of elastic interfaces beyond the linear regime by considering penny-shaped crack fronts propagating through toughness heterogeneities. Using fracture mechanics simulations, we drive crack fronts through arrays of obstacles with tunable toughness contrast. At low contrast, the crack front stiffness remains finite and stabilizes front perturbations. Above a critical threshold, however, the stiffness vanishes and the front destabilizes into long fingers that evolve into daughter cracks propagating between obstacles while the original crack remains pinned. Near threshold, the crack front response displays the characteristic square-root scaling behavior of classical saddle-node bifurcations. Yet, our analysis reveals a fundamentally different mechanism: the stable energy-minimizing crack-front configuration disappears without colliding with an unstable counterpart. Instead, the instability originates from a global loss of Griffith-compatible equilibria governed by the nonlocal interactions along the crack front. Beyond fracture mechanics, these findings point toward a broader class of collective global bifurcations in nonlocal elastic interfaces and may help rationalize the brittle-to-quasibrittle transition in heterogeneous solids.

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可解释的物理信息检索增强生成语言模型（Physics-Informed Retrieval-Augmented Generation Language Model, PIRAG-LM），用于无机晶体合成规划的端到端任务。该模型基于包含13,820种实验合成无机晶体的结构化合成知识库（SSKB），通过化学、结构和热力学相似性检索历史先例，并利用结构化大语言模型推理模块提出合成路线、前驱体和工艺条件。模型在合成方法预测上达到91.4%准确率（对比纯LLM的72.1%），并成功指导实验合成了五种新化合物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无机材料的合成规划需要在微观热力学稳定性与宏观合成方法、前驱体和工艺条件之间建立关联，同时预测材料的可合成性与可行合成路线。现有方法缺乏将领域物理知识（如热力学可行性）与大语言模型的推理能力有效结合的端到端方案，且依赖参数化记忆导致泛化受限。

Abstract: Synthesis planning for inorganic materials requires predicting both synthesizability and viable routes by linking microscopic thermodynamic stability with macroscopic synthesis methods, precursors, and processing conditions. Here, we develop an interpretable Physics-Informed Retrieval-Augmented Generation Language Model (PIRAG-LM) for end-to-end inorganic crystal synthesis planning. We construct a material-centered Structured Synthesis Knowledge Base (SSKB) containing route-level records for 13,820 experimentally synthesized inorganic crystals. PIRAG-LM retrieves historical precedents using chemical, structural, and thermodynamic similarity, then employs a structured LLM reasoning module to propose routes, precursors, and processing conditions and assess thermodynamic feasibility, kinetics, and accessibility. It achieves 91.4% accuracy in synthesis-method prediction, compared with 72.1% for the LLM alone, and generalizes to materials reported after the knowledge cutoff. Because the framework relies on retrieval rather than parametric memorization, its performance can be improved by expanding the SSKB without retraining the language model. Guided by PIRAG-LM, we experimentally synthesize five new compounds: BaMo0.3In0.7O2.95, BaNb0.4In0.6O2.9, Hg[B(CN)4]2, CoCo(CN)6, and SrNb2Fe2(PO4)6, via solid-state and solution routes. These results demonstrate an interpretable machine-learning approach that helps bridge computational materials discovery and experimental realization.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极性半导体中激子与极性声子的耦合（Fröhlich相互作用）存在长波红外发散，理论上似乎必然导致强声子修饰，但实验上卤化物钙钛矿表现出反常弱的声子修饰，而过渡金属硫化物则呈现强声子伴带。如何统一解释这些看似矛盾的现象，并提供一个可预测激子-声子耦合强度的普适判据，是本研究的主要动机。

Method: 对激子形状因子（excitonic form factor）进行精确的多极展开，并与氢原子包络函数（hydrogenic envelope functions）的数值积分交叉验证；分析不同宇称态之间的弹性散射和非弹性跃迁，考察电子-空穴质量不对称性对高阶多极项的影响，并验证选择定则在体相和二维系统中的维度不变性。

Result: 相同宇称激子态之间的弹性散射中，Fröhlich相互作用的长波红外发散被电子-空穴相消干涉精确抵消；不同宇称态间的跃迁由于相长干涉而保持有限且稳健的耦合。电子与空穴的质量不对称会激活弹性散射通道中的高阶多极项，但对相长干涉的非弹性通道影响甚微。该选择定则具有维度不变性，适用于体相和二维系统。

Conclusion: 该框架为激子-声子耦合强度提供了普适的解析精确判据，可统一解释卤化物钙钛矿中反常弱的声子修饰与过渡金属硫化物中强声子伴带现象，为按需设计具有定制声子相互作用的激子材料提供了原理性指导。

Abstract: We demonstrate that exciton-phonon coupling in polar semiconductors is governed by a parity-controlled interference selection rule. By performing an exact multipole expansion of the excitonic form factor and validating it against numerical integration of hydrogenic envelope functions, we show that the long-range infrared divergence of the Fröhlich interaction is exactly canceled for elastic scattering between excitonic states of the same parity. The ground-state exciton is thereby protected from long-wavelength polar phonons by destructive electron-hole interference. In contrast, transitions between states of opposite parity exhibit constructive interference, preserving a finite, robust coupling to macroscopic polar fields independent of band-structure details. Mass asymmetry between the electron and hole activates higher-order multipole terms in the elastic channel but leaves the constructive inelastic channel essentially unaffected. The selection rule is dimensionally invariant, applying to bulk and two-dimensional systems alike, and naturally explains the anomalously weak phonon dressing observed in halide perovskites as well as the strong phonon sidebands in transition-metal dichalcogenides. Our framework provides a universal, analytically exact criterion for exciton-phonon coupling strength, offering a design principle for engineering excitonic materials with tailored phonon interactions.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直流热电机械测试基于电阻焦耳加热（resistive Joule heating），能够实现快速升降温、陡峭热梯度和同时机械加载，在传统炉基方法无法实现的条件下探测变形、相变、氧化辅助损伤和蠕变等行为。尽管使用日益广泛，该方法缺乏正式标准化，且常被误解为等同于整体等温测试（bulk isothermal testing），忽略了热场和机械场的内在差异。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 本文以综述形式整合了四个领域（试样几何、温度测量、应变表征和环境控制）的四十年研究，并根据建模与实验证据将不确定性来源分类为主要的（dominant）、次要的（secondary）和条件性的（conditional）三类，以系统评估DC-TMT方法的可靠性和适用边界。

Result: 建模与实验证据表明，温度梯度、加热速率和标距代表性主导了推断材料行为的可靠性。综述对铝合金、钢、镍基高温合金、钛合金、硬质合金、锆合金、形状记忆合金及增材制造（additively manufactured）体系中的DC-TMT应用进行了批判性评估，确定了该方法能提供可复现机理洞察的领域，以及不宜与整体数据直接等同的条件。

Conclusion: 综述指出，需要透明的数据报告、多传感器温度验证，以及与电热建模（electro-thermal modelling）的集成，才能实现严谨的、以机理为导向的DC-TMT结果解读。

Abstract: Direct current thermo-mechanical testing (DC-TMT), based on resistive Joule heating, enables rapid heating and cooling, steep thermal gradients and simultaneous mechanical loading, making it a powerful tool for probing deformation, phase transformations, oxidation-assisted damage and creep under conditions inaccessible to conventional furnace-based methods. Despite its growing use, DC-TMT lacks formal standardisation and is often misinterpreted as equivalent to bulk isothermal testing, overlooking intrinsic differences in thermal and mechanical fields. This review addresses that gap by consolidating four decades of research on specimen geometry, temperature measurement, strain characterisation and environmental control, and by classifying uncertainty sources as dominant, secondary and conditional. Evidence from modelling and experiment shows that temperature gradients, heating rate and gauge representativeness govern the reliability of inferred material behaviour. Applications across aluminium, steels, nickel-based superalloys, titanium alloys, hardmetals, zirconium alloys, shape memory alloys and additively manufactured systems are critically assessed. The review highlights domains where DC-TMT provides reproducible mechanistic insight and conditions where direct equivalence with bulk data is not warranted. Implications include the need for transparent reporting, multi-sensor temperature validation and integration with electro-thermal modelling to enable rigorous, mechanism-focused interpretation.

</details>

Abstract: The anomalous Hall effect (AHE) and anomalous Nernst effect (ANE), which describe transverse electrical and thermoelectric responses in magnetic materials, respectively, are promising for spintronic and energy-harvesting applications. Here, we employ high-throughput first-principles calculations to investigate 2251 chemically substituted tetragonal $L1_0$ alloys. Small exchange splitting keeps majority- and minority-spin bands near the Fermi level, giving chemical substitution more opportunity to modify near-Fermi-level band crossings and amplify the Berry curvature. Our results identify the exchange splitting of the parent compound as a descriptor for chemical tunability toward giant Berry-curvature-driven transport responses.

Our observation implies the existence of an intrinsic thermal Hall effect in a generic phonon gas. Abstract: Self-driving laboratories (SDLs) are transforming materials research by closing the loop among synthesis, characterization, data analysis and experimental decision making. Bayesian optimization (BO) is a decision engine for these loops because it can select experiments from scarce and noisy data while balancing exploitation and exploration. Yet real materials campaigns often depart from the standard black-box setting, involving failed or missing experiments, noise and drift, mixed variables, constraints, multiple objectives, variable cost and fidelity, transfer from historical data, batch or asynchronous operation, and prior physics knowledge. This review presents BO for materials SDLs through the lens of these practical challenges. We summarize Gaussian-process-based BO and the formulation of materials goals as quantitative objectives, then discuss major choices in surrogate modelling and acquisition. Particular emphasis is placed on physics-informed Bayesian optimization (PIBO), in which domain knowledge enters through representations, priors, kernels, acquisition functions, and constraints. We survey achievements enabled by BO and related active-learning approaches across semiconductors, catalysis, chemical reactions, batteries, alloys, functional materials and quantum materials, highlighting advances beyond parameter optimization, including new materials and synthesis routes, improved functional performance, and reusable scientific knowledge. We conclude by outlining open problems for BO-driven materials SDLs, including nonstationarity, multimodal observations, adaptive problem formulation, and scientific reasoning by humans, large language models and research agents. Addressing these challenges may advance SDLs beyond efficient optimization toward interpretable and knowledge-generating experimentation.

Multiple resonances associated with the electron spin resonance in graphene are observed for both out-of-plane and in-plane magnetic-field orientations. Their magnetic-field positions vary linearly with frequency, their amplitudes reverse sign across the charge-neutrality point, and the resonant contribution has the opposite sign to the nonresonant Drude photogalvanic background. Their opposite signs originate from the orthogonal momentum alignments produced by indirect Drude absorption and direct spin-resonant transitions. Result: 液氩呈现非平稳的"导通"（turn-on）行为，反复放电后击穿电压逐渐升高；液氦在饱和蒸气压附近呈现双峰击穿分布，低场分量与气泡介导过程一致；液氮的击穿场强高于液氩，与加压液氦相当。

Abstract: We present a comparative study of DC electrical breakdown in liquid nitrogen, liquid helium, and liquid argon using a common cryostat, electrode assembly, voltage-ramp protocol, and analysis procedure. This common experimental configuration minimizes systematic differences that often complicate comparisons among cryogenic liquids, including variations in electrode geometry, stressed area, surface finish, and gap spacing. For each liquid, breakdown was characterized as a statistical distribution rather than by a single characteristic voltage, allowing the stochastic nature of the process to be examined. Measurements were performed both near saturated vapor pressure and under modest pressurization to assess the influence of bubble formation and related thermodynamic effects. Liquid argon exhibited a non-stationary "turn-on" behavior, with breakdown voltage increasing over repeated discharges, consistent with evolution of the local impurity environment or electrode surface state. Near saturated vapor pressure, liquid helium showed a bimodal breakdown distribution, with a lower-field component consistent with bubble-mediated processes. Liquid nitrogen exhibited higher breakdown fields than liquid argon and fields comparable to those measured in pressurized liquid helium, within the observed run-to-run variation associated with surface state. Taken together, these results support a comparative framework in which breakdown strength reflects both thermodynamic stability against bubble formation and the transport properties of the dominant negative charge carrier.

Laboratory results revealed that power conversion efficiency increased by 17.5% with graphene oxide and by 36.8% with reduced graphene oxide. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锂离子电池的物理模型需要每个电极的开路电位（OCP）作为其锂化程度的函数，而MSMR模型为此提供了紧凑且具有热力学基础的描述。目前尚缺乏针对汽车电池中广泛使用的NCA正极的MSMR参数集，这限制了该类模型的参数化应用。

Method: 从全新电芯及在两种荷电状态区间循环老化至寿命终点的电芯中提取正极材料，通过慢速恒流循环（slow galvanostatic cycling）测量半电池OCP。采用直方图计数法获得平滑的微分容量曲线，并利用约束、尺度不变的优化算法分别拟合充放电分支，使每个反应锚定到可见峰。

Result: 首次报道了NCA正极的MSMR参数集；通过不同老化状态下的微分容量峰分析表明，该电极的热力学特征基本不受老化影响。

Conclusion: NCA电极的内在参数在老化过程中保持不变，支持了将老化归因于电极级容量和电极对齐（alignment）变化、而保持电极固有参数固定的常见建模实践。

Abstract: Physics-based models of lithium-ion batteries require the open-circuit potential (OCP) of each electrode as a function of its lithiation. The multi-species multi-reaction (MSMR) model provides a compact, thermodynamically grounded description of this relationship, representing each insertion reaction by a standard potential, a site fraction and a disorder parameter. Here, we parametrize the MSMR model for nickel-cobalt-aluminum oxide (NCA), a cathode chemistry widespread in automotive cells, using the positive electrode of a commercial silicon-graphite/NCA cell. Cathode material was harvested from a pristine cell and from cells cycle-aged to the end of life under two state-of-charge windows, and its half-cell OCP was measured by slow galvanostatic cycling. Smooth differential-capacity curves were obtained by histogram counting, and the charge and discharge branches were fitted with a constrained, scale-invariant optimization that keeps every reaction anchored to a visible peak. We report the first MSMR parameter set for an NCA cathode and show, through a differential-capacity peak analysis across aging states, that its thermodynamic signature is essentially unchanged by aging. This finding supports the common modeling practice of holding the electrode's intrinsic parameters fixed and ascribing aging to electrode-level capacity and alignment changes.

Conclusion: 该首次报道的完整机理建模框架（含全部方程、参数与变量）可推广至其他正极材料和酸还原浸出体系，为锂离子电池回收过程中的过程建模与优化提供了系统工具。

Abstract: We developed a new mechanistic model for lithium and cobalt recovery during acidic-reductive leaching from LiCoO$_2$. The system considers HCl as an acid and H$_2$O$_2$ as a reducing agent. We introduced the film passivation effects for reduced recovery in the absence of H$_2$O$_2$. We validated the model against the experimental data of recovered Li and Co reported in literature at three acid concentrations (0.5 M, 1.5 M, and 2.5 M) and four H$_2$O$_2$ concentrations (0%, 0.2%, 0.4%, and 0.6% (v/v)). The model works reasonably well at 0.5 M and 1.5 M HCl for the 0% and 0.6% concentrations of H$_2$O$_2$. At 2.5 M HCl, the model over-predicts the data in the presence of H$_2$O$_2$, while it works well at 0% H$_2$O$_2$. We suggest pathways for further improvements in the model. ### [70] [Anomalous behavior of Wilson fermions in the presence of monopoles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25143)
*Manuel Cortina,Rajamani Narayanan,Ray Romero*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究三维紧致U(1)规范理论（three-dimensional compact U(1) gauge theory）中单极子（monopole）对两味Wilson费米子（two-flavor Wilson fermions）低能谱的影响。研究发现，在单极子存在时，Wilson-Dirac算子失去了预期的可交换性，导致在特定裸Wilson质量（bare Wilson mass）符号下出现大量极小本征值，这些本征模与单极子一一对应，并暗示味对称性破缺（flavor symmetry breaking）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维紧致U(1)规范理论在固定格点规范耦合下支持有限密度的单极子。作者希望理解单极子对两味Wilson费米子的低能谱行为的影响，以及这种影响是否破坏理论在连续极限下预期的性质，如宇称不变性（parity invariance）和算子可交换性。

Method: 作者首先验证了单极子密度对格点规范耦合的预期依赖关系，随后以裸Wilson质量（bare Wilson mass）为函数，系统研究了两味两分量Wilson费米子的低能谱。两味的Wilson-Dirac算子互为厄米共轭（hermitian conjugate）以恢复宇称不变性，并在不同格点规范耦合下考察其可交换性与本征谱结构。

Result: 研究发现，单极子存在时两个Wilson-Dirac算子的可交换性在所有格点规范耦合值下均不成立。在特定符号的裸Wilson质量范围内，观测到大量极小本征值（very small eigenvalues），这些本征模与规范场定义的单极子数一一对应。在渐近耦合区间，这些本征模谱与体谱（bulk spectrum）之间存在能隙。本征值关于零点成对出现，其中一种符号的本征值耦合到单极子，另一种符号的本征值耦合到反单极子（anti-monopole），表明味对称性破缺。

Conclusion: 单极子通过破坏Wilson-Dirac算子的可交换性，诱导出与单极子密度直接相关的近零本征模，并导致味对称性破缺，这超越了典型连续极限标准预期所描述的行为。

Abstract: Three dimensional compact U(1) gauge theory supports a finite density of monopoles at a fixed lattice gauge coupling. After showing evidence for the expected dependence on the lattice coupling of the density of monopoles, we study the low lying spectrum of a two flavors of two-component Wilson fermions as a function of the bare Wilson mass. The Wilson-Dirac operator that realizes the two flavors are hermitian conjugate of each other to restore parity invariance and the commutativity of the two operators expected in the typical continuum limit is not the case in the presence of monopoles for all values of the lattice gauge coupling. This results in an anomalous behavior where we see clear evidence for the presence of very small eigenvalues for a wide range of a particular sign of the bare Wilson mass. These eigenmodes are typically in one-to-one correspondence with the number of monopoles as defined by the gauge field and the spectrum associated with these modes are separated by a gap from the bulk in the asymptotic regime of the lattice gauge coupling. The eigenvalues come in pairs around zero and the eigenvalues of one sign couple to the monopoles while the modes of the opposite sign couple to the anti-monopole implying flavor symmetry breaking.

</details>

### [71] [Probing sub-GeV dark matter through dark matter-electron scattering in the superheated $C_2H_2F_4$ target of the InDEx experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24960)
*Utkarsh Patel,Astitva Kathait,Mala Das*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于印度暗物质探测试验（InDEx）的过热度液滴探测器，将其工作温度扩展至45–70°C范围，将Seitz阈值降低至26.8 eV，从而首次探讨了该探测器通过电子反冲通道探测亚GeV暗物质的可能性。在零背景假设和10³ kg·days基准曝光量下，项目得到的90%置信水平上限表明，该电子通道可将InDEx的暗物质质量探测下限延伸至约12 MeV，为现有核反冲计划提供了互补的低质量暗物质探测途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: InDEx（印度暗物质搜索实验）此前仅通过核反冲通道探测暗物质，对亚GeV质量范围的暗物质灵敏度有限。本文旨在通过降低工作温度对应的Seitz阈值，开启电子反冲探测通道，从而扩展InDEx对低质量暗物质（特别是与轻子耦合的暗物质候选体）的探测能力。

Method: 采用InDEx实验的四氟乙烷（C₂H₂F₄）过热度液滴探测器，考虑六个工作温度（T=45–70°C，间隔5°C），对应Seitz阈值从0.61 keV降至26.8 eV。在孤立原子近似下，基于Roothaan–Hartree–Fock波函数计算C₂H₂F₄目标的电离响应，并针对暗光子型媒介子在重媒介子和轻媒介子极限下推导暗物质–电子散射截面的90%置信水平投影上限。

Result: 在最低阈值E_th=26.8 eV（T=70°C）条件下，探测的暗物质质量下限可达约12 MeV。对于接触相互作用（contact interaction），在m_χ≈100 MeV处90%置信水平上限达到2.7×10⁻⁴¹ cm²；对于长程相互作用（long-range interaction），在m_χ≈83 MeV处上限为3.0×10⁻³⁷ cm²。

Conclusion: 电子反冲通道将InDEx的暗物质探测质量范围扩展到亚GeV区间（低至约10 MeV量级），与核反冲计划形成互补，增强了其对主要与轻子耦合的低质量暗物质候选体的灵敏度。

Abstract: The Indian Dark Matter Search Experiment (InDEx) operates superheated droplet detectors with tetrafluoroethane ($\mathrm{C_2H_2F_4}$) as the active target at the Jaduguda Underground Science Laboratory (JUSL), and has so far probed dark matter (DM) through nuclear recoils at operating temperatures at which the target is insensitive to electron recoils. The relative phases are not left free, but are fixed by the signature factors of the corresponding even and odd contributions. In this way, the real and imaginary parts of each term are linked by crossing. The $C$-even amplitude is first described by three effective Pomeron-like terms. Abstract: Gravitational radiation from the early Universe offers a powerful window into physics beyond the Standard Model (SM). In this paper, we investigate the low-frequency tail of gravitational radiation produced by the decay of massive particles. The energy spectrum in Minkowski space-time, $dE/dω$, exhibits two distinct regimes: a flat bremsstrahlung/memory spectrum above a characteristic cutoff frequency and a suppressed quadratic frequency scaling below it. The typical rate of momentum change of the decay products determines the cutoff frequency. We calculate $dE/dω$ in two independent but equivalent ways: using Weinberg's soft graviton theorem and by solving the equations of motion. This further establishes the connection between Weinberg's soft graviton theorem and the gravitational memory formula, which we generalize to incorporate the effects of final-state scattering. We then compute the cosmological stochastic gravitational energy spectrum $h^2Ω_{\rm h}$ generated by many particle decays in the early Universe. We demonstrate that $h^2Ω_{\rm h}$ exhibits linear scaling with frequency above the cutoff frequency, transitioning to cubic scaling below it. Our findings reveal a suppression of the low-frequency signal compared to a naive linear extrapolation, with implications for detection prospects. Our results also reveal the connection between bremsstrahlung and memory in the context of cosmological stochastic gravitational energy spectra from the early Universe.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限密度QCD研究中，实化学势与实转动都面临符号问题（sign problem），这使得规范格点计算极为困难。虚转动和虚化学势为绕过符号问题提供了途径，因此建立虚转动与虚化学势之间的精确映射关系，并明确该映射在何处对应热力学奇异性（特别是Roberge-Weiss端点附近电荷共轭对称性的自发破缺），具有重要的理论意义。

Result: 证明了虚转动系统在(Ω_I/T, θ_q)=(2π,0)处精确等价于Roberge-Weiss点(0,π)，该结论不依赖任何模型与近似。解析求得二次相变点 Ω_{I,C}/(πT)=(22-2√22)/9（此处Im L连续上升）以及一阶相变点 Ω_{I,lock}/(πT)=2/3+2√111/27（此处系统锁定到非平凡中心元素）。发现无质量单圈近似下全QCD与纯Yang-Mills理论均出现连续简并，但在全QCD侧该简并仅为偶然性，被有限奇异夸克质量所解除。

Conclusion: 虚转动与虚化学势之间的精确恒等式不依赖模型与近似，揭示了(2π,0)点与Roberge-Weiss点(0,π)之间的精确映射，并给出了无质量近似下两类相变点的解析闭式表达式。最后指出了在格点QCD中检验精确关系与轴上（on-axis）预言所需满足的条件。

Abstract: Starting from the identity $\mathrm{e}^{2π\mathrm{i}J_z}=(-1)^{F}=\mathrm{e}^{\mathrm{i}πQ}$ for the angular momentum, fermion parity, and quark number operators, valid on the color-singlet physical state space, we derive a density-operator identity that ties imaginary rotation to an imaginary quark chemical potential, $ρ(Ω_I/T+2π,θ_q)=ρ(Ω_I/T,θ_q+π)$. It follows that the imaginarily rotating system of $SU(3)$ QCD at $(Ω_I/T,θ_q)=(2π,0)$ is mapped exactly onto the Roberge-Weiss point $(0,π)$, where charge conjugation is spontaneously broken above the Roberge-Weiss endpoint. This conclusion depends on no model and no approximation. Minimizing the one-loop effective potential on the rotation axis over the entire $SU(3)$ Weyl alcove, we further obtain analytically, for three massless quark flavors, the second-order transition point $Ω_{I,C}/(πT)=(22-2\sqrt{22})/9$ at which $\mathrm{Im}\,L$ rises continuously, and the first-order transition point $Ω_{I,\mathrm{lock}}/(πT)=2/3+2\sqrt{111}/27$ at which the minimum locks into the nontrivial center elements. In the massless one-loop approximation a continuous degeneracy appears in full QCD and in pure Yang-Mills theory alike, but on the full-QCD side it is merely accidental, lifted by a finite strange-quark mass. Finally, we state the conditions under which the exact relations and the on-axis predictions can be tested in lattice QCD.

</details>

Once ultraviolet regulators are restricted to genuinely divergent vacuum contributions, however, all three prescriptions yield nearly identical stability diagrams. Both the onset of the moat regime and the subsequent finite-momentum instability become quantitatively robust. The apparent scheme dependence originates from regulating ultraviolet-finite medium contributions associated with the Fermi-surface response. Our results identify spatially modulated chiral correlations as a genuine property of the dense medium rather than an artifact of the ultraviolet prescription.

Abstract: We perform a theoretical study of defect states within the photonic band gap of three-dimensional diamond crystals composed of point scatterers and doped with substitutional defects. Off-site Green's functions are also calculated as function of distance and are shown to vanish within approximately two unit cells. The latter not only reproduce the eigenfrequencies of the defect states within the band gap, but also provide their lifetimes originating from the finite crystal size. The lifetimes of the defect states increase exponentially with crystal size, becoming very long for large crystals. In addition to defect states in the three-dimensional photonic band gap, the defects also give rise to strongly detuned states outside the gap, which decouple from the spectrum of the ideal defect-free crystal.

Abstract: We study the localization of a quantum particle in a one-dimensional disordered system with long-range hopping amplitudes $t(r)\propto r^{-a}$. Abstract: Hindered settling of non spherical particles remains significantly less understood than spherical particles due to the computational challenges in resolving the complex contact mechanics and hydrodynamic interactions. In this paper, we present a scaleable fluid structure interaction (FSI) framework coupling the open-source LBM software in OpenLB with the Discrete Element Method (DEM) implemented in LAMMPS to simulate arbitrary shaped rigid bodies in a fluid. Particle contacts are captured using a multi-sphere "clump" representation in DEM, while particle geometries are resolved on the fluid grid via voxelization. We validate our model against single and multi-particle benchmarks, and proceed to study the hindered setttling of cubes in systems containing upto 100,000 cubes. Our simulations show distinct differences between the settling of cubes and sphere, as cubes form pronounced coordination shells without face-parallel contact, in contrast to the contact-dominated clustering observed in spheres. Our results find that correlation length in velocity fluctuations scale with system size even for the largest system studied. These findings highlight the role of particle morphology in suspension dynamics and demonstrate a robust framework for simulating large-scale geotechnical and particulate flows.

Abstract: We establish a uniqueness theorem in four-dimensional spacetime, demonstrating that a nonlinear spinor system naturally constrains the background geometry to the Kastor-Traschen spacetime, which describes dynamical, cosmic multi-black-holes. By analyzing a nonlinear field equation where the super-covariant derivative of a Dirac spinor is sourced by intrinsic fermion currents, we evaluate its integrability under two physical requirements: a phase-locking condition enforcing a real hermitian product $\overlineψ_R ψ_L$, and a pure-electric source condition. We show that the field equation uniquely compels the spinor to be an eigenvector of the super-covariant Dirac operator, with the eigenvalue dynamically identified as the Hubble constant of the de Sitter background. These results provide a novel geometric mechanism wherein both the cosmological constant and dynamical black hole configurations spontaneously emerge from fermion condensates.

### [85] [Anti-Ultralocality and Plateau Models of Inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24997)

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 从一般初始条件出发，爱因斯坦方程中梯度项相对非梯度项的增长要么阻止暴胀持续足够多的e-fold数，要么触发量子逃逸阶段。避免这些问题的初始条件精细调节难度随暴胀能量尺度减小而加剧。

Abstract: Anti-ultralocality refers to the growth of spatial gradient terms relative to velocity terms in the coupled Einstein--scalar field equations. It is a characteristic feature of decelerated expansion before the onset of inflation. In this paper, we show that models with plateau-shaped inflaton potentials, which are considered to be the simplest way to generate a tensor-to-scalar ratio below current observational upper limits, are especially vulnerable to anti-ultralocality effects. The reasons are the flatness of the plateau and the energy density gap of $\sim 10$ orders of magnitude between the Planck density and the plateau potential energy. To study the problem, we develop a protocol for assessing the viability of inflationary models in general, and we apply it to a plateau potential using a previously validated numerical relativity code. We find that, starting from generic initial conditions, the growth of gradient terms in the Einstein equations relative to non-gradient terms either prevents inflation from lasting for enough $e$-folds or triggers a phase of quantum runaway. We show that the fine-tuning of initial conditions necessary to avoid these issues becomes more severe as the energy scale of inflation is made smaller, disfavoring common approaches for reducing the tensor-to-scalar ratio.

</details>

Method: 作者聚焦于$(\mathcal{M},g)$的不完全测地线，构造时空的包络$(\mathcal{M},g,\hat{\mathcal{M}})$，使不完全测地线$γ:[0,1) \rightarrow \mathcal{M}$在$\hat{\mathcal{M}}$中获得终点$q$。若不存在趋近$q$且相互交织的测地线对，则判定$q$为奇点；该奇点不会被具有无限仿射参数（infinite affine parameter）的测地线趋近，因而无法覆盖无穷远点。该方法被应用于Graf和Beld-Serrano在arXiv:2307.11034中引入的极大g-边界，并用于分析方向性奇点（directional singularity）与纯奇点之间的可分离性。

Conclusion: 文章在抽象边界框架（abstract boundary framework）中确立了纯奇点的判定方法，并证明了该框架下奇点与无穷远点的可分离性；端点定理的适用性通过Schwarzschild时空得到了验证，为时空边界理论提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: Ideal attached points are a core concept in general relativity for pseudo-Riemannian manifolds, and whether the spacetime can be extended with certain properties is a central consideration in their choice. This paper establishes a sufficient condition for the separability between singularities and points at infinity for any maximally extended pseudo-Riemannian manifold. We focus on the incomplete geodesics of $(\mathcal{M},g)$, and produce an envelopment $(\mathcal{M},g,\hat{\mathcal{M}})$ of the spacetime such that an incomplete geodesic $γ:[0,1) \rightarrow \mathcal{M}$ has an endpoint $q$ in $\hat{\mathcal{M}}$. If there is no pair of geodesics approaching $q$ which is intertwined, then $q$ is a singularity. Additionally, $q$ will not be approached by any geodesic with infinite affine parameter, and therefore cannot cover a point at infinity, thereby rendering it a {\it pure singularity} in the abstract boundary framework. We apply the Endpoint Theorem to the maximal g-boundary introduced by Graf and Beld-Serrano in arXiv:2307.11034, and also provide a result on the separability between directional singularities and pure singularities. This analysis is then applied to the Schwarzschild spacetime.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展一种在双曲切片（hyperboloidal slices）上求解线性波动方程的稳定数值格式，以处理坐标奇异点（原点、z轴）和无穷远处的问题，为未来数值求解完全非线性系统（如爱因斯坦场方程）并提取无系统误差和规范歧义引力波奠定基础。

Method: 在球极坐标下推导完全三维SBP格式；通过紧致化（compactification）及随之的重新标度（rescaling）将无穷远包含进计算域；将Kreiss-Oliger耗散算子（dissipation operator）推广到曲线坐标，并确保在能量范数（energy norm）下满足耗散性质；提出包含所有分辨率下所有网格点的新型范数收敛测试。

Result: 数值实现采用二阶精度有限差分方法，获得了有前景的结果；所提出的新范数收敛测试在各分辨率下包含所有网格点，产生更精确的结果；格式被证明是稳定的。

Conclusion: 该格式可类似地推广到标准柯西问题（Cauchy problem）或带外边界的有限类空切片；为扩展到完全非线性系统（如爱因斯坦场方程）并提取无系统误差或规范歧义的引力波提供了希望。

Abstract: This paper summarises our previous work on a fully $3$D Summation-by-Parts scheme, derived for a class of linear wave equations on hyperboloidal slices on a fixed Minkowski background. The scheme is derived in spherical polar coordinates, and allows having grid points at the origin and on the $z$-axis, despite coordinate singularities, and at infinity, by introducing compactification followed by rescaling, and is proved to be stable. Reducing it to the standard Cauchy problem, or to finite spacelike slices with an outer boundary, will follow a similarly. Second-order accurate finite-difference methods are used to implement this scheme numerically, but higher-order finite-difference or spectral methods could also be used. Kreiss-Oliger dissipation operators are generalized to curvilinear coordinates and are defined everywhere in the domain, including at the boundary points, such that they satisfy the dissipative property in the energy norms. We also propose new norm convergence tests that include all the grid points at all resolutions and produce more accurate results. Promising results are obtained, giving hope for application to fully nonlinear systems, like the Einstein Field Equations, and extracting the resulting gravitational waves free of systematic errors or gauge ambiguities.

</details>

Abstract: We consider a class of three-parameter metrics that was introduced in \cite{azizallahi2024three}. The class of metrics contain Zipoy-Voorhees (ZV) and Fisher-Janis-Newman- Winicour (FJNW) spacetimes at certain values of the parameters. Method: 将Kerr-Dirac系统映射为全局积分的有源径向问题，严格分离由视界因果性（horizon causality）决定的边界约束；通过角向正则分支分析研究拓扑结构；利用径向指标分析（indicial analysis）考察Frobenius矩阵的缺陷性与对数发散行为。

Result: 由于方位角量子数为严格半整数，角向正则分支阻止局部场密度在旋转轴上消失，从而使旋转费米子云形成整体填充的扁椭球几何；在同步化运动学位置，Frobenius矩阵非缺陷且不含对数发散，导致零源振幅被因果通量势垒严格平凡化。

Conclusion: 黑洞支撑宏观定态场的能力不仅由超辐射（superradiant）运动学决定，还取决于局部视界因果性与量子自旋统计（quantum spin statistics）之间的深刻相互作用；该同步化否决（synchronization veto）揭示了费米子场与玻色子场在黑洞背景上的根本差异。

Abstract: Ultralight bosonic fields can form macroscopic clouds around rotating black holes, whereas the existence of analogous stationary fermionic condensates is strictly constrained by their intrinsic spin. Here we establish a complete geometric and kinematic framework to resolve the stationary bound states of massive Dirac fields on Kerr and Kerr-Newman backgrounds. By mapping the Kerr-Dirac system to a globally integrated sourced radial problem, we strictly isolate the boundary constraints dictated by horizon causality. The angular sector reveals a fundamental topological distinction: because the azimuthal quantum number is strictly half-integer, the regular boundary branches prevent the local field density from vanishing on the rotation axis. Consequently, rotating fermionic clouds inherently form globally filled, oblate geometries, in stark contrast to the hollow toroidal structures characteristic of scalar condensates. Crucially, our radial indicial analysis unveils the exact mathematical origin of the absence of synchronized Dirac hair. Precisely at the kinematic synchronization locus, the Frobenius matrix of the Dirac operator is non-defective and entirely devoid of logarithmic divergences. Without these singular branches to be selectively excised by boundary regularity, the physical burden of existence falls entirely onto the causal flux barrier, which strictly trivializes the zero-source amplitude. This synchronization veto demonstrates that a black hole's capacity to support macroscopic stationary fields is governed not merely by superradiant kinematics, but by the profound interplay between local horizon causality and quantum spin statistics.

</details>

Conclusion: 该研究建立了弱背景场下粒子对产生概率的统一计算框架，并将其与引力场中的物质分布联系起来，展示了方法在真实天体物理系统（如双星系统）中的可用性，为后续研究提供了有力工具。

Abstract: We study pair creation for quantum fields of spin $0$, $1/2$, and $1$ in the presence of weak gravitational and gauge backgrounds. ### [93] [Two-branch detector response for Dirac infall into a Schwarzschild--MOG black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25566)

  <summary>Details</summary>
