Abstract: We study a localized spin-$1/2$ detector falling into a Schwarzschild--MOG black hole. The detector interacts with a neutral massless scalar field through a charge-preserving two-level transition, while its translational wave packet obeys the MOG-charged Dirac equation. Near the outer horizon, the separated radial system reduces to an inverse-square equation with index $Θ_{\rm D}=E_{\rm H}/(2\hbarκ_α)$. The gauge-invariant horizon energy satisfies $E_{\rm H}=m_ψU_{\rm H}>0$ for every future-directed crossing trajectory. We quantize the scalar field in a globally normalized Boulware scattering basis and retain both radial-flux branches of a mode that is outgoing at infinity. This treatment does not identify a local outgoing ansatz with a complete mode. A finite radial gate $χ_p(x)=(x/L_χ)^p e^{-x/L_χ}$ gives closed-form excitation and absorption probability densities that include the ingoing contribution and scattering-phase interference. Within the controlled near-horizon approximation, the full detailed-balance ratio factorizes into a branch-resolved outgoing ratio and a two-branch factor. Only the outgoing ratio approaches $\exp(-2πν/κ_α)$ when the near-horizon, adiabatic, high-gap, and branch-isolation conditions all hold. The leading switching correction is controlled by $(ν/κ_α)/(S_\pm L_χ)$, not by $(S_\pm L_χ)^{-1}$ alone. In a weak-MOG expansion, the local correction separates into surface-gravity, trajectory-prefactor, and finite-gate terms. The full response also contains a contribution from global scattering. This separation shows which terms follow from the local horizon geometry and which depend on the detector protocol and scalar propagation outside the horizon region.

</details>


### [94] [Reconstructing $f(R)$ gravity from generalized entropies: exact Lagrangians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25722)
*Ankit Anand,Sahil Devdutt,Kimet Jusufi,Ayan Chatterjee,Emmanuel N. Saridakis*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过Wald构造（Wald construction）从广义视界熵（generalized horizon entropies）重构度量f(R)引力，建立了从熵-面积关系到修正引力的非微扰直接联系，并给出了分支依赖的判定准则与稳定性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义视界熵作为Bekenstein-Hawking面积定律的理论修正被广泛使用，但通过Wald构造，这些熵修正可能隐含着底层引力动力学的修改。本文旨在系统性地从给定的熵-面积关系重构f(R)引力，揭示这一过程的固有结构与物理含义。

Method: 从预设的熵-面积关系出发重构度量f(R)引力；在最大对称分支（maximally symmetric branch，A=48π/R精确成立）上，重构约化为单一求积（quadrature），可实现非微扰求解。推导了若干广义熵的闭合形式拉格朗日量，并建立分支无关的稳定性判据与标量场质量公式。

Result: 熵项a·S_{BH}^q产生与R^{2-q}成正比的曲率项；Kaniadakis熵（Kaniadakis entropy）产生1/R修正项，对数熵（logarithmic entropy）产生R²ln R项。推导了分支无关判据∂²_R f=(ds/dR)d(S/s)/ds联系Dolgov-Kawasaki稳定性，以及最大对称分支上的标量质量m²_sc=S'(s)/(3f_RR)。与固定质量Schwarzschild-de Sitter分支的对比显示重构拉格朗日量不同且稳定性属性相反。弱孤立视界boost电荷再现原始广义熵。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了广义视界热力学与修正引力动力学之间的直接非微扰联系。重构过程本质上依赖于分支选择（branch dependent），但存在分支无关的判定准则，这为从热力学信息约束引力理论提供了坚实的框架。

Abstract: Generalized horizon entropies are widely used as theoretical modifications of the Bekenstein-Hawking area law, but through the Wald construction they may also encode modifications of the underlying gravitational dynamics. We reconstruct metric $f(R)$ gravity from prescribed entropy-area relations and show that the procedure is intrinsically branch dependent through the required area-curvature map. On the maximally symmetric branch, where $A=48π/R$ exactly, the reconstruction reduces to a single quadrature and can be performed non-perturbatively. We obtain closed-form Lagrangians for several generalized entropies and show that an entropy term $a~S_{BH}^{q}$ generates a curvature term proportional to $R^{2-q}$. In particular, Kaniadakis entropy produces a $1/R$ correction, while logarithmic entropy generates an $R^2\ln R$ term. We further derive a branch-independent criterion, $\partial_{R}^{2} f=(ds/dR)d(S/s)/ds$, relating Dolgov-Kawasaki stability directly to the entropy functional, together with $m_{\rm sc}^2=S'(s)/(3f_{RR})$ on the maximally symmetric branch. Comparison with the fixed-mass Schwarzschild-de Sitter branch reveals different reconstructed Lagrangians and reversed stability properties. Finally, the weak-isolated-horizon boost charge reproduces the original generalized entropy. These results establish a direct non-perturbative link between generalized horizon thermodynamics and modified gravitational dynamics.

</details>


### [95] [Vacuum energy problem in anti-de Sitter space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25860)
*E. V. Arbuzova,A. D. Dolgov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究具有初始大负真空能（对应反德西特时空）的宇宙学演化，提出一种基于标量场与曲率标量非最小耦合的动力学补偿机制，该机制可完全抵消真空能的引力效应，使宇宙避免奇点坍缩并进入辐射主导的幂律膨胀阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙若初始具有大的负真空能，将对应于反德西特（anti-de Sitter, AdS）时空，不可避免地收缩并坍缩为奇点。如何阻止这一坍缩、避免奇点形成，是本文拟解决的核心问题。

Method: 提出一种基于标量场（scalar field）与曲率标量（curvature scalar）非最小耦合（non-minimal coupling）的真空能补偿动力学机制，并通过数值计算（numerical calculations）和解析计算（analytical calculations）验证该机制的有效性。

Result: 数值与解析计算表明，所提出的机制能完全补偿真空能的引力效应，成功将曲率驱动至零，从而避免奇点。最终宇宙进入典型辐射主导宇宙学（radiation-dominated cosmology）的幂律膨胀（power-law expansion）阶段。

Conclusion: 该动力学补偿机制为负真空能宇宙提供了一条避免奇点的演化路径，使宇宙能够从AdS初态过渡到辐射主导的膨胀宇宙学。

Abstract: We investigate the cosmological evolution of a universe with an initially large negative vacuum energy, corresponding to an anti-de Sitter (AdS) space-time. Such a universe inevitably contracts and collapses into a singularity. To prevent this collapse, we propose a dynamical mechanism of vacuum energy compensation based on a scalar field non-minimally coupled to the curvature scalar. Numerical and analytical calculations demonstrate that the suggested mechanism completely compensates the gravitational impact of the vacuum energy, successfully driving the curvature to zero. As a result, the universe avoids the singularity and evolves into a power-law expansion, typical for a radiation-dominated cosmology.

</details>


### [96] [Mode stability for the scalar wave equation on subextremal Kerr-de Sitter spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25868)
*Peter Hintz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明了 Kerr-de Sitter 黑洞时空（Kerr-de Sitter black hole spacetimes）在全次极值参数区间（full subextremal range）内无质量标量波方程（massless scalar wave equation）的模式稳定性（mode stability），并结合 Petersen-Vasy 的近期工作，无条件证明了标量波方程充分正则的解以指数速度指数衰减到常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kerr-de Sitter 黑洞时空上的波动方程稳定性是一个重要的数学物理问题，模式稳定性是证明长时间衰减行为的核心先决条件。此前该性质仅在有限参数范围内被证明，本文旨在填补全次极值区间内的空白。

Method: 本文采用解析方法证明模式稳定性，即证明标量波方程不存在衰减至零的亏损模态（decaying modes）。证明过程与 Petersen-Vasy 的近期框架相结合，从而闭合稳定性论证的链条。

Result: 在全次极值参数范围内证明了 Kerr-de Sitter 时空中无质量标量波方程的模式稳定性，并由此无条件地得到充分正则解以指数速率衰减到常数的结论。

Conclusion: 该结果完整解决了 Kerr-de Sitter 黑洞时空上标量波方程的模式稳定性问题，为更一般的拟线性波动方程稳定性理论提供了重要基础。

Abstract: We prove mode stability for the massless scalar wave equation on Kerr-de Sitter black hole spacetimes in the full subextremal range. In combination with recent work by Petersen-Vasy, this proves, unconditionally, that sufficiently regular solutions of the scalar wave equation decay exponentially fast to constants.

</details>


### [97] [Atmospheric Newtonian noise in torsion-balance measurements of the gravitational constant $G$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26020)
*Jyotirmaya Mohanta,Yutaka Shikano*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个符合GUM规范的框架，用于将大气密度涨落产生的牛顿噪声（Newtonian noise）经由扭矩估计器传播至扭秤（torsion-balance）测量的不确定度预算中，并通过封闭形式的空间传递函数分析了两种基准几何构型下的耦合与抑制特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牛顿引力常数的测量仍受环境及装置依赖的系统效应（systematics）限制，而大气密度涨落产生的不可屏蔽重力梯度会以大气牛顿噪声的形式直接耦合到扭秤观测量中，但目前缺乏对此贡献进行系统化不确定性传播的规范框架。

Method: 开发了一个符合GUM（测量不确定度表示指南）规范的框架，将该噪声贡献经扭矩估计器传播至扭秤测量的不确定度预算中；推导了两种基准几何的封闭形式空间传递函数（transfer function）：双质量哑铃（dumbbell）构型（提供上耦合基准）与完全对称十字（cross）构型（代表理想化抑制极限）；将平稳相关输入与非平稳基线漂移分离，并以Ornstein–Uhlenbeck过程仅作为前者的基准模型。

Result: 大气压强基准分析表明，由此产生的大气背景贡献目前低于现有参考不确定度水平，但在系统效应下限逼近百万分之一（part-per-million）量级时变得不可忽略。完全对称十字构型暴露了抑制低阶环境耦合与保持信号响应之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 该框架为将站点特定（site-specific）的环境重力梯度纳入未来扭秤测量的不确定度预算提供了实用途径。

Abstract: Measurements of Newton's gravitational constant remain limited by environmental and apparatus-dependent systematics whose treatment is often less explicit than that of instrumental noise. Among these, atmospheric density fluctuations generate unshieldable gravity gradients that couple directly to torsion-balance observables as atmospheric Newtonian noise. Here, we develop a GUM-consistent framework for propagating this contribution through the torque estimator into the uncertainty budget of torsion-balance measurements. We derive closed-form spatial transfer functions for two benchmark geometries: a two-mass dumbbell, which provides an upper-coupling reference, and a perfectly symmetric cross, which serves as an idealized rejection limit and exposes the trade-off between suppressing low-order environmental coupling and preserving signal response. We also separate stationary correlated inputs from non-stationary baseline drift, using the Ornstein--Uhlenbeck process only as a benchmark for the former. Atmospheric pressure benchmarks indicate that the resulting background contribution is below present reference uncertainty levels, but can become relevant as systematic floors approach the part-per-million regime. This framework provides a practical route for incorporating site-specific environmental gravity gradients into future torsion-balance uncertainty budgets.

</details>


### [98] [Torsion balances as operational probes of semiclassical gravity: Matched-filter bounds, torque-diffusion constraints, and quantum-noise benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26096)
*Jyotirmaya Mohanta,Yutaka Shikano*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套统一的统计框架，用于从校准的扭秤（torsion balance）谱数据中约束标准牛顿引力的确定性偏差与随机性偏差，涵盖噪声预算扣除、匹配滤波边界以及扭矩扩散系数约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭秤实验既可用于检验确定性的牛顿引力偏离，也可用于搜索由半经典引力（semiclassical gravity）等理论预言的随机扭矩噪声，但此前缺乏统一且校准过的统计分析方法将这些不同目标衔接起来。

Method: 利用单侧谱数据推导角度等效噪声预算及有限时间匹配滤波（matched-filter）/Cramér-Rao 界；对于随机模型，从观测角度谱中扣除校准的标准噪声预算（热、牛顿、环境、测量不精密度与反作用）得到残余谱，再借助校准的扭转磁化率（torsional susceptibility）转换为等效残余扭矩谱，进而约束额外平稳随机扭矩噪声；Page–Geilker 分支判别与 Fedida–Kent 混合等价检验经投影到扭矩模板后被统一为同一匹配滤波判别问题。

Result: 室温 Cavendish 基准实验的热噪声角度 ASD 为 1.36×10⁻⁴ rad/√Hz，测量引入的标准量子极限（SQL）为 3.25×10⁻¹² rad/√Hz。针对 Yan 等人的实验，其在 2.5 mHz 处报告的 0.3 μrad/√Hz 灵敏度可给出白噪声假设下的保守约束 D_τ ≲ 2.4×10⁻²³ N²·m²·s。

Conclusion: 该框架为校准扭秤数据、确定性检验和随机半经典引力搜索之间提供了统一的接口，但作者强调这并不等于直接检验了完整的相对论性半经典爱因斯坦方程（semiclassical Einstein equation）；频率分辨的残余谱仅在马尔可夫白噪声极限下可压缩为单一扭矩扩散系数 D_τ，非马尔可夫或有色模型需使用完整残余谱。

Abstract: Calibrated torsion-balance spectra constrain deterministic and stochastic deviations from standard Newtonian gravity. Using one-sided spectra, we derive a calibrated angle-equivalent noise budget and finite-time matched-filter/Cramér-Rao bounds for known torque templates. For stochastic models, subtracting the calibrated standard noise budget (thermal, Newtonian, environmental, imprecision, backaction) from the observed angle spectrum yields a residual spectrum, convertible to an equivalent residual torque spectrum via calibrated torsional susceptibility. This bounds additional stationary stochastic torque noise. This frequency-resolved bound compresses to a single torque-diffusion coefficient, $D_τ$, only in the Markovian white-noise limit; non-Markovian or colored models require the full residual spectrum. Page--Geilker branch discrimination and Fedida-Kent mixture-equivalence tests address distinct physical questions, but upon projection onto torque templates, both reduce to the same statistical matched-filter discrimination problem. For a room-temperature Cavendish benchmark, resonant thermal angle ASD is $1.36\times10^{-4}\,\mathrm{rad}/\sqrt{\mathrm{Hz}}$, while measurement-added SQL is $3.25\times10^{-12}\,\mathrm{rad}/\sqrt{\mathrm{Hz}}$. For the Yan \emph{et al.} search, the reported $0.3\,μ\mathrm{rad}/\sqrt{\mathrm{Hz}}$ sensitivity at $2.5\,\mathrm{mHz}$ yields a conservative bound $D_τ\lesssim 2.4\times10^{-23}\,\mathrm{N^2\,m^2\,s}$, assuming white torque noise. These formulations provide an interface linking calibrated torsion-balance data, deterministic tests, and stochastic semiclassical-gravity searches, without asserting a direct test of the full relativistic semiclassical Einstein equation.

</details>

### [99] [Dual modes in Kerr spacetimes and the Whiting transform: Mode stability revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26034)
*Oliver Petersen,András Vasy*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文旨在将 Whiting 的经典增长模式稳定性论证（经 Shlapentokh-Rothman 扩展到标量波动方程实频率情形，并经 Andersson、Ma、Paganini 和 Whiting 推广到一般情形）置于经典偏微分方程（PDE）理论框架中。核心步骤包括：对偶（或伴随）模式的描述、通过傅里叶变换实现的奇异相空间配对论证，以及标准唯一延拓（unique continuation）结果的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将该稳定性论证纳入经典偏微分方程理论的框架中，以提供更系统化的数学基础，并澄清论证中各步骤之间的逻辑联系。

Method: 论文通过三个关键步骤展开：首先刻画对偶模式（dual/adjoint modes）的结构；其次通过傅里叶变换执行一个奇异的相空间配对论证（phase space pairing argument）；最后应用标准的唯一延拓结果来完成稳定性证明。

Result: 在对偶模式的描述中，论文将其直接与在事件视界（event horizon）上光滑的标准模式解联系起来。在零自旋（zero spin）情形下给出几何解释：在非零实频率下跨越未来事件视界光滑的拟正则模（quasinormal modes），可通过零延拓穿过过去事件视界，成为分布意义下的 QNM 解。此外还刻画了模式解在分叉球面（bifurcate sphere）处的行为。

Conclusion: 该工作将增长模式稳定性论证系统化地纳入经典 PDE 框架，并通过对偶模式的刻画揭示其与视界光滑模式解之间的深层联系，同时给出了零自旋情形下的几何直观解释。

Abstract: The purpose of the paper is to place Whiting's classical growing mode stability argument, extended to real frequencies by Shlapentokh-Rothman for the scalar wave equation and by Andersson, Ma, Paganini and Whiting in general, in the framework of classical PDE theory. The key steps are: a description of the dual or adjoint modes, a singular phase space pairing argument which is technically executed via the Fourier transform, followed by a standard unique continuation result.
  One part of our description of the dual modes connects them directly to the standard mode solutions which are smooth over the event horizon. In the zero spin case, there is a geometric interpretation of this: quasinormal modes (for non-zero real frequencies) which are smooth across the future event horizon can be extended as distributional QNM solutions by 0 through the past event horizon. We also describe the behavior of mode solutions at the bifurcate sphere.

</details>

### [100] [Dark Matters of Principle: Principles in the Dark Matter Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25954)
*Patrick Duerr,James Read*

Main category: physics.hist-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过提出一种基于功能主义的物理学原理分类学框架，重新审视暗物质（Dark Matter, DM）问题，系统分析了超对称WIMP、轴子、惰性中微子和原初黑洞四种主流暗物质方案，论证了暗物质研究范式如何受制于共享的调节性与构成性原理，以及各方案在偏离正统物理原理时的不同程度，最终指出暗物质问题正演变为一种方法论危机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文的动机在于从物理学哲学的方法论视角重新审视暗物质问题。作者认为，仅从经验证据层面讨论暗物质存在与否是不够的，需要理解暗物质研究背后所依赖的科学原理及其方法论功能。随着暗物质候选粒子持续未获探测，作者希望阐明这种困境的本质——即研究者赖以指导搜索的物理学原理可能已经"用尽了启发性余力"（heuristic steam），从而为理解暗物质问题为何构成一种日益令人不安的危机提供哲学解释。

Method: 本文分为两步展开方法论工作。第一步，作者提出一个功能主义（functionalist）的物理学原理学说及其分类体系，主张原理应依据其预期功能或目的来定义，其论证力量取决于语境敏感的、往往具有试探性的理由。第二步，作者将此方法论框架应用于暗物质问题，对四种主流暗物质方案——超对称WIMP、轴子（axion）、惰性中微子（sterile neutrino）和原初黑洞（primordial black hole）——进行详细分析，并据此论证四项主张（C1–C4），涵盖共享原理的识别、各模型的不同偏离方式、偏离程度的差异，以及原理指导力的枯竭。

Result: 作者证实了四项核心主张：第一，暗物质范式由共享的重力与非重力物理的调节性（regulative）和构成性（constitutive）原理所围限的可能性框架构成；第二，四种暗物质模型还在不同方面偏离正统物理学的其他原理；第三，各模型的偏离程度不同，表现出对既有非重力物理原理的不同形式与程度的"最小伤害"（minimal mutilation）；第四，暗物质问题构成日益严重的危机——研究者通常依赖的原理可能已失去启发性指导力，促使学界主张"不留死角"地探索更激进的新思路。

Conclusion: 作者结论认为，"原理驱动探究"（principled inquiry）是一种在拓展知识边界时历史上广泛存在、自然且方法论上审慎的研究策略，但暗物质问题正考验该策略的极限。随着现有原理的启发力枯竭，暗物质研究应越来越多地探索更激进地偏离传统原理驱动的审慎探索路线的想法，这意味着暗物质危机不仅是经验层面的困境，更是方法论层面的转折点。

Abstract: This paper re-examines the Dark Matter (DM) problem through the lens of physical principles. We first develop a functionalist account and taxonomy of principles. We propose to define principles in terms of their intended functions or purposes. Their argumentative force boils down to context-sensitive and often tentative reasons for believing them adequate for those purposes. In research contexts in which novel theories or models are sought which push the boundaries of knowledge into new domains, reliance on well-motivated principles as principal building blocks is a historically widespread, natural, and methodologically prudent research strategy---what we'll dub "principled inquiry". In a second step, we apply this methodological machinery to the DM problem. Through detailed analyses of four mainstream DM approaches---supersymmetric WIMPs, axions, sterile neutrinos, and primordial black holes---we substantiate four claims. (C1) The DM paradigm is formed by the framework of possibilities, circumscribed by a shared cluster of regulative and constitutive principles of gravitational and non-gravitational physics. (C2) Besides those common principles, the four DM models also depart from other principles of orthodox physics in distinct ways. (C3) They do so to different degrees---displaying different forms and extents of "minimal mutilation" with respect to some of the principles of established non-gravitational physics. (C4) The guidelines of principled inquiry clarify the sense in which the DM problem constitutes an ever-more disconcerting crisis: worries are growing that the principles on which DM researchers have routinely and rationally relied may have run out of heuristic steam. Pressure is mounting to "leave no stone unturned" (Bertone and Tait 2018): scientists ought to increasingly explore ideas that stray more radically from the cautious explorations of principled inquiry.

</details>

### [101] [Development and Commissioning of the Cryogenic Target Detectors for the Technical Run of the NUCLEUS Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25595)
*N. Schermer,H. Abele,G. Angloher,B. Arnold,M. Atzori Corona,A. Bento,E. Bossio,F. Buchsteiner,J. Burkhart,F. Cappella,M. Cappelli,N. Casali,R. Cerulli,A. Cruciani,G. Del Castello,S. Dorer,A. Erhart,M. Friedl,S. Fichtinger,V. M. Ghete,M. Giammei,J. Hakenmüller,D. Hauff,M. Hock,F. Jeanneau,E. Jericha,M. Kaznacheeva,H. Kluck,A. Langenkämper,T. Lasserre,D. Lhuillier,M. Mancuso,B. Mauri,A. Mazzolari,L. McCallin,H. Neyrial,C. Nones,L. Oberauer,L. Peters,F. Petricca,W. Potzel,F. Pröbst,F. Reindl,M. Romagnoni,J. Rothe,J. Schieck,S. Schönert,C. Schwertner,L. Scola,L. Stodolsky,A. Schröder,R. Strauss,R. Thalmeier,C. Tomei,L. Valla,M. Vignati,M. Vivier,A. Wallach,P. Wasser,L. Wienke*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本文报道了NUCLEUS实验的低温靶探测器模块在慕尼黑工业大学（Technical University of Munich）的研制与调试结果。模块包含四个克量级CaWO₄探测器、总质量6.96 g，平均基线分辨率为(2.79 ± 0.60) eV，最优探测器分辨率达(2.16 ± 0.02) eV，超过设计目标两倍，且与低温外反符合系统（Cryogenic Outer Veto）联机运行无串扰，证明了该系统已为Chooz反应堆的CEνNS技术运行做好准备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NUCLEUS实验旨在法国Chooz核电站测量反应堆电子反中微子的相干弹性中微子-核散射（coherent elastic neutrino-nucleus scattering，CEνNS），该测量需要具备极高能量分辨率和低背景的低温探测器系统。

Method: 研制并调试了一个包含四个克量级CaWO₄探测器（每个配备两个过渡边传感器 Transition Edge Sensors，TES）的低温靶探测器模块，通过X射线进行能量标定，并记录了探测器表征与性能评估数据；同时与由六个千克级Ge探测器组成的低温外反符合系统联合运行以测试信号串扰。

Result: 在六个探测器上实现了平均基线分辨率为(2.79 ± 0.60) eV；最佳探测器达到(2.16 ± 0.02_stat) eV，超过设计目标两倍，代表了低温CaWO₄探测器的先进水平。靶探测器模块与低温外反符合系统同时运行未发现可测量的串扰。

Conclusion: 靶探测器系统已具备在Chooz进行NUCLEUS技术运行（Technical Run）的条件，标志着用于反应堆CEνNS测量的低温探测器研发迈出了关键里程碑。

Abstract: The NUCLEUS experiment aims to study coherent elastic neutrino-nucleus scattering (CE$ν$NS) of reactor electron antineutrinos at the Chooz nuclear power plant in France. In this work, the cryogenic target-detector module for the NUCLEUS Technical Run was developed and commissioned at the Technical University of Munich. The module comprises four gram-scale CaWO$_4$ detectors, each equipped with two Transition Edge Sensors (TES), providing a total target mass of 6.96 g. We present the design, integration, detector characterization, and X-ray-based energy calibration of these detectors. Across six characterized detectors, a mean baseline resolution of $\overlineσ_{\mathrm{BL}} = (2.79 \pm 0.60)\,\mathrm{eV}$ was achieved. The best-performing detector reached $σ_{\mathrm{BL}} = (2.16 \pm 0.02_{\mathrm{stat}})\,\mathrm{eV}$, surpassing the design goal by a factor of two and representing a state-of-the-art result for a cryogenic CaWO$_4$ detector. The target detector module was successfully operated simultaneously with the surrounding Cryogenic Outer Veto, which consists of six kg-scale Ge detectors for background discrimination, showing no measurable cross-talk. These results demonstrate the readiness of the target-detector system for the NUCLEUS Technical Run at Chooz and mark a key milestone in the development of cryogenic detectors for reactor-CE$ν$NS measurements.

</details>

### [102] [Exceptional supersphere integration and logarithmic Pizzetti kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21241)
*Juan Bory-Reyes,Baruch Schneider,Diana Schneiderova,Yifan Zhang*

Main category: math.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了在例外超维数（exceptional superdimension）$M=-2u$ 下正交辛不变超球面（orthosymplectically invariant supersphere）的积分理论，此时调和 Fischer 结构（harmonic Fischer structure）变为非半单的、Pizzetti 配对退化为奇异情形。通过解析延拓的齐次逆核生成函数，作者证明其 Laurent 展开的极点留数（residue）和对数有限部分（logarithmic finite part）能够恢复固定超空间上的完全对偶结构与广义调和模上的非退化 head-socle 配对，并以此推导协变径向 q-单模（q-monogenic）区域符号及标量 Pizzetti 再现的转移障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在超对称（supersymmetric）积分理论中，例外超维数 $M=-2u$ 处调和 Fischer 结构失去半单性、Pizzetti 配对退化，导致标准的反向核与对偶理论失效，需要建立新的分析框架来处理这一奇异情形。

Method: 作者对解析延拓的齐次逆核（homogeneous inverse kernel）写出显式生成函数，并研究其在 $μ=-2u$ 处的 Laurent 展开结构，通过分析极点留数与对数有限部分的代数性质，分别考察三个度数区间（$k\le u$, $u<k\le2u$, $k\ge2u+1$）中的配对行为与对偶结构。

Result: 证明留数在度数 $k\le u$ 时反转了正则化重正化配对（renormalized pairing）；在碰撞区间 $u<k\le2u$ 中有限部分重构商空间、留数经反射度输运后重现根子空间；对 $k\ge2u+1$ 有限部分即普通逆核。同时在广义调和模上建立了非退化的 head-socle 配对，并导出了协变右-左径向 q-单模区域符号及度数一障碍条件。

Conclusion: 在例外超维数下，尽管标准 Pizzetti 积分配对退化，通过 Laurent 展开的留数与有限部分仍可完整恢复超空间上的度级对偶结构；所建立的 head-socle 配对与 q-Fischer 投影分析为超空间积分与单模函数理论提供了新的工具，并明确了标量 Pizzetti 再现转移的一阶障碍。

Abstract: We study orthosymplectically invariant supersphere integration at the exceptional superdimensions $M=-2u$, where the harmonic Fischer structure becomes nonsemisimple and the Pizzetti pairing degenerates. For the meromorphically continued homogeneous inverse kernels we obtain the generating function \[
  \mathscr G_μ(ρ;x,y)
  =\frac{Γ(μ/2)}{2π^{μ/2}}
  \bigl(1+ρ\{x,y\}+ρ^2x^2y^2\bigr)^{-μ/2}. \] At $μ=-2u$, its Laurent expansion has a polynomial residue and a logarithmic finite part. We prove that these coefficients recover the complete degreewise duality structure on a fixed superspace with nonzero bosonic dimension. In degrees $k\le u$, the residue inverts a canonical renormalized pairing on $\mathcal P_k$. In the collision range $u<k\le2u$, the ordinary pairing has radical $(x^2)^{k-u}\mathcal P_{2u-k}$; the finite part reproduces the quotient, while the residue reproduces the radical after transport from the reflected degree. For $k\ge2u+1$, the finite part is the ordinary inverse kernel. We also establish the nondegenerate head--socle pairing on the generalized harmonic modules. As an application, we derive covariant right--left radial $q$-monogenic zonal symbols and identify precise degree-one obstructions to transferring scalar Pizzetti reproduction through a one-sided $q$-Fischer projection.

</details>

### [103] [Genus 4 Supermoduli Space Is Not Projected](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24996)
*Ron Donagi,Simone Noja*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了亏格为4的偶自旋分量的超模堆（supermoduli stack）${\mathfrak M}_4^+$是非投影的（non-projected），将已知的$g \ge 5$时的非投影性结果扩展到了$g = 4$的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知超模堆${\mathfrak M}_g$在亏格$g \ge 5$时是非投影的，但低亏格情形（特别是$g = 4$）的投影性尚未被完全判定。作者旨在将该非投影性结果推广到${\mathfrak M}_4$的偶自旋分量上，从而完善对超模堆几何结构的理解。

Method: 作者在自旋模空间（spin moduli space）${\mathcal S\mathcal M}_4^+$中构造了一条适当的紧曲线（compact curve），然后计算阻碍${\mathfrak M}_4^+$投影性的类在该曲线上的限制。通过验证该限制非零，即可证明${\mathfrak M}_4^+$是非投影的。

Result: 所构造曲线上阻碍投影性的类别限制（restriction）被证明是非零的，由此确立了${\mathfrak M}_4^+$是非投影的这一结论。

Conclusion: 本文成功将超模堆非投影性结果从$g \ge 5$扩展至$g = 4$的偶自旋分量，填补了低亏格情形下关于超模堆投影性的一个重要空白。

Abstract: The supermoduli stack ${\mathfrak M}_g$, parametrizing smooth unpunctured super Riemann surfaces of genus $g$, is known to be non-projected for $g \ge 5$. We extend this result to the even-spin component of ${\mathfrak M}_4$. This is done by exhibiting an appropriate compact curve in the spin moduli space ${\mathcal S\mathcal M}_4^{+}$, and computing the restriction to it of the class obstructing the projectedness of ${\mathfrak M}_4^{+}$. This restriction turns out to be non-zero, proving that ${\mathfrak M}_4^{+}$ is non-projected.

</details>


### [104] [From Spectral Curves to Variational Equations: Geometry and Differential Galois Theory of KdV Cnoidal Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25227)
*Juan Jose Morales-Ruiz,Jean Pierre Ramis,Maria-Angeles Zurro*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种谱几何（spectral geometry）描述，将KdV变分方程（KdV variational equation）在椭余弦波（cnoidal wave）附近的动力学用两条相互作用的谱曲线（spectral curves）来刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KdV方程在周期波解附近的动力学具有深刻而复杂的几何结构，需要一种自然的框架来理解其变分方程的结构。谱曲线作为可积系统的核心工具，有望提供统一的几何视角。

Method: 作者采用谱几何方法，将KdV变分方程围绕椭余弦波展开，并引入两条相互作用的谱曲线来描述系统在该背景解附近的演化行为。

Result: 得到了KdV变分方程在椭余弦波附近的谱几何描述，其中涉及两条谱曲线及其相互作用。

Conclusion: 摘要未提供明确的结论性表述，仅指出了一个谱几何描述框架的存在，具体性质与推论需参考正文。

Abstract: We provide a spectral geometric description of the KdV variational equation around cnoidal waves in terms of two interacting spectral curves.

</details>


### [105] [Spin structures and measures on the moduli space of curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25237)
*Paul Norbury*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该文基于CIME暑期学校迷你课程讲义，发展光滑自旋曲线模空间（moduli space of smooth spin curves）${\cal M}_{g,n}^{\rm spin}$上的微分几何构造，重点强调其在双曲几何（hyperbolic geometry）中的解释，并构建特征形式与自旋测度，为双曲曲面体积及其递推关系提供Teichmüller方法框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前双曲自旋结构与全纯层的对应关系仅适用于一般标记点，尚未推广到具有Ramond标记点的自旋曲线情形；同时，自旋模空间上的体积计算缺乏系统的Teichmüller理论工具。

Method: 从CIME暑期学校课程笔记出发，在光滑自旋曲线模空间${\cal M}_{g,n}^{\rm spin}$上发展微分几何构造，借助双曲几何解释；将双曲自旋结构诱导的局部常值层（locally constant sheaf）与相应全纯层（holomorphic sheaf）对应，推广至含Ramond标记点的情形；利用双曲描述构造特征形式（characteristic forms）与自旋测度（spin measures）。

Result: 成功将双曲自旋结构与全纯层的已知对应关系推广至含Ramond标记点的自旋曲线；在带测地边界的双曲曲面模空间上构造了特征形式与自旋测度；分别研究自旋模空间的偶分量与奇分量，发现了体积贡献之间的对称性，这种对称性从单个边界层（boundary strata）的贡献中并不显式可见。

Conclusion: 自旋模空间的偶、奇分量在体积贡献上存在非平凡对称性，为利用Teichmüller理论方法计算双曲表面体积及其递推关系提供了统一框架。

Abstract: This article arose out of notes for a CIME summer school mini-course in Cetraro. In it we develop differential-geometric constructions on the moduli space ${\cal M}_{g,n}^{\rm spin}$ of smooth spin curves, with particular emphasis on their interpretation in hyperbolic geometry. We relate the locally constant sheaf arising from a hyperbolic spin structure to the corresponding holomorphic sheaf, extending this known relationship to spin curves with Ramond marked points. We use the hyperbolic description to construct characteristic forms and spin measures on moduli spaces of hyperbolic surfaces with geodesic boundary, providing a framework for applying Teichmüller-theoretic methods to their volumes and volume recursions. Finally, we study separately the even and odd components of spin moduli space and find symmetries between their volume contributions which are not apparent from the contributions of individual boundary strata.

</details>


### [106] [On monodromy of monodromy surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25594)
*Pieter Roffelsen,Alexander Stokes*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明Riemann-Hilbert对应将Painlevé方程的初始值空间与由线性常微分方程的单值不变量构造的仿射簇（即单值曲面）联系起来，并证明此类仿射簇可嵌入为仿射del Pezzo曲面。文章针对Painlevé VI、IV、II、I四类方程，证明其单值群构成对应仿射Weyl对称群的有限部分，并从解析、Galois理论及组合学三个角度实现了该单值群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在为Painlevé方程的单值曲面（monodromy surface）提供更几何化的刻画——将其实现为嵌入的仿射del Pezzo曲面，并澄清单值群（monodromy group）与仿射Weyl对称群之间的关系，从而揭示Painlevé方程参数空间的模解释与Riemann-Hilbert对应下对称群结构的命运。

Method: 作者针对Painlevé VI、IV、II、I四类方程，构造其单值曲面作为嵌入的仿射del Pezzo曲面，并以其次数（degree）和无穷远处分量来描述。进而通过三种途径实现单值群：解析上作为参数空间环路沿续在直线置换上的诱导作用，Galois理论层面通过直线的关联簇（incidence variety）的函数域刻画，以及组合上通过直线的交图（intersection graph）实现。

Result: 作者证明这些单值群恰好构成对应Painlevé方程仿射Weyl对称群的有限部分。由此可将Painlevé方程的参数空间（模对称）解释为嵌入仿射簇范畴的模空间（moduli space）。同时发现，尽管Riemann-Hilbert映射共轭下仿射Weyl群对称变得平凡，有限Weyl群部分仍作为单值曲面的单值群存留。

Conclusion: 本文建立了Painlevé方程单值曲面与嵌入仿射del Pezzo曲面之间的对应，并通过三种等价视角统一了单值群的描述，揭示了有限Weyl群在Riemann-Hilbert对应下作为单值不变量存留的关键结构。

Abstract: Different instances of the Riemann-Hilbert correspondence relate initial value spaces of Painlevé equations to affine varieties known as monodromy surfaces, built from monodromy invariants for associated linear ODEs. We show that these affine varieties admit realisations as embedded affine del Pezzo surfaces, characterised by their degree and a prescribed divisor at infinity, first in this paper for cases associated with Painlevé equations $\rm{VI},\rm{IV},\rm{II},\rm{I}$. We prove that the monodromy groups of these monodromy surfaces form the finite parts of the affine Weyl symmetry groups of the corresponding Painlevé equations. We realise the monodromy group in each case: analytically as permutations of lines induced by continuation along loops in parameter space, Galois-theoretically in terms of the function field of the incidence variety of lines, and combinatorially via their intersection graph. This in particular yields an interpretation of the parameter spaces of Painlevé equations, modulo symmetries, as moduli spaces of categories of embedded affine varieties. Further, it shows that, despite the affine Weyl group symmetries becoming trivial when conjugated by the corresponding Riemann-Hilbert map, the finite Weyl group part survives as monodromy of the monodromy surface.

</details>


### [107] [Quasimaps to Nakajima varieties as critical loci](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25938)
*Spencer Tamagni*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本注释观测到：从射影直线（projective line）$\mathbb{P}^1$到Nakajima箭图簇（Nakajima quiver variety）$X$、且将无穷远点送到给定点的一类准映射（quasimaps）所构成的参数空间，可以整体性地呈现为一个显式函数的临界轨迹（critical locus）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Nakajima箭图簇是几何表示论与可积系统研究中的核心对象，其相关准映射空间的几何结构具有重要研究价值。本文旨在给出这类参数空间的全局显式刻画。

Method: 通过构造一个显式函数，将该准映射参数空间呈现为该函数的临界轨迹（critical locus），从而实现整体性（globally）描述。

Result: 证明了从$\mathbb{P}^1$到Nakajima箭图簇$X$、在无穷远点处取值固定的准映射所构成的参数空间，可以整体地表示为某个显式函数的临界轨迹。

Conclusion: 该结果为准映射参数空间的几何结构提供了显式且全局的刻画，为进一步研究其变形理论、交叉理论（intersection theory）及可积性提供了新的视角。

Abstract: $X$ is a Nakajima quiver variety. In this note we report the observation that the space parameterizing quasimaps from $\mathbb{P}^1$ to $X$, sending infinity to a given point, may be presented globally as the critical locus of an explicit function.

</details>


### [108] [Topological String Blowup Equations via Stable Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25397)
*Lutian Zhao*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文针对局部 Hirzebruch 三流形 \(Y_\ell\)（\(0\le\ell\le2\)）证明了双变量、环面等变（torus-equivariant）对称化 K 理论稳定对序列（stable-pair series）的 blowup 方程，并利用 framed 秩二层模空间上的相关量将其与单位性方程和消失方程联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在为局部 Hirzebruch 三流形上的稳定对不变量建立 blowup 方程，从而将其与模空间上的等变 Euler 示性数级数建立精确对应，为进一步理解不变量的结构性质提供方法。

Method: 采用四图局部化（four-chart localization）方法，将稳定对级数除以纤维类贡献后，与 framed 秩二层模空间上的等变 Euler 示性数级数按系数逐一对应；其中 \(\mathcal V\) 表示反演层（tautological bundle），并利用框架层 blowup 公式推导出单位性方程（unity equation）和消失方程（vanishing equation）。

Result: 对 \(\ell=0,1,2\) 三种情况证明了 blowup 方程，其中 \(\ell=2\) 的情形给出局部化指标（localized indices）的恒等式；同时单独陈述了有条件的局部 \(\mathbb P^2\) 方程所需的两个猜想、一般的 Huang–Sun–Wang 猜想，以及与 \(E_8\) 格相关的有理-椭圆特化结果。

Conclusion: 本文建立的 blowup 方程为稳定对不变量与模空间 Euler 示性数之间提供了统一的框架性联系，并将若干未解决猜想单独列出，为后续研究指明方向。

Abstract: For the local Hirzebruch threefolds \(Y_\ell=\operatorname{Tot}_{\mathbb F_\ell}K_{\mathbb F_\ell}\), \(0\leq\ell\leq2\), we prove blowup equations for the two-variable, torus-equivariant symmetrized \(K\)-theoretic stable-pair series. After division by the fibre-class contribution, four-chart localization is identified coefficientwise with the equivariant Euler-characteristic series of \((\det\mathcal V)^\ell\) on moduli spaces of framed rank-two sheaves, with \(\mathcal V\) tautological. The framed-sheaf blowup formulas then give the unity and vanishing equations; for \(\ell=2\) this is an identity of localized indices. We state separately the two conjectures needed for the conditional local \(\mathbb P^2\) equations, the general Huang--Sun--Wang conjecture, and a rational-elliptic specialization involving the \(E_8\) lattice.

</details>


<div id='hep-ex'></div>

# hep-ex [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [ADoNIS: A Differentiable generatOr of Neutrino Interaction Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25107)
*César Jesús-Valls*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出 ADoNIS，一个完全可微的（fully differentiable）中微子相互作用事件发生器，能够直接提供事件权重及其梯度，通过潜硬散射振幅（hard-scattering amplitudes）和随机核内级联（stochastic intranuclear cascade）传播精确导数。ADoNIS 在保持物理保真度的同时，为拟合设计、模型调优、测量设计与实验设计提供了定量化工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确振荡分析（precision oscillation analyses）依赖中微子相互作用发生器，但传统实现不直接提供预测对物理参数的导数，分析中通常需借助重加权（reweighting）、有限差分（finite differences）或外部响应模型来获取参数依赖关系，缺乏精确且高效的梯度信息。

Method: ADoNIS 是一个完全可微的（fully differentiable）中微子事件发生器，它沿袭 ACHILLES 的物理选择，并通过传播精确导数覆盖核基态（nuclear ground state）、硬散射振幅（hard-scattering amplitudes）以及随机核内级联（stochastic intranuclear cascade）全过程，使事件重加权及其梯度直接可用。

Result: ADoNIS 在中微子、电子和强子（hadron）探针上的预测与 ACHILLES 一致，表明可微性不以牺牲物理保真度为代价。所获得的雅可比矩阵（Jacobian）可揭示哪些测量和相空间区域约束各参数，以及不同探针如何打破简并（degeneracies）。可微预测同时适用于频率学派（frequentist）与贝叶斯（Bayesian）推断，并可携带至探测器响应与反解（unfolding）过程中，同时带来计算效率的提升。

Conclusion: ADoNIS 为构建可微中微子事件发生器提供了通用方法，其适用性不限于本文实现的特定物理模型；论文展示了该方法在理论、实验相关的广泛任务中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Neutrino interaction generators are central to precision oscillation analyses, but conventional implementations do not directly provide derivatives of their predictions with respect to physics parameters, leaving analyses to obtain this dependence through reweighting, finite differences or external response models. We present ADoNIS, a fully differentiable neutrino interaction event generator that makes event reweighting and its gradients directly available, propagating exact derivatives through the nuclear ground state, hard-scattering amplitudes and stochastic intranuclear cascade. Following the physics choices of ACHILLES, we demonstrate agreement with its predictions across neutrino, electron and hadron probes, showing that differentiability is achieved without loss of physical fidelity. We then show how the resulting Jacobian exposes which measurements and regions of phase space constrain each parameter and how different probes break degeneracies, providing a quantitative tool for fit design, model tuning, measurement design and experiment design. The same differentiable predictions can be used directly in frequentist and Bayesian inference and carried through detector response to reconstructed observables for unfolding, while also yielding computational gains. In sum, ADoNIS provides a practical recipe for constructing differentiable neutrino event generators, applicable beyond the particular physics models implemented here, and this paper demonstrates their use across a wide range of tasks relevant to both theory and experiment.

</details>


### [110] [Operation of Unshielded Kinetic-Inductance Traveling-Wave Parametric Amplifiers in Multi-Tesla Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25328)
*Christian Boutan,Erik Lentz,Corwin Shiu,Deniz Erdag,Stephen Jones,Joseph Van Vlack,Logan Howe,Andrea Giachero,Paul Szypryt,Michael Vissers,Jason Austermann,Johannes Hubmayr,Stefan Knirck,Douglas Bennett,Joel Ullom*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文首次系统验证了动力学电感行波参量放大器（KTWPA）可在多特斯拉（multi-Tesla）磁场环境下保持高增益（>20dB）和多GHz带宽，并揭示了其增益对磁场强度、器件取向、偏置电流及泵浦参数的系统依赖关系，为其在暗物质轴子搜索等强磁场场景中的量子极限射频测量应用奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约瑟夫森参量放大器等低温参量放大器对磁场高度敏感（临界电流受夫琅禾费效应抑制），而暗物质轴子搜索实验需要在强磁场中将轴子转化为可探测微波光子，因此亟需一种能在强磁场环境中可靠工作的量子极限放大器。

Method: 对动力学电感行波参量放大器进行多次多特斯拉磁场暴露实验，系统测量其增益与带宽表现，并分别考察增益作为磁场强度、器件在磁场中的取向、施加偏置电流以及泵浦功率与频率的函数变化规律。

Result: KTWPA在多次暴露于多特斯拉磁场后仍能提供超过20dB的增益和数GHz带宽；器件垂直于0.02T磁场时增益消失，但当器件平面接近平行于磁场且场强超过1T时增益仍可超过10dB，且在0.25T至0.5T场强下获得峰值增益。

Conclusion: KTWPA有望扩展量子极限射频测量在特斯拉级磁场环境中的可用性，为暗物质轴子搜索等强磁场应用提供理想的放大器方案。

Abstract: Cryogenic parametric amplifiers are used to amplify radio-frequency signals for a range of applications in basic and applied science. Both Josephson Parametric Amplifiers and Josephson Traveling-Wave Parametric Amplifiers have been used as first-stage amplifiers enabling readout chains operating within a few quanta of the quantum limit. However, these devices are highly sensitive to magnetic fields, having critical current suppressed by the Fraunhofer effect, requiring substantial field-free zones. In a dark matter axion search experiment, axions convert to detectable microwave photons in the presence of a strong magnetic field, necessitating amplifiers that can reliably operate close to these environments. Kinetic-inductance Traveling-Wave Parametric Amplifiers (KTWPAs) may be the ideal candidate for this type of application having high critical magnetic field of the materials used throughout their construction. In this letter we demonstrate that KTWPAs can provide high gain (>20dB) over a multi-GHz bandwidth in spite of from multiple exposures to multi-Tesla fields. Further, we explore operational characteristics of these devices under harsh conditions as a function of overall field strength, device orientation within the field, applied bias current, and pump power & frequency. In so doing, we find KTWPA gain vanishes in devices oriented perpendicularly to a field of 0.02T, but gain values >10dB are achievable in fields over 1T when oriented near~parallel to the device plane, with peak gain achieved with an applied 0.25T to 0.5T field. It is our expectation that KTWPAs will expand the accessibility of quantum-limited RF measurements in the presence of Tesla-scale fields.

</details>


### [111] [Measurement of $tZq$ inclusive and differential cross-sections in $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s} = 13~\text{TeV}$ with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25476)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文基于ATLAS实验在√s=13 TeV、积分亮度140 fb⁻¹的质子-质子碰撞数据，在三轻子末态中测量了tZq（t通道顶夸克伴随Z玻色子产生）过程。测得的包含截面为95.2⁻⁷.⁹⁺₈.⁵（stat.）⁻₅.₃⁺₅.₅（syst.）fb，与标准模型预言一致；同时给出了顶夸克自旋不对称度A=0.34±0.14（stat.）±0.06（syst.），并在标准模型有效场论框架下对涉及顶夸克的维度-6算符设置了限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对tZq过程进行精确测量可检验标准模型在高能标度下的预言，同时该过程对顶夸克与Z玻色子耦合的新物理效应敏感，可为标准模型有效场论（Standard Model Effective Field Theory, SMEFT）提供约束。

Method: 利用ATLAS实验2015至2018年收集的√s=13 TeV质子-质子碰撞数据（积分亮度140 fb⁻¹），在三轻子（e或μ）通道中选取含三个轻子及2至4个喷注（其中1至2个被识别为b夸克起始喷注）的事件，进行包含截面与微分截面的测量，并通过非折叠（unfolding）方法提取顶夸克极化角以测量自旋不对称度。

Result: 包含截面测为95.2⁻⁷.⁹⁺₈.⁵（stat.）⁻₅.₃⁺₅.₅（syst.）fb，与最先进的标准模型预言一致；报告了粒子级（particle level）和部分子级（parton level）相位空间中的绝对与归一化微分截面；顶夸克自旋不对称度A=0.34±0.14（stat.）±0.06（syst.），与标准模型预言吻合。

Conclusion: 测量结果与标准模型预言一致，并在SMEFT框架下对涉及顶夸克的维度-6算符给出限制，为超越标准模型的新物理搜索提供了实验依据。

Abstract: The $t$-channel production of a top quark in association with a $Z$ boson, $tZq$, is measured in the trilepton channel. The measured $pp \rightarrow t\ell^+\ell^-q$ process is defined for $m_{\ell\ell} > 30\,\text{GeV}$ and includes both resonant $Z$-boson production and non-resonant dilepton production. The data collected by the ATLAS experiment in 2015 to 2018 in proton-proton collisions at a centre-of-mass energy of $\sqrt{s} = 13\,\text{TeV}$ are used, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $140\,\text{fb}^{-1}$. Events containing three light leptons ($e$ or $μ$) and two to four jets, one or two of which are identified as $b$-quark-initiated jets, are selected. The inclusive $pp \rightarrow t \ell\ell q$ cross-section with $m_{\ell\ell} > 30\,\text{GeV}$ is measured, along with the separate cross-sections for top-quark and top-antiquark production and their ratio. The inclusive cross-section is measured to be $95.2\;^{+8.5}_{-7.9}\;\text{(stat.)}\;^{+5.5}_{-5.3}\;\text{(syst.)}\,\text{fb}$ and found to be in agreement with the most advanced Standard Model predictions. Differential cross-section measurements as a function of many kinematic variables are also presented. Both absolute and normalised differential cross-section measurements are made at particle level and parton level in their respective phase spaces. The top-quark spin asymmetry is measured from the unfolded measurement of the top-quark polarisation angle as $A = 0.34 \pm 0.14 \;\text{(stat.)} \pm 0.06\;\text{(syst.)}$, in good agreement with the Standard Model prediction. The differential cross-section measurement results are interpreted in the framework of Standard Model effective field theory and limits are set on dimension-6 operators involving the top quark.

</details>


### [112] [A High Performance Partial Wave Analysis Framework for Hadron Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25963)
*Benhou Xiang,Shuangshi Fang,Beijiang Liu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出CTPWA，一个基于协变张量形式（covariant tensor formalism）的高性能分波分析框架，通过预计算和缓存机制及全GPU似然计算与最小化，实现了相较于自微分基GPU程序的百倍加速，使高统计量分波分析在高精度实验中成为可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分波分析（partial wave analysis, PWA）是强子物理中提取强子共振态性质的关键方法，但随着实验统计量增长，传统实现在内存占用和计算速度上面临严重困难，亟需高性能解决方案。

Method: CTPWA框架基于协变张量形式开发，采用大规模预计算与缓存机制，并实现完全基于GPU的似然计算与最小化流程。

Result: 相比基于自动微分（autograd）的GPU分波分析程序，拟合速度提升了两个数量级，使得高统计量分波分析在BESIII等高精度实验中切实可行。

Conclusion: CTPWA通过架构优化显著解决了传统PWA在内存和速度上的瓶颈，为新一代高统计量强子物理实验的数据分析提供了高效的实用工具。

Abstract: Partial wave analysis (PWA) is a key method in hadron physics for extracting the properties of hadronic resonances. As experimental statistics grow, conventional implementations face severe difficulties in both memory usage and speed. We present \texttt{CTPWA}, a high performance PWA framework based on covariant tensor formalism. This framework adopts extensive precomputation and caching mechanisms, as well as fully GPU-based likelihood computation and minimization. With these optimization, the fitting speed is accelerated by two orders of magnitude relative to autograd-based GPU PWA programs, rendering high-statistics partial-wave analysis feasible at high-precision experiments like BESIII.

</details>


### [113] [A Weighting Method for Incorporating Mass Resolution Effects in Amplitude Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25969)
*Benhou Xiang,Wenqian Zheng,Hongxun Yang,Xiaolin Kang,Shuangshi Fang*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出一种相空间加权（phase-space weighting）方法，利用全模拟蒙特卡洛样本中真值级与重建级运动学的对应关系，构建逐事件权重，从而在似然拟合中高效、灵活地纳入探测器质量分辨率效应，无需显式多维卷积或实时探测器模拟。应用于 $J/\psi\to\Xi^{-}\bar{\Xi}^{+}\pi^{0}$ 和 $J/\psi\to K^{-}\Lambda\bar{\Xi}^{+}$ 衰变，可显著降低拟合共振参数和干涉模式的偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高精度强子实验中，探测器质量分辨率效应（detector mass-resolution effect）会模糊观测到的相空间分布，传统处理方式需要对理论振幅进行显式多维卷积或实时调用探测器模拟，计算成本高昂且灵活性不足，因此需要一种更高效、通用且能自然处理达利兹平面（Dalitz plane）上非均匀分辨率的方法。

Method: 方法利用同时包含真值级（truth-level）与重建级（reconstructed）运动学的全模拟蒙特卡洛样本，在每个观测事件附近估计局部探测器响应；基于真值-重建对应关系构建逐事件权重，将理论振幅从真实相空间映射到经模糊处理（smeared）的观测空间。该过程避免了显式多维卷积和实时探测器模拟，且可扩展到不变质量之外的动量与角度变量。

Result: 将方法应用于 $J/\psi\to\Xi^{-}\bar{\Xi}^{+}\pi^{0}$ 和 $J/\psi\to K^{-}\Lambda\bar{\Xi}^{+}$ 两种衰变，结果显示该方法显著减小了拟合共振参数和干涉模式（interference pattern）的偏差，抑制了人工假结构，并提升了部分波分析（partial-wave analysis）与共振谱学（resonance spectroscopy）的可靠性。

Conclusion: 该相空间加权方法为在高精度强子实验中纳入探测器质量分辨率效应提供了一种高效灵活的途径，能够自然处理达利兹平面上非均匀的探测器分辨率，并具有扩展到更多运动学变量的潜力，有助于提升共振分析的整体可靠性。

Abstract: We present a phase-space weighting method for incorporating detector mass-resolution effects into amplitude analysis in an efficient and flexible way. The method uses fully simulated Monte Carlo samples containing both truth-level and reconstructed kinematics to estimate the local detector response around each observed event. Based on these truth-reconstruction correspondences, event-by-event weights are constructed to map theoretical amplitudes from the true phase space to the smeared observational space. In this way, detector smearing can be included in the likelihood fit without performing an explicit multidimensional convolution or on-the-fly detector simulation. The procedure naturally handles nonuniform resolution across the Dalitz plane and can be extended beyond invariant masses to momentum and angular variables. Applied to the decays $J/ψ\rightarrow Ξ^{-} \barΞ^{+}π^0$ and $J/ψ\rightarrow K^-Λ\barΞ^+$, the method significantly reduces biases in fitted resonance parameters and interference patterns, suppresses artificial structures, and improves the reliability of partial-wave analysis and resonance spectroscopy in high-precision hadron experiments.

</details>

### [114] [Finding and using interpretable latents in a neutrino foundation model with sparse autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26090)
*Raphaël Bonnet-Guerrini,Johann Ioannou-Nikolaides,Inar Timiryasov,Vincenzo Piuri*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文首次将基于稀疏自编码器（sparse autoencoder）的机制性可解释性（mechanistic interpretability）方法应用于粒子物理领域。研究者针对于一个基于IceCube数据预训练并针对方向重建进行微调的中微子基础模型，识别出模型表示中的物理概念图集，并发现方向头几乎没有利用该图集。基于这一发现，研究者训练了一个不确定性头来预测模型的角度重建误差，该头因果性地依赖于图集中的质量和亮度特征，在20%选择效率下将中位角分辨率从20.2°提升至3.2°。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粒子物理中的深度学习模型常被视为黑箱，缺乏对其内部表示中如何编码物理概念的深入理解。此前机制性可解释性主要应用于语言和视觉领域，尚未在粒子物理中被系统采用。

Method: 采用稀疏自编码器对中微子基础模型的隐藏表示进行分解，识别物理概念图集。通过严格验证协议（包括留出测试、匹配的混杂控制以及跨独立字典训练的重现）确认概念有效性，并使用因果干预验证方向头和图集之间的依赖关系。

Result: 识别出一套经验证的物理概念图集，但因果干预表明方向头几乎不使用该图集。基于相同的事件级表示训练的不确定性头则因果性地依赖图集中的质量与亮度特征，并在20%选择效率下将中位角分辨率从20.2°提高到3.2°。

Conclusion: 机制性可解释性能够揭示模型内部表示中编码的潜在物理特征，并有助于设计利用这些特征的下游任务，从而在粒子物理中实现可解释且性能更优的模型。

Abstract: We present a first application of sparse-autoencoder-based mechanistic interpretability to particle physics. Studying a neutrino foundation model pretrained on IceCube data and fine-tuned for direction reconstruction, we identify a validated atlas of physical concepts in the model representation, using a strict validation protocol consisting of held-out tests, matched nuisance controls, and replication across independent dictionary trainings. Causal interventions show that the direction head barely draws on this atlas. Motivated by this underused information, we train an uncertainty head on the same event-level representation to predict the model's angular reconstruction error. Unlike the direction head, it depends causally on quality and brightness features from the atlas. At $20\%$ selection efficiency, this interpretable estimator improves the median angular resolution from $20.2^\circ$ to $3.2^\circ$. These results suggest that mechanistic interpretability can reveal learned latent physics encoded within a model's internal representation and help design downstream tasks that exploit it.

</details>


### [115] [The properties of the first continuous gravitational waves: Optimizing pulsar timing observations for supermassive black hole binary detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25715)
*Blaze Houlden,Eric Thrane,Katie Auchettl*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究通过模拟受引力波背景（gravitational-wave background）约束的超大质量黑洞双星（supermassive black hole binaries）种群，预测了首个可单独分辨的双星系统的最可能性质，并评估了不同观测策略对探测和电磁识别概率的影响。研究发现，在现有MeerKAT观测策略下探测单个双星的几率为10-27%，提高观测精度、观测频率和二者结合分别可将概率提升至11-28%、12-30%和12-33%，且高频率观测更有利于电磁对应体（electromagnetic counterpart）的识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多个脉冲星计时阵列（pulsar timing arrays）已报告存在纳赫兹引力波背景（nanohertz gravitational-wave background）的证据，该背景被认为来自大量旋进中的超大质量黑洞双星信号的叠加。验证这一解释的关键途径是寻找信号高出背景噪声、可被单独分辨的双星系统，因此需要预测首个可分辨双星的属性并优化观测策略以利于电磁识别。

Method: 该方法通过模拟受引力波背景证据约束的超大质量黑洞双星种群，提取每次模拟中最亮的双星（loudest binary），据此推断首个可单独分辨系统（individually resolvable binary）的预期属性。同时系统比较了不同观测参数组合，包括保持当前策略9年、将计时精度（timing precision）提高两倍、将观测频率（cadence）提高四倍，以及两者同时提升对探测概率的影响。

Result: 在MeerKAT采用当前观测策略总共9年的条件下，探测到单个双星的概率约为10-27%；将计时精度提高两倍可使概率变为11-28%；将观测频率提高四倍可使概率变为12-30%；两者同时改进则给出12-33%的探测概率。此外，高频率（high-cadence）观测方案优先分辨较高频率（约≥10 nHz）的系统，这类系统的较短轨道周期使其比低频重复不频繁的双星更容易通过电磁观测识别。

Conclusion: 研究为脉冲星计时阵列对未来可分辨超大质量黑洞双星的探测和电磁识别提供了定量预测和策略优化建议。高频率观测虽对探测概率提升有限，但对电磁后续观测（electromagnetic follow-up）具有重要优势，目前全天光学巡天（all-sky optical surveys）可为此类引力波可分辨双星的电磁识别提供后续观测机会。

Abstract: Several pulsar timing arrays have reported evidence for a nanohertz gravitational-wave background. This background is thought to arise from the superposition of signals from a population of inspiraling supermassive black hole binaries. A key test of this interpretation is the search for individually resolvable binaries whose signals emerge above this background. Here we investigate this possibility by addressing two related questions: (1) what are the most probable properties of the first resolvable binary; and (2) how can pulsar timing observations be optimized to maximize its prospects for electromagnetic identification? By simulating populations of supermassive black hole binaries constrained by evidence for the gravitational-wave background and studying the loudest binary in each simulation, we infer the expected properties of the first individually resolvable systems. If MeerKAT continues its current observing strategy for a total of 9 years, the probability of detecting an individual binary is $\approx10-27\%$. Improving the timing precision by a factor of two changes this probability to $\approx11-28\%$, whereas increasing the cadence by a factor of four gives $\approx12-30\%$. Combining both improvements yields a detection probability of $\approx12-33\%$. Furthermore, implementing a high-cadence observing campaign preferentially resolves higher-frequency systems ($\gtrsim\unit[10]{nHz}$), whose shorter orbital periods make them considerably easier to identify through electromagnetic observations than lower-frequency binaries that repeat infrequently. We discuss prospects of electromagnetic follow-up of gravitational-wave resolved binaries with current high-cadence all-sky optical surveys.

</details>

### [116] [$(k,n)$-core percolation on hypergraphs with anchor nodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25560)
*Hoseung Jang,Byungjoon Min,Ginestra Bianconi*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了超图上的广义(k,n)-核逾渗（(k,n)-core percolation）理论框架，通过引入节点角色异质性（锚点概率θ），构建自洽方程描述超图去边修剪过程中的相变行为，并用数值模拟在随机超图上验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超图（hypergraph）描述涉及多个节点的更高阶相互作用，其鲁棒性强烈依赖于节点在超边中的不同功能角色——关键节点（essential node）的丢失会完全摧毁所属超边，而非关键节点（non-essential node）的丢失仅导致超边缩小。现有模型尚未系统刻画这种节点角色异质性对超图鲁棒性的影响，因此需要建立统一的理论框架。

Method: 论文将超图节点标记为锚点（anchor）的概率为θ，超边在锚点失效时即告失败；据此将(k,n)-核逾渗问题分为第一邻居（first-neighbor）和第二邻居（second-neighbor）两类修剪过程，分别推导节点级和超边级修剪的自洽方程（self-consistency equations），并求解巨型(k,n)-核（giant (k,n)-core）的规模。

Result: 理论预测与随机超图上的数值模拟一致，得到了包含连续与非连续跃迁的完整相图（phase diagram），揭示了节点功能角色的异质性（heterogeneity）以及交互作用扩展范围对更高阶网络鲁棒性的显著影响。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，节点功能角色的异质性和交互范围的延伸是决定更高阶网络（higher-order networks）鲁棒性的关键因素，所提出的框架为理解超图在部分节点失效情境下的结构退化行为提供了统一的理论工具。

Abstract: Hypergraphs describe higher-order interactions that involve more than a pair of nodes. A characteristic feature of hypergraphs is that their robustness can be strongly affected by the different roles of the nodes. Indeed, some nodes might be essential for a hyperedge's function, while others might not be. The loss of a single essential node completely destroys the hyperedge it belongs to, while the loss of a non-essential node has a buffering effect, inducing the hyperedge to simply reduce its size. In order to capture this phenomenology, we formulate a comprehensive theoretical framework for $(k,n)$-core percolation models on hypergraphs, where each node of a hyperedge is an anchor with probability $θ$, and a hyperedge fails if an anchor node fails. Hypergraph $(k,n)$-core percolation problems can be classified as first-neighbor and second-neighbor problems, indicating that in the pruning process the connectivity is ensured only by the state of the first neighbors or the second neighbors, respectively. We derive self-consistency equations for first-neighbor and second-neighbor (node- and hyperedge-based) pruning processes, and obtain the size of the giant $(k,n)$-core. We obtain the phase diagram, including continuous and discontinuous transitions, and confirm our theory on random hypergraphs using numerical simulations. The results show how the heterogeneity of the nodes' functional roles and the extended range of the interactions affect the robustness of higher-order networks.

</details>

### [117] [Experimental Characterization of Additively Manufactured Metallic Alloys for Electric Propulsion Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26048)
*J. Chamberlain,A. Shashurin*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 本研究探究增材制造（AM）材料在栅极离子源应用中的溅射行为，以316L不锈钢为例证明其不会产生比传统制造材料更不利的溅射特性，并进一步论证了使用增材制造钨铼栅极的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 栅极离子源（gridded ion source）应用中，材料溅射会限制部件寿命并影响性能，因此需要评估增材制造（additive manufacturing, AM）材料是否具有更差的溅射行为（如更高的溅射产额）以及是否可用于实际离子源栅极。

Method: 研究分为两部分：第一部分将三块增材制造316L不锈钢样品分别暴露于KDC-40静电栅极离子源束流中，在400、600和800 eV三档能量下各轰击一小时，样品加掩膜以形成处理/未处理的清晰边界并偏置至-18 V；随后使用Bruker光学轮廓仪（optical profilometer）测量并结合开源软件Gwyddion处理数据以评估溅射产额（sputter yield）。第二部分验证增材制造钨铼（tungsten-rhenium）栅极在现有KDC-40离子源中的可行性。

Result: 在400-800 eV离子能量范围内，溅射产额在0.2-2原子/离子之间变化并随离子能量增加而升高；测量值与文献中分析模型预测基本一致，但存在差异，可能源于温度升高及氧化层等附加效应。

Conclusion: 增材制造的316L不锈钢与传统制造材料相比不表现更差的溅射行为，且增材制造钨铼栅极在现有离子源中被证明可行，表明增材制造材料可适用于栅极离子源应用。

Abstract: This work explores the sputtering of additively manufactured (AM) materials for use in gridded ion source applications. The first part of the paper uses 316L stainless steel as an example to demonstrate that additively manufactured material does not exhibit adverse sputtering behavior, such as higher sputter yield, compared with conventionally manufactured material. To this end, three additively manufactured 316L stainless steel samples were exposed to the beam of a KDC-40 electrostatic gridded ion source at three distinct energy levels of 400, 600, and 800 eV on each side of the sample for a duration of one hour. The samples were masked to create a distinct boundary between treated and untreated regions, identifiable using profilometry, and were biased to -18V for testing. Samples were then examined using a Bruker optical profilometer and further processed using the open-source software Gwyddion to evaluate the sputtering yield. The sputter yield varied in the range 0.2-2 atoms/ion for 400-800 eV ions and increased with ion energy. The measured sputtering yield was fairly consistent with predictions from analytical models developed in prior literature, while exhibiting some variations potentially due to added effects of increased temperature and oxide layers. The second part of the paper demonstrates feasibility of using an additively manufactured tungsten-rhenium grids in existing KDC-40 electrostatic gridded ion source.

</details>

### [118] [Uniform Resolvent Estimates for the Discrete Schrödinger Operator in Higher Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25458)
*Yuda Chen*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文在整数格$d$维空间（$d\geq4$）上，针对标准离散拉普拉斯算子的预解式建立了从$\ell^p$到$\ell^{p'}$的一致对角线预解估计（uniform diagonal resolvent estimates）。这些估计通过证明费米面（Fermi surface）上表面测度的局部傅里叶衰减界（local Fourier-decay bounds）获得，并利用各向异性Knapp型构造（Knapp-type constructions）给出了对应的必要条件。文中还导出了Birman–Schwinger界、Kato平滑估计（Kato smoothing）以及改进的薄谱投影估计（thin spectral projection estimates）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预解估计是调和分析与谱理论中的核心工具，而离散拉普拉斯算子的预解估计对研究格点系统的谱性质与色散方程具有重要价值。作者希望建立对角线$\ell^p\to\ell^{p'}$预解估计的锐利（sharp）全域范围，并厘清不同维度下估计的最优性。

Method: 通过对格点色散关系的费米面表面测度建立局部傅里叶衰减界来证明预解估计。在正规点处，依据二次项消失的坐标方向数确定衰减指数：经局部变量替换后，这些方向产生立方型一维相位，其余方向保持二次相位。各向异性Knapp型构造被用于导出对应的必要条件（necessary conditions）。

Result: 对于$d\geq5$，证明了锐利全域$p$范围$1\leq p\leq\frac{2(d+2)}{d+5}$；在远离阈值能量处，奇数维仍保持该范围锐利，而偶数$d\geq6$时可改进为$1\leq p\leq\frac{2(2d+5)}{2d+11}$。在四维情形，获得了$1\leq p<4/3$的一致估计以及带平方根对数损失的$\ell^{4/3}\to\ell^4$端点估计。此外还导出了Birman–Schwinger界、Kato平滑估计、复特征值估计及改进的薄谱投影估计。

Conclusion: 作者系统地刻画了高维（$d\geq4$）离散拉普拉斯算子对角线预解估计的锐利$p$范围，揭示了奇数维与偶数维在阈值能量处及远离阈值时的不同行为，并通过Knapp反例证实了范围的必要条件，为后续相关研究提供了完整的估计框架。

Abstract: Let $H_0$ be the standard discrete Laplacian on $\mathbb Z^d$, $d\geq4$, let $R_0(z)=(H_0-z)^{-1}$, and let $p'$ denote the Hölder conjugate of $p$, with $p'=\infty$ when $p=1$. We establish uniform diagonal resolvent estimates from $\ell^p(\mathbb Z^d)$ to $\ell^{p'}(\mathbb Z^d)$ by proving local Fourier-decay bounds for surface measure on the Fermi surfaces of the lattice dispersion relation. At a regular point, the decay is governed by the number of coordinate directions in which the quadratic term vanishes: after a local change of variables, these directions produce cubic one-dimensional phases, while the remaining directions are quadratic. For $d\geq5$, we prove the sharp global range \[ 1\leq p\leq\frac{2(d+2)}{d+5}. \] Away from the threshold energies, this range remains sharp in odd dimensions, whereas for even $d\geq6$ the sharp range improves to \[ 1\leq p\leq\frac{2(2d+5)}{2d+11}. \] In dimension four, we obtain uniform estimates for $1\leq p<4/3$ and an $\ell^{4/3}$-to-$\ell^4$ endpoint estimate with a square-root logarithmic loss. Matching anisotropic Knapp-type constructions give the corresponding necessary conditions. We also derive Birman--Schwinger and Kato smoothing bounds, complex-eigenvalue estimates, and improved thin spectral projection estimates.

</details>
