### [154] [A Simple Regularization of the Smooth Quantum Hydrodynamic Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24950)
*Carl L. Gardner*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一种对光滑量子流体动力学模型（quantum hydrodynamic model）方程的简单正则化方案，以抑制不稳定增长模式，并将其应用于谐振隧穿二极管（resonant tunneling diode）的时域模拟，获得与实际量子力学模拟一致的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光滑量子流体动力学模型方程在数值求解中存在不稳定增长模式，影响模拟的可靠性。需要一种简单有效的正则化方法来防止该不稳定性的产生。

Method: 正则化方案的核心在于：将动量守恒方程右端中电子密度的空间导数，替换为由经典玻尔兹曼分布（classical Boltzmann distribution）导出的表达式。随后对谐振隧穿二极管进行到稳态的时域依赖（time-dependent）模拟。

Result: 模拟结果展示了真实的负微分电阻（negative differential resistance，量子共振的实验信号）以及电流-电压曲线中的滞回现象（hysteresis）。模拟结果与谐振隧穿二极管的完全量子力学模拟（fully quantum mechanical simulation）结果高度吻合。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于经典玻尔兹曼分布的正则化方法能够有效抑制不稳定增长模式，并在量子输运器件的数值模拟中提供与全量子力学方法一致的结果，验证了该方法的实用性。

Abstract: A simple regularization of the smooth quantum hydrodynamic model equations to prevent an unstable growing mode is proposed. The regularization involves replacing the spatial derivative of the electron density on the right-hand side of the momentum conservation equation by using the classical Boltzmann distribution for electron density.
  Time-dependent simulations of the resonant tunneling diode to steady state using this regularization are presented, which show realistic negative differential resistance (the experimental signal of quantum resonance) and hysteresis in the current-voltage curve. The simulations are in good agreement with fully quantum mechanical simulations of the resonant tunneling diode.

</details>


### [155] [Photonic spin-Hall effect as a probe for time-reversal-symmetry broken band topological phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25002)
*Deblina Samanta,Darshan G. Joshi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出光子自旋霍尔效应（photonic spin-Hall effect，PSHE）可作为探测时间反演对称性破缺带拓扑相（time-reversal symmetry broken band topological phase）的非侵入性、无歧义的探针。作者证明质心位移（centroid shift）直接反映光学霍尔电导率（optical Hall conductivity），且其随光频变化的符号结构可区分拓扑相与平庸相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测材料的拓扑性质对于凝聚态物理和拓扑电子学具有重要意义，但现有方法往往具有侵入性或难以无歧义地判定系统处于拓扑相还是平庸相。作者旨在利用光子自旋霍尔效应作为一种非侵入性光学探针来识别时间反演对称性破缺的带拓扑相。

Method: 作者从理论上研究平面偏振高斯光束入射表面时，反射光分裂为空间分离的左旋和右旋圆偏振光束这一光子自旋霍尔效应。他们通过计算质心位移——即左旋和右旋圆偏振光束位移的强度加权平均——来分析其与系统光学霍尔电导率之间的关系，并考察质心位移随光频的变化规律及其符号结构。

Result: 质心位移直接探测光学霍尔电导率，后者携带系统拓扑性质的关键信息。质心位移作为光频的函数，其符号结构在时间反演对称性破缺的带拓扑相与平庸相之间具有明确的差异。

Conclusion: 光子自旋霍尔效应可作为探测时间反演对称性破缺带拓扑相的无歧义且非侵入性的探针，为借助光学手段鉴定材料的拓扑性质提供了一条新途径。

Abstract: When a plane polarized Gaussian beam of light is incident on a surface, upon reflection it splits into right and left circularly polarized beams that are spatially separated in the direction perpendicular to the plane of incidence. This is known as the photonic spin-Hall effect (PSHE). In this work, we show that the centroid shift, which is the intensity weighted average of the shifts of the right and the left circularly polarized beams directly probes the optical Hall conductivity, which carries the essential information about the topological properties of the system. We show that the centroid shift as a function of the frequency of light has a unique sign structure depending on whether the system is in a time-reversal symmetry broken band topological phase or a trivial phase. Thus, the PSHE may serve as an unambiguous and a non-invasive probe to detect time-reversal symmetry broken band topological phases.

</details>


### [156] [Carrier-Resolved Attosecond Valley Polarimetry of Monolayer MoS$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25121)
*Navdeep Rana,Lun Yue,Mette B. Gaarde*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过半导体布洛赫方程仿真单层MoS₂，证明阿秒瞬态吸收光谱可将Mo 4p半芯能级转化为定量谷极化测量仪，实现对飞秒级谷极化的逐载流子（carrier-by-carrier）读写检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然近年已在飞秒时间尺度上实现二维半导体中谷极化的全光写入和切换，但在相同时间尺度上逐载流子读取该极化却始终未能实现。

Method: 对单层MoS₂进行半导体布洛赫方程（semiconductor Bloch equation）仿真，利用阿秒瞬态吸收（attosecond transient absorption）使Mo 4p半芯能级边缘成为定量谷极化测量仪，通过泵浦激发的芯-价带吸收探测空穴，芯-导带漂白探测电子。

Result: 泵浦使芯-价带吸收在平衡态下受泡利阻塞（Pauli blocking）的空穴通道无背景地浮现；其圆二色性（circular dichroism）随泵浦螺旋度反转符号，归一化幅度与谷极化成正比；扫描探测延迟可实时计时几飞秒的写入过程。

Conclusion: 阿秒芯能级二色性是一种对载流子敏感、定量的探测手段，可用于实时观测谷极化的产生与演化过程。

Abstract: In recent years, all-optical writing and switching of valley polarization in two-dimensional semiconductors has been demonstrated on femtosecond timescales. Reading this polarization out on the same timescale, carrier by carrier, has so far remained out of reach. Employing semiconductor Bloch equation simulations of monolayer MoS$_2$, we show that attosecond transient absorption closes this gap by turning the Mo $4p$ semicore edge into a quantitative valley polarimeter. We find that pump-enabled core-to-valence absorption probes the holes while core-to-conduction bleaching probes the electrons, so that a single spectrum identifies each carrier by its photoabsorption energy. The hole channel, Pauli-blocked in equilibrium, emerges background-free. Its circular dichroism reverses sign with the pump helicity, and its normalized magnitude is proportional to the valley polarization. Finally, we show that scanning the probe delay clocks the few-femtosecond write in real time. Thus, attosecond core-level dichroism is a carrier-sensitive, quantitative probe of the creation and evolution of valley polarization.

</details>


### [157] [Signatures of a ferro-Josephson effect in twisted graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25257)
*Ruiheng Su,Zhenxiang Gao,Christopher Coleman,Manabendra Kuiri,Dacen Waters,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Matthew Yankowitz,Nemin Wei,Chunli Huang,Allan H. MacDonald,Joshua Folk*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文报道在扭转石墨烯范德华异质结构中观测到与“铁约瑟夫森效应”（ferro-Josephson effect）一致的实验信号：自旋极化电流驱动磁畴壁磁矩进动，产生拓扑性质的电动势，当自旋-谷同位旋简并自发破缺时，纵向电阻在极微小面内磁场附近出现尖锐峰，并随电流减小时消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在验证自旋极化电流穿过磁畴壁时产生的自旋转移力矩（spin-transfer torque）能否驱动磁矩进动，从而通过贝里曲率（Berry curvature）调制产生拓扑性质的电动势，即铁约瑟夫森效应，并探索其在凝聚态材料中的实验可探性。

Method: 在扭转石墨烯范德华异质结构中，通过调控填充实现同位旋（isospin）简并的自发破缺，测量纵向电阻在零面内磁场附近的行为，并结合微分电阻对电流和磁场的依赖关系，探测自旋磁畴壁磁矩的电流驱动进动及其与极小外加面内磁场设定的各向异性（anisotropy）之间的竞争。

Result: 在面内磁场B_∥为零附近千分之一毫特斯拉的范围内观测到纵向电阻的尖锐峰，该峰在电流趋近零时消失；微分电阻中该特征分解为在微特斯拉和皮安培尺度上随B_∥色散的尖锐共振结构。

Conclusion: 这些实验特征源于电流驱动自旋畴壁磁矩的进动，与小外加磁场设定的面内各向异性相互竞争；该工作确立了非线性输运作为极低能量尺度（远低于k_BT）下同位旋畴壁动力学敏感探测手段的有效性。

Abstract: When a spin-polarized current is driven across a magnetic domain wall, the resulting spin-transfer torque could, beyond a critical threshold, set the wall's moments into precession. This precession would modulate the Berry curvature experienced by electrons traversing the wall, producing an electromotive force that is topological in nature, proportional to the precession frequency, mapping precisely onto the DC Josephson effect and leading to the name ferro-Josephson effect. We report signatures consistent with this effect in a twisted graphene van der Waals heterostructure, where spin and valley textures are linked by exchange, Hund's coupling, and spin-orbit interactions. Tuned to fillings where the isospin degeneracy is spontaneously broken, the samples develop a sharp peak in the longitudinal resistance within a fraction of a millitesla of $B_\parallel=0$---a peak that disappears as the current is reduced toward zero. In differential resistance the feature resolves into sharp resonances that disperse with $B_\parallel$ on microtesla and picoampere scales. We argue that these arise from the current-driven precession of spin-domain-wall moments, in competition with the in-plane anisotropy set by a minuscule applied field, and that they establish nonlinear transport as a sensitive probe of isospin domain-wall dynamics at energy scales far below $k_BT$.

</details>


### [158] [Metastable magnetic domains and the anomalous $B_\parallel=0$ resistance peak in twisted double bilayer graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25263)
*Zhenxiang Gao,Christopher Coleman,Silvia Folk,Ruiheng Su,Manabendra Kuiri,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Nemin Wei,Chunli Huang,Joshua Folk*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了扭转型双层石墨烯（twisted double bilayer graphene）中谷极化四分之一金属态在零面内磁场下出现的尖锐亚稳态纵向电阻峰。作者证明该电阻峰依赖于轨道磁性域（orbital magnetism domain）的构型，而该构型由进入有序态时的栅压轨迹和磁场（尤其是面内分量）决定。面内磁场分量的敏感性表明自旋（spin）通过与谷极化耦合的自旋-轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）成为域形成和电阻峰的关键自由度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨烯莫尔超晶格中，谷极化产生轨道磁性，表现为反常霍尔效应，并在域构型变化改变准粒子散射时导致纵向电阻中的巴克豪森跳跃。然而，超出简单极化域图像，域内和域间的自旋与谷织构及其对输运的影响尚不明确。扭转型双层石墨烯的谷极化四分之一金属态中，零面内磁场下常出现尖锐且亚稳态的纵向电阻峰，其微观起源尚未得到确认，这正是本文要解决的问题。

Method: 通过实验观察扭转型双层石墨烯中谷极化四分之一金属态的纵向电阻行为，系统改变栅压轨迹（gate-voltage trajectory）和磁场条件（特别是面内分量）进入有序态，分析电阻峰的出现及其与轨道磁性域构型的关联。

Result: 研究发现，零面内磁场下的尖锐电阻峰依赖于轨道磁性域的构型，而域构型本身由进入有序态时的栅压轨迹和磁场（尤其是面内分量）决定。电阻峰对面内磁场分量的敏感性表明，自旋通过自旋-轨道耦合与谷极化相连，是域形成和电阻峰的关键自由度。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了扭转型双层石墨烯谷极化四分之一金属态中，轨道磁性域的构型同时受栅压历史和磁场（含面内分量）控制，且自旋通过自旋-轨道耦合与谷极化耦合，构成域形成与电阻峰现象的核心物理机制。

Abstract: In graphene moirés, valley polarization gives rise to orbital magnetism, manifested as an anomalous Hall effect and resulting in Barkhausen jumps in longitudinal resistance when changing domain configurations modify quasiparticle scattering. Beyond a simple picture of polarized domains, however, spin and valley textures within and between the domains are less well understood, as is the effect of these textures on transport. In the valley-polarized quarter-metal state of twisted double bilayer graphene, a sharp and metastable peak in longitudinal resistance often appears at zero in-plane magnetic field, whose microscopic origin has yet to be identified. Here, we show that this peak depends on the configuration of domains of orbital magnetism, which is itself set by the gate-voltage trajectory used to enter the ordered state and by the magnetic field --- particularly the in-plane component --- present during that trajectory. The sensitivity of the effect to in-plane magnetic field components points to spin, linked to valley polarization through spin-orbit coupling, as the key degree of freedom in both the domain formation and the resistance peak.

</details>


### [159] [Quantum geometric signatures of Link-Unlink transitions and nonlinear Hall response in Hopf-link semimetals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25352)
*Kamalesh Bera,Arijit Saha,Debashree Chowdhury*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在理论上研究了Hopf-link半金属（Hopf-link semimetal）的量子几何性质，计算了带间光导率以区分链接与平凡相，并揭示了在该系统中由于PT对称性导致Berry曲率贡献被完全抑制后，通过适当的微扰引入，可以产生纯粹由量子度量（quantum metric）驱动的非线性霍尔效应，且该效应不掺杂任何外在成分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子几何（quantum geometry）由量子度量（quantum metric）和Berry曲率（Berry curvature）构成，在固体的电子输运性质中起重要作用。已有研究确立了量子度量偶极子介导的无散射非线性霍尔效应，而在Berry曲率偶极子贡献为零的系统中该效应更为引人注目，这促使作者对Hopf-link半金属这一独特拓扑类别进行量子几何性质的理论研究。

Method: 作者采用理论分析方法，计算了Hopf-link半金属的带间光导率（interband optical conductivity），用以有效区分链接相（linked phase）与平凡相（trivial phase）。基于该体系固有的PT对称性对Berry曲率及其非线性霍尔贡献的完全抑制，作者通过引入适当的微扰来研究由此产生的有限非线性霍尔电导。

Result: 计算结果表明，Hopf-link半金属的带间光导率能够有效区分链接相与平凡相。由于体系的PT对称性，Berry曲率及其对应的非线性霍尔效应贡献被完全抑制；在引入适当微扰后，出现仅由量子度量决定的有限非线性霍尔电导，且该纯内在的、对称性驱动的非线性响应完全不含外在成分（extrinsic components）的混合。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了一种完全由量子度量驱动、不受Berry曲率影响的纯内在非线性霍尔效应机制，并证明这种响应在Hopf-link半金属中可被对称性调控地实现，为探索非Berry曲率起源的量子几何输运现象提供了新的理论途径。

Abstract: Quantum geometry, comprising of quantum metric and Berry curvature, plays a significant role in the electronic transport properties of solids. In this work, we theoretically investigate the quantum geometric properties of a Hopf-link semimetal, a distinct topological class that is charecterized by a nodal link-unlink transition. We compute the interband optical conductivity of the Hopf link, which effectively distinguishes between linked and trivial phases. While recent studies establish quantum metric dipole-mediated scattering-free nonlinear Hall effect, this effect becomes even more fascinating in systems where the Berry-curvature-dipole contribution to nonlinear Hall conductivity vanishes. Owing to the underlying $PT$ symmetry of the Hopf-link semimetal, the Berry curvature and its corresponding contribution to the nonlinear Hall effect are entirely suppressed. Consequently, by introducing an appropriate perturbation, a finite nonlinear Hall conductivity emerges solely due to the quantum metric in the Hopf semimetal. Notably, this purely intrinsic, symmetry-driven nonlinear response remains entirely unmixed with extrinsic components.

</details>


### [160] [Non-Hermitian topological Euler insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25411)
*Longwen Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文将拓扑Euler绝缘体（topological Euler insulator）的概念从厄米（Hermitian）系统推广至非厄米（non-Hermitian）系统，针对二维三带对称非厄米晶格模型提出了描述其拓扑Euler能带、纠缠谱（entanglement spectrum）和体-边对应（bulk-boundary correspondence）的理论框架，并构建了三个典型非厄米Euler绝缘体模型进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑Euler绝缘体已在多带厄米系统中被发现，其特征由退化带的Euler类描述，并由二维中的PT或C2T对称性保护，超越了十重（tenfold）K理论分类。然而，非厄米拓扑物态在过去的研究中蓬勃发展，而Euler拓扑是否能在非厄米开放系统中存在仍属空白，这正是本工作的核心动机。

Method: 研究对象为具有对称哈密顿量的二维三带非厄米晶格模型。作者提出了一个理论框架，涵盖非厄米系统的拓扑Euler能带描述、纠缠谱分析以及体-边对应的完整刻画，并通过构造三个典型的非厄米Euler绝缘体模型进行详细验证。

Result: 作者确认了非厄米系统中存在拓扑Euler能带，并发现了具有非厄米来源的独特拓扑相变以及异常的边带重叠（anomalous edge-band overlaps）现象，这些现象在厄米系统中不存在。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了非厄米系统中拓扑Euler能带的存在性，揭示了其有趣的物理特征，从而将拓扑物态的版图拓展到了非厄米开放系统领域。

Abstract: Topological Euler insulators emerge in multiband systems with real Bloch Hamiltonians and wavefunctions. Their fragile topologies are characterized by the Euler class of degenerate bands and protected by the $PT$ or $C_2T$ symmetry in two dimensions, which go beyond the tenfold $K$-theory classification of topological matter. In this work, we extend the conception of topological Euler insulators to non-Hermitian systems and propose a theoretical framework to unlock their nontrivial Euler topology. Focusing on two-dimensional, three-band non-Hermitian lattice models with symmetric Hamiltonians, we formulate a comprehensive description of their topological Euler bands, entanglement spectrum and bulk-boundary correspondence. Three typical models of non-Hermitian Euler insulators are constructed and investigated explicitly to illustrate our theory. Unique topological phase transitions and anomalous edge-band overlaps with non-Hermitian origins are further identified. Our study establishes the presence of topological Euler bands in non-Hermitian systems and unveils their intriguing physical characteristics, thereby broadening the existing territory of topological matter in non-Hermitian open systems.

</details>


### [161] [Chirality-Selective Phonon Pumping by Ferroelectric Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25684)
*Dapeng Yao,Ping Tang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在普通介电体中产生传播手性声子（chiral phonons）的通用机制：利用相邻铁电体极化进动通过电致伸缩耦合将角动量传递给晶格，从而泵浦手性选择声子，其手性由极化进动方向决定，并以LiNbO₃|Y₃Al₅O₁₂双层结构为例证明了远超手性晶体的泵浦效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性声子的发现扩展了晶格振动作为被动热载体的传统认识，为声子自旋电子学器件提供新机遇，但现有实现方式仅限于手性晶体或某些非手性材料的特定动量空间区域，亟需通用且可电控的产生机制。

Method: 提出利用铁电体（ferroelectric）中电极化的进动过程，通过电致伸缩耦合（electrostrictive coupling）将极化动力学携带的固有角动量转移至相邻介电晶格，从而定向泵浦手性选择声子；手性由极化进动方向唯一决定，并以LiNbO₃|Y₃Al₅O₁₂双层结构为典型实例进行理论验证。

Result: 以界面转换率（interfacial convertance）量化的泵浦效率在LiNbO₃|Y₃Al₅O₁₂双层中显著超过手性晶体中热致手性声子产生的效率，得益于铁电体中的强电致伸缩耦合。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了铁电动力学作为手性声子的通用电学源，为电可编程的手性声子学与自旋电子学功能提供了一条普适路径。

Abstract: The discovery of chiral phonons has expanded the conventional view of lattice vibrations as passive heat carriers, opening new opportunities for phononic spintronic devices. However, their realization has been largely limited to chiral crystals or to specific regions of momentum space in certain achiral materials. Here, we propose a generic mechanism for generating propagating chiral phonons in an ordinary dielectric through the precession of the electric polarization in an adjacent ferroelectric. The polarization dynamics transfers its intrinsic angular momentum to the lattice via electrostrictive coupling, thereby pumping chirality-selective phonons whose handedness is dictated by that of the polarization precession. For a typical LiNbO$_{3}$$|$Y$_{3}$Al$_{5}$O$_{12}$ bilayer, we find that the pumping efficiency quantified by an interfacial convertance substantially exceeds those of thermally induced chiral-phonon generation in chiral crystals, owing to the strong electrostrictive coupling in ferroelectrics. Our work establishes ferroelectric dynamics as a versatile electrical source of chiral phonons and provides a general route toward electrically programmable chiral-phononic and spintronic functionalities.

</details>


### [162] [Light-Hole Spin Qubits in Strained SiGe Lattice-Matched to Ge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25852)
*Edmondo Valvo,Davide Costa,Patrick Del Vecchio,Stefano Bosco,Giordano Scappucci,Maximilian Rimbach-Russ*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出并研究了一种与锗（Ge）晶格匹配的异质结构，其中硅锗合金（SiGe）量子阱和势垒均受张应变，通过组分对比提供限制势垒，而张应变使轻空穴（light-hole, LH）基态稳定。研究表明该平台相比重空穴（heavy-hole, HH）量子比特具有更大的自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling, SOC）和更各向同性的自旋响应，同时提高了退相干品质因子，并提出双层异质结构实现HH与LH基态的电学切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的锗量子阱自旋量子比特平台依赖变质缓冲层或呈现重空穴主导的各向异性自旋响应，限制了其性能。作者希望设计一种无需变质缓冲层、且通过张应变稳定轻空穴基态的替代异质结构，以改善自旋响应的各向同性并提升量子比特性能。

Method: 作者提出一种与Ge晶格匹配的异质结构，其中SiGe量子阱和势垒均为张应变，利用组分差异产生限制势垒，而张应变稳定轻空穴基态。通过对该结构进行理论计算，研究其线性与立方自旋轨道耦合、自旋响应的各向同性程度、退相干特性，并设计双层异质结构以实现HH与LH基态的电学切换。

Result: 结果显示该平台同时具有线性和立方的强自旋轨道耦合，自旋响应相比张应变重空穴量子比特明显更加各向同性；退相干品质因子相比HH对应结构有显著提升；双层异质结构可实现HH与LH基态之间的电学切换。

Conclusion: 该张应变SiGe/Ge异质结构为基于轻空穴的自旋量子比特提供了一种无需变质缓冲层的可行平台，在自旋各向同性、自旋轨道耦合以及退相干性能方面均优于传统的重空穴方案，并具备通过电场调控空穴基态性质的灵活性。

Abstract: Strained germanium ($\varepsilon$-Ge) quantum wells on metamorphic SiGe buffers have enabled advanced hole-based spin qubit devices. Alternatively, unstrained Ge with lattice-matched strained silicon-germanium ($\varepsilon$-SiGe) barriers eliminates the need for metamorphic buffers altogether. The ground state character of both these platforms is predominantly heavy-hole (HH) with a largely anisotropic spin response. We propose and study an alternative heterostructure, lattice-matched to Ge, in which both the SiGe quantum well and barriers are tensile strained, with their composition contrast providing the band offset for confinement and the tensile strain stabilizing a light-hole (LH) ground state. We show large spin-orbit coupling (SOC), both linear and cubic, along with a significantly more isotropic spin response compared to strained HH qubits. We also study the decoherence properties of the proposed device, showing an appreciable gain in the quality factor compared to their HH counterparts. Finally, we propose a bilayer heterostructure that allows for electrical switching between HH and LH ground state character.

</details>


### [163] [Topological phases of a generalised tripartite Haldane model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25875)
*Ahmed Al-kharusi,Alessandro Principi,Niels R. Walet*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一种具有最近邻复数跳跃参数（complex nearest neighbour hopping）的广义三部分Haldane模型（generalised tripartite Haldane model），该系统每个初原胞由三个六边形单元格构成，总磁通量为零，因而具有环流模型（loop-current model）结构。作者计算了Chern数（Chern number）作为最近邻与次近邻跳跃相位函数的拓扑相图（topological phase diagram），发现其拓扑相图远比标准Haldane模型复杂，但部分特征可通过解析方法处理；相的Chern数绝对值可高达7。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Haldane模型（Haldane model）是凝聚态物理中拓扑相研究的重要模型，但其拓扑结构相对简单。作者旨在通过将单格点模型推广到三部分结构并引入复数最近邻跳跃参数，探索更丰富的拓扑相行为，并寻求可在解析层面理解复杂拓扑相图的方法。

Method: 作者构建了一个三部分Haldane模型，其整体磁通为零从而具有环流模型结构。通过固定跳跃参数幅值之比，以最近邻和次近邻跳跃相位为变量，数值计算Chern数并绘制拓扑相图；同时利用布里渊区（Brillouin zone）高对称点上能带（energy bands）交叉的显式表达式，推导相图中的线性相边界。

Result: 拓扑相图极为复杂，与标准Haldane模型形成鲜明对比，但部分结构可由解析方法描述；各拓扑相的Chern数绝对值可达7。高对称点上的能带交叉表现为相图中的线性边界，这些直线能解释拓扑相图中的许多特征。此外，作者还分析了几类远离高对称点的能级交叉（level crossings）的代表性案例及其随模型参数的演化。

Conclusion: 广义三部分Haldane模型提供了比标准Haldane模型更丰富的拓扑相结构，高对称点解析分析为理解复杂拓扑相图提供了有效框架，表明此类推广模型可作为探索高Chern数拓扑相的可行平台。

Abstract: We present a generalised tripartite Haldane model with a complex nearest neighbour hopping parameter. The total magnetic flux through the primitive unit cell, which consists of three hexagonal unit cells is zero, and thus the model has the structure of a loop-current model. We calculate topological phase diagrams of the Chern numbers as a function of the phases of the nearest and next-nearest neighbour hopping parameters for a fixed ratio of the magnitude of the hopping parameters. We show that, unlike the Haldane model, the topological phase diagram of this model is very complex, but some aspects can still be dealt with analytically. Furthermore, the Chern numbers of the topological phases are as large as $7$ in absolute value. The analysis is supported by explicit expressions for energy bands crossing at high symmetry points in the Brillouin zone, which show as linear phase boundaries in the phase diagram. We show that such lines explain many features in the topological phase diagrams. Finally, we analyse a few representative examples of the nature of level crossings away from high symmetry and their evolution with model parameters.

</details>


### [164] [Switchable heavy-hole/light-hole spin qubit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25914)
*Zoltán György,Dmitry Miserev,Jelena Klinovaja,Daniel Loss*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于双层Ge异质结构和SiGeSn势垒的可切换重空穴-轻空穴（HH-LH）自旋量子比特，通过在不同量子阱之间穿梭实现空穴特性切换，既保留轻空穴的强自旋轨道耦合优势，又获得比轻空穴更长的相干时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应变压缩（compressively strained）Ge量子阱虽具有稳健的相干时间，但因波函数的重空穴（heavy-hole, HH）特性而自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit interaction, SOI）较弱；近年提出的GeSn/Ge异质结构中的轻空穴（light-hole, LH）量子比特虽提供强线性动量SOI，但缺乏长相干时间。因此需要一种方案同时兼顾两者的优势。

Method: 提出在具有SiGeSn势垒的双层Ge异质结构中構建可切换的HH-LH自旋量子比特，通过将量子比特从LH量子阱穿梭至HH量子阱来改变其空穴特性，并实现基于跳跃（hopping）的快速单量子比特旋转。采用理论计算分析面内限制（in-plane confinement）引入的HH-LH共振及其对g因子和电荷噪声的影响。

Result: 计算揭示面内限制诱导的HH-LH共振产生g因子峰和一阶电荷噪声甜点（sweet spot）；在该甜点处拉比频率（Rabi frequency）约为100 MHz量级，与LH模式相当，但相干时间超过十个倍的提升，达到约100 μs量级。

Conclusion: 该双层Ge异质结构方案成功将HH与LH器件的优势整合于同一平台：既实现强SOI和快速旋转，又显著提升相干时间，为自旋量子比特设计提供了新途径。

Abstract: Compressively strained Ge quantum wells in planar SiGe/Ge heterostructures are the state-of-the-art platform for hole spin qubits. While they exhibit robust coherence times, they possess weak intrinsic spin-orbit interaction (SOI) due to the heavy-hole (HH) character of the wavefunction. Recently, light-hole (LH) qubits were proposed in GeSn/Ge heterostructures, offering strong, intrinsic, linear-in-momentum SOI. In this work, we propose a switchable HH-LH spin qubit in a bilayer Ge heterostructure with SiGeSn barriers, combining the advantages of HH and LH devices. The character of the qubit can be changed by shuttling from an LH well to an HH well, which also enables fast, hopping-based single-qubit rotations. Additionally, we observe an HH-LH resonance introduced by the in-plane confinement, resulting in $g$-factor peaks and first-order charge noise sweet spots. Our calculations reveal a sweet spot with Rabi frequencies on the order of 100 MHz, comparable to the LH regime, but with a more than tenfold increase in coherence time, on the order of 100 $μ$s.

</details>


### [165] [Size and Impurity Effects on Scattering of Valley Hall Modes in Gate-Defined Bilayer Graphene Superlattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25931)
*Marcus N. Kanestrøm,Antonio L. R. Manesco,D. O. Oriekhov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过紧束缚（tight-binding）模拟研究了伯纳尔双层石墨烯（Bernal bilayer graphene, BLG）中门控定义岛上的谷霍尔（valley Hall）模式，重点考察了这些拓扑保护的界面模式能否用于量子行走（quantum walk）或弱耦合系统模拟，以及带隙内杂质和上下门图案错位对岛间隧穿性质的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者关注的核心问题是：BLG中门控定义的岛周围存在的拓扑保护的谷霍尔模式是否适合作为量子行走的载体或用于模拟弱耦合系统，以及这些模式的隧穿行为如何受带隙内杂质影响。该研究旨在探索此类拓扑态的潜在量子模拟应用价值。

Method: 采用紧束缚模拟方法，对伯纳尔双层石墨烯中由门控定义的岛结构进行数值仿真。研究通过反转岛边界处的能隙符号来产生拓扑保护的谷霍尔模式，并系统考察了带隙内杂质对隧穿的影响、上下门图案错位对岛间隧穿性质的影响，以及双岛几何构型下的物理行为。

Result: 主要结果显示：通过杂质产生的共振隧穿（resonant tunneling）能够增强超晶格岛之间的重叠；门图案错位在较宽的参数范围内不会破坏拓扑保护。此外，双岛几何研究表明可以利用抑制散射来实现同一样品上更密集的岛排列。

Conclusion: 谷霍尔模式在BLG门控定义岛中展现出对门错位等扰动的鲁棒性，并且通过杂质辅助的共振隧穿可以有效增强岛间耦合。这些发现为在单一样品上实现更密集排布的多岛量子模拟平台提供了可行性依据。

Abstract: In the present paper we perform a tight-binding simulation of gate-defined islands in Bernal bilayer graphene (BLG). The inversion of the gap sign on the boundaries of the islands creates topologically-protected valley Hall modes. We focus on the specific questions of whether the valley Hall modes around such islands could serve as a host for quantum walks or simulate weakly coupled systems, and how their tunneling is affected by in-gap impurities. In addition, we discuss the effect of misalignment of top and bottom gate patterns on the tunneling properties between islands. Our main results show that resonant tunneling via an impurity enhances overlap between superlattice islands, while misalignment does not break topological protection over a wide parameter regime. In addition, we study the two-island geometry and show that it is possible to leverage suppressed scattering to place islands more densely on a single sample.

</details>


### [166] [Depth Control of Room-Temperature Quantum Emitters in Gallium Nitride](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26057)
*Alexandros Bampis,Johann Stachurski,Anna Schwab,Jean-François Carlin,Raphaël Butté,Nicolas Grandjean*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了氮化镓（GaN）外延层中可见光与近红外量子发射器的定位问题，发现天然发射器靠近GaN/衬底界面从而限制了与光学模式的耦合；通过插入低温GaN中间层，可在任意深度（精度优于60 nm）可控地形成量子发射器，且光学性质与天然发射器相当，为高效发射器-腔体耦合及GaN基腔增强量子发射铺平了道路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GaN中的辐射点缺陷是室温单光子发射的有力候选者，但实验光子提取效率远低于模拟预测，其归因而未明确。识别这一限制的起源是实现高效发射器-腔耦合的关键。

Method: 实验方法包括：在c面蓝宝石衬底上生长GaN外延层，对可见及近红外量子发射器进行光学表征（线宽、计数率、反聚束g^(2)(0)测量）；通过定位分析确定发射器在结构中的深度位置；随后在衬底与GaN之间插入低温生长的薄GaN中间层，验证发射器可在任意深度形成且位置可控。

Result: 天然发射器被定位在GaN/衬底界面附近，解释了其光学模式耦合受限的现象；插入低温GaN中间层后，可在任意深度（精度<60 nm）形成量子发射器，其线宽约6 nm，饱和计数率>1.5 MHz，Debye-Waller因子0.69-0.98，反聚束显著（g^(2)(0)低至0.06）。外延层在<250 nm内完全合并，兼容p-i-n二极管架构。

Conclusion: 研究表明发射器深度位置是可调的，并可通过低温GaN中间层实现精确控制，使发射器可直接嵌入腔体及p-i-n结构的有源区，标志着向高效发射器-腔耦合和GaN腔增强量子发射迈出了决定性一步。

Abstract: Bright quantum emitters are key components for quantum communication systems. Radiative point defects in gallium nitride (GaN) are promising candidates for room-temperature single-photon emission, operating from the visible to the telecom O-band. Despite their potential, their integration into photonic structures has remained limited in the literature, with experimental photon extraction efficiencies far below simulated predictions. To identify the origin of this limitation, we investigate visible and near-infrared quantum emitters in GaN epilayers grown on $c$-plane sapphire substrates exhibiting narrow linewidths ($\sim$4 nm), high photon count rates ($ > $2 MHz), and strong antibunching, reaching $g^{(2)}(0)$ values as low as 0.06 at room temperature. We find that these emitters are located near the GaN/substrate interface, explaining their limited coupling to optical modes. Building on this observation, we show that the insertion of a thin low-temperature GaN interlayer enables the formation of quantum emitters at arbitrary depths with sub-60 nm accuracy, independent of the substrate. The intentionally introduced emitters retain optical properties comparable to naturally occurring ones, including narrow linewidths ($\sim$6 nm), saturation count rates exceeding 1.5 MHz, high Debye-Waller factors (0.69-0.98), and strong antibunching. The resulting epilayer fully coalesces within less than 250 nm ensuring compatibility with GaN-based cavity fabrication and enabling emitter placement within intrinsic regions of p-i-n diode architectures. These results mark a decisive step toward efficient emitter-cavity coupling, enabling the realization of cavity-enhanced quantum emission in GaN.

</details>


### [167] [Viscochiral Transport: Chiral Selection of Hydrodynamic Vortices by Berry Curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26102)
*Archisman Panigrahi,Khachatur Nazaryan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文预测了一种新的电子输运“粘手性区”（viscochiral regime），其中空间变化的霍尔粘度（Hall viscosity）会选择涡旋流动模式，该机制对装置几何细节不敏感，并可通过谷极化的双层石墨烯在实验中实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀霍尔粘度不能改变不可压缩的体流（incompressible bulk flow），因此需要探索空间变化（spatial gradient）的霍尔粘度能否产生可观测的输运效应，以拓展电子流体力学中粘度作用的认知边界。

Method: 通过理论分析霍尔粘度的空间梯度如何重新分配涡量（vorticity），在流入不同腔室（chamber）时分别增强或抑制涡旋；进一步论证该机制对循环流动具有普适性，并通过绘制相图（phase diagram）预测谷极化双层石墨烯中的实验可行性。

Result: 发现空间变化的霍尔粘度可放大一个腔室中的涡旋并抑制另一腔室中的涡旋，从而在电子输运中形成粘手性区；该机制与装置几何细节无关，且已被映射到相图中，表明在谷极化双层石墨烯（valley-polarized bilayer graphene）中可实验观测。

Conclusion: 粘手性区代表一种先前未被识别的电子输运范式，其机制对循环流动是普适的，为在新型二维材料中实验研究空间变化的霍尔粘度提供了具体路径。

Abstract: We predict a new \textit{viscochiral regime} of electronic transport in which spatially varying Hall viscosity selects vortical flow patterns. Although uniform Hall viscosity cannot alter incompressible bulk flow, its spatial gradient redistributes vorticity, amplifying vortices in one chamber while suppressing the vortex in the other. We further determine the underlying mechanism to be generic to recirculating flows and insensitive to the details of the device geometry. The paper maps the resulting phase diagram and shows that the regime is experimentally accessible in valley-polarized bilayer graphene.

</details>


### [168] [Multicomponent Magnetic Domain Walls in Rhombohedral Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26104)
*Mainak Das,Nemin Wei,Chunli Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过微观研究，揭示了菱方多层石墨烯（rhombohedral multilayer graphene）谷不平衡四分之一金属相中一维畴壁的两类不同结构：一类类似于传统磁畴壁，另一类本质上是多组分的并探索了体态中不存在的状态；并证明该畴壁纹理的辐合相干相位可直接耦合超导结，其进动可产生跨结电压。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁序的空间纹理（如畴壁和斯格明子）是磁学中的基本研究对象，菱方多层石墨烯中磁序涉及自旋和谷自由度，可能产生质上全新的空间纹理，但目前缺乏系统的微观理解。

Method: 采用微观研究方法，对谷不平衡四分之一金属相中的一维畴壁进行计算分析，考察谷间洪德耦合（intervalley Hund's coupling）与自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）之间的竞争，并分析跨畴壁超导结中的相位耦合关系。

Result: 发现两类不同的畴壁：一类类似传统磁畴壁，在两体态间局部旋转；另一类本质多组分，占据两体态以外的新态。两类畴壁的实现由谷间洪德耦合与自旋轨道耦合的竞争控制，并识别了区分二者的实验特征；此外，超导结中超导相位差与纹理的谷间相干相位直接耦合，该内部相位的进动可产生跨结电压。

Conclusion: 菱方石墨烯确实存在超越传统磁体的磁纹理，且其动力学可耦合超导输运，为磁纹理-超导相互作用研究提供了新平台。

Abstract: Spatial textures of magnetic order, such as domain walls and skyrmions, are fundamental objects in magnetism. In rhombohedral multilayer graphene, magnetic order involves spin and valley degrees of freedom, opening the possibility of qualitatively new spatial textures. Here, we explore this possibility through a microscopic study of a one-dimensional domain wall in the valley-imbalanced quarter-metal phase of rhombohedral graphene. We uncover two different classes of domain walls. One resembles a conventional magnetic domain wall, locally rotating between the two bulk states, whereas the other is intrinsically multicomponent and explores states that are not occupied in either bulk domain. Which texture is realized is controlled by the competition between intervalley Hund's coupling and spin-orbit coupling, and we identify experimental signatures to distinguish them. We further show that, in a superconducting junction formed across the wall, the superconducting phase difference couples directly to the intervalley-coherent phase of the texture. Precession of this internal phase can therefore generate a voltage across the junction. Our theory shows that rhombohedral graphene indeed has magnetic textures beyond conventional magnet and that their dynamics can couple to superconducting transport.

</details>


<div id='physics.optics'></div>

# physics.optics [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [Nanophotonic control of spatial information in scintillation detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25294)
*Joshua Chen,Simo Pajovic,Seou Choi,Sachin Vaidya,William Michaels,Louis Martin-Monier,Christina M. Spägele,Steven E. Kooi,Juejun Hu,Rajiv Gupta,Charles Roques-Carmes,Marin Soljačić*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出将超构透镜（metalens）直接集成在块状闪烁体（bulk scintillator）上，利用纳米光子波前控制（nanophotonic wavefront control）将体积内产生的闪烁光中高频空间信息优先传递至探测器，在保留厚闪烁体X射线吸收效率的同时实现高分辨率成像。实验恢复了无机和生物样品的X射线精细细节；在与CT相关的探测器几何中，模型预测相比最先进的像素化闪烁体可将所需X射线剂量降低五倍，分辨率带宽提升25倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代X射线成像系统需要在更低辐射剂量下分辨更精细的结构，但传统闪烁体（scintillator）存在长期矛盾：厚闪烁体虽能有效吸收X射线，却因整个体积内产生的光学光子传播扩散而降低空间分辨信息。现有方案主要依赖像素化、柱状晶体或微结构通道物理限制光传播以保持分辨率。

Method: 采用与块状闪烁体直接集成的超构透镜，通过纳米光子波前控制将体积内产生的闪烁光中高空间频率（high-spatial-frequency）信息优先传递到探测器，从而在保持厚闪烁体X射线吸收能力的同时实现高分辨率检测，无需对闪烁光进行体积限制。

Result: 实验成功恢复了无机和生物标本X射线图像中的精细空间细节。在与计算机断层扫描（computed tomography, CT）相关的探测器几何条件下，经实验验证的模型预测相比最先进的像素化闪烁体，可将所需X射线剂量降低五倍并将分辨率带宽提高25倍。

Conclusion: 研究确立波前工程（wavefront engineering）作为将高效X射线吸收与光学图像形成分离的有效途径，有望实现更高分辨率、更低剂量的CT成像。

Abstract: X-rays enable non-invasive imaging across medicine, security, materials science, and beyond, yet modern systems remain constrained by the need to resolve finer structures at lower radiation dose. Scintillators are the dominant materials for detecting X-rays, but face a longstanding compromise: thick scintillators absorb X-rays effectively, whereas optical photons generated throughout their volume spread before detection, degrading spatial information. Existing scintillator architectures largely try to preserve resolution by physically confining light using pixels, columnar crystals, or microstructured channels. Here, we show that high-resolution detection does not require the volumetric confinement of scintillation light. A metalens integrated directly with a bulk scintillator uses nanophotonic wavefront control to preferentially transfer high-spatial-frequency information from volumetrically generated scintillation light to the detector, while retaining the X-ray absorption of a thick scintillator. We experimentally recover fine spatial detail in X-ray images of inorganic and biological specimens. In a detector geometry relevant to computed tomography (CT), the experimentally validated model predicts a fivefold reduction in required X-ray dose and a 25-fold increase in resolution bandwidth relative to a state-of-the-art pixelated scintillator. These results establish wavefront engineering as a route to separating efficient X-ray absorption from optical image formation, with the potential for substantially higher-resolution, lower-dose CT.

</details>


### [170] [First-Principles Prediction of Nonlinear Optical Response in $\text{TiO}_2$ for High-Power Dielectric Mirror Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25129)
*Koya Shimaoka,Yusuke Kondo,Kazunori Shibata,Mitsuharu Uemoto*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文利用基于含时密度泛函理论（time-dependent density functional theory, TDDFT）的实时第一性原理电子动力学模拟，系统研究了二氧化钛（TiO₂）在强超短激光脉冲下的非线性光学响应，揭示了强度依赖的吸收机制与反射率下降现象，并比较了多种晶相与非晶结构的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二氧化钛是强激光光学实验中广泛使用的高折射率介电材料，但高功率激光条件下其光学性能会发生退化，理解这一非线性光学响应机制对于光学元件的设计与可靠性评估至关重要。

Method: 采用基于含时密度泛函理论（TDDFT）的实时第一性原理电子动力学模拟方法，并构建多尺度麦克斯韦–TDDFT（Maxwell–TDDFT）框架来模拟TiO₂纳米薄膜中的电子–光耦合动力学；同时直接评估透射场与反射场，并对金红石（rutile）、锐钛矿（anatase）、板钛矿（brookite）、TiO₂-II、TiO₂-B及非晶超胞模型等多种晶相进行对比。

Result: 揭示出由多光子激发（multiphoton excitation）和光激发自由载流子引起的强度依赖吸收；在高强度（I ~ 10¹³ W/cm²）条件下，反射率显著降低，表现出明显的非线性光学响应；不同TiO₂晶相与非晶结构的响应性质各不相同。

Conclusion: 这些结果为TiO₂基介电光学元件在强飞秒激光场下发生强度依赖的光学退化提供了微观层面的机制性认识。

Abstract: Dielectric multilayer mirrors are essential components in optical experiments using high-power lasers, where titanium dioxide ($\text{TiO}_2$) is widely employed as a high-refractive-index dielectric material. In this study, we investigate the nonlinear optical response of $\text{TiO}_2$ under intense ultrashort laser pulses using real-time first-principles electron-dynamics simulations based on time-dependent density functional theory (TDDFT). We reveal intensity-dependent absorption driven by multiphoton excitation and optically excited free carriers, and simulate the resulting electron--light coupled dynamics in $\text{TiO}_2$ nanofilms using a multiscale Maxwell--TDDFT framework. Direct evaluation of the reflected and transmitted fields shows reduced reflectance at high intensities ($I \sim 10^{13}~\text{W}/\text{cm}^2$), demonstrating a pronounced nonlinear optical response. Furthermore, the optical response properties are compared among various stable and metastable crystalline phases of $\text{TiO}_2$---rutile, anatase, brookite, $\text{TiO}_2$-II, and $\text{TiO}_2$-B---as well as an amorphous supercell model. These results provide microscopic insight into intensity-dependent optical degradation in $\text{TiO}_2$-based dielectric optical components exposed to intense femtosecond laser fields.

</details>


### [171] [Generalizing Thermal Transport in High-Contrast Metamaterials through Interfacial Fresnel Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25499)
*Seung Hyeon Ham,Yu Min Kim,In Hyeok Choi,Jeong Woo Han*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出了有效介质近似（effective medium approximation, EMA）的推广形式，通过引入两个修正系数，使其适用于相邻层间热导率对比度高的热超构材料（thermal metamaterials）体系，并揭示了热流穿透行为与光学中菲涅尔反射系数（Fresnel reflection coefficient）的数学同构性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式人工智能的快速发展使大规模数据中心对高效散热的需求日益迫切，分层结构热超构材料被广泛用于控制热流方向，但传统有效介质近似在相邻层间热导率对比度高的体系中预测精度不足。

Method: 通过引入两个修正系数对传统有效介质近似进行推广，使模型扩展到含背景介质的高对比度热超构材料体系；其中所提系数之一在数学形式上与光学中的菲涅尔反射系数一致，由此建立热流穿透行为的类反射性解释框架。

Result: 推广后的EMA在高热导率对比度体系中能够给出准确预测，且由于修正系数与菲涅尔反射系数的形式统一，热流穿透相邻高对比度层的类反射行为可借助射线光学（ray optics）框架加以直观理解。

Conclusion: 热扩散（heat diffusion）传统上被视为纯耗散过程，但本研究结果表明其可以通过射线光学框架进行直观理解，为高热导率对比条件下的热流控制提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: The rapid growth of generative AI has intensified the need for efficient heat dissipation in large-scale data centers. To control heat flow, thermal metamaterials with layered structures have been widely used, which impart the anisotropic properties of thermal conductivities. However, the conventional effective medium approximation (EMA) often fails to provide accurate predictions in systems with a high thermal conductivity contrast between adjacent layers embedded in a background medium. Here, we generalize the EMA by introducing two corrective coefficients that extend its validity to regimes where the conventional EMA was previously inapplicable, i.e., high-contrast thermal metamaterials with the background medium. Notably, one of these coefficients that we proposed has the same mathematical form as the Fresnel reflection coefficient in optics. This allows us to interpret the "reflection-like" behavior of heat flow as it penetrates adjacent layers with high thermal contrast. Our findings suggest that heat diffusion, traditionally viewed as a purely dissipative process, can be understood intuitively through the framework of ray optics.

</details>


### [172] [Room-temperature graphene sub-terahertz detector integrated on a silicon dielectric waveguide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25895)
*A. Titchenko,K. Shein,M. Titova,M. Kashchenko,O. Popova,R. Izmaylov,E. I. Titova,I. Gayduchenko,G. Goltsman*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文首次展示了将石墨烯亚太赫兹探测器单片集成（monolithically integrated）在高阻硅介质波导上的室温工作器件，适用于D波段（110-170 GHz）操作，实现了11.5 V/W的电压响应度和1.94 GHz的3 dB带宽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太赫兹（THz）光子学是6G无线通信、片上光谱仪和生物医学传感器的关键平台；然而在太赫兹频段，低损耗介质波导上的室温单片集成检测至今仍未有解决方案，现有的混合倒装芯片（hybrid flip-chip assembly）集成方案存在模式耦合差、尺寸大和机械稳定性差等问题。

Method: 将hBN封装的石墨烯（graphene）沟道通过直接刻蚀在硅表面的锥形槽线天线（tapered slot-line antenna）与波导导模耦合，实现在高阻硅介质波导上的室温单片集成探测器。

Result: 探测器实现11.5 V/W的电压响应度（voltage responsivity）和1.94 GHz的3 dB带宽，带宽目前受限于读出互连的寄生电感（parasitic inductance）而非石墨烯本征响应。

Conclusion: 该平台为全集成室温太赫兹光子电路提供了实用路径，静电门控（electrostatic gating）和阻抗匹配（impedance matching）有望将响应度和带宽提升约一个数量级。

Abstract: Terahertz (THz) photonics offers a promising platform for next-generation 6G wireless communications, on-chip spectrometers, and non-invasive biomedical sensors. In the near- and mid-infrared, monolithically integrated photodetectors are a cornerstone of photonic integrated circuits (PICs). In the THz range, however, such integration on low-loss dielectric waveguides remains an open challenge: to date, room-temperature on-waveguide detection has been realized only through hybrid flip-chip assembly of discrete elements, which leads to poor mode coupling, larger footprint, and reduced mechanical stability. Here we address this gap by demonstrating a room-temperature graphene sub-THz detector monolithically integrated on a high-resistivity silicon dielectric waveguide for D-band (110-170 GHz) operation. We couple an hBN-encapsulated graphene channel to the guided mode using a tapered slot-line antenna patterned directly onto the silicon surface. The detector achieves a voltage responsivity of 11.5 V/W and a 3 dB bandwidth of 1.94 GHz, with the latter currently limited by parasitic inductance of the readout interconnects rather than by the intrinsic graphene response. This platform provides a practical pathway to fully integrated room-temperature THz photonic circuits, where electrostatic gating and impedance matching can improve both responsivity and bandwidth by roughly an order of magnitude.

</details>


### [173] [From rings to resonance: an inverse method links biophotonic structural color to inverse photonic glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25900)
*Florin Hemmann,Matthias Saba,Ullrich Steiner,Mauro S. Ferreira,Felipe A. Pinheiro*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种逆向方法（inverse method），通过将未知体系的反射光谱与计算机生成的随机光子网络模拟光谱数据库比对，来识别光谱中编码的结构特征。将该方法应用于模拟网络和象鼻虫Pachyrhynchus congestus mirabilis的蓝色生物光子网络，发现环（rings）和孔（pores）是关键的局域散射基元（scattering motifs），其特征尺寸决定反射峰位置，短程无序控制峰宽。结果表明蓝色结构色由局域散射机制主导而非光子带隙效应，并提出"逆光子玻璃"（inverse photonic glass）的概念为仿生结构色材料提供设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然界中无序光子网络（disordered photonic networks）是最复杂的天然光子结构之一，其结构-颜色之间的基本关系目前尚不明确。与周期性光子晶体和随机均匀球构成的光子玻璃不同，无序光子网络的光学响应机制缺乏系统性理解，亟需建立结构与颜色之间的定量对应关系。

Method: 采用逆向方法，将未知体系的反射率光谱与计算机生成的光子网络模拟光谱数据库进行比对，从而推断其结构特征。该方法应用于模拟网络以及象鼻虫Pachyrhynchus congestus mirabilis蓝色背光生物光子网络，通过分析光谱特征识别关键的局部散射结构。

Result: 识别出环（rings）和孔（pores）为关键局部散射基元，其特征尺寸决定反射峰的光谱位置，短程无序（short-range disorder）则控制峰宽。逆向方法揭示了短程有序的清晰光谱特征，而超均匀性（hyperuniformity）和原始相似性（primitive similarity）的影响相对较弱。这表明蓝色结构色由局域散射机制主导，而非光子带隙效应。

Conclusion: 提出"逆光子玻璃"（inverse photonic glass）的概念，将孔和环类比为关联的局域谐振子（correlated local resonators）。这一视角为仿生结构色材料提供了新的设计原则，有望指导具有特定颜色响应的新型人工光子材料的开发。

Abstract: Structural color arises from the interaction of light with nanoscale structures and is widespread in nature. As structural complexity increases, the mechanisms governing coloration become progressively less understood. The optical response of periodic photonic crystals with a spatially periodic refractive index is well described by Bloch theory, whereas that of photonic glasses composed of randomly assembled uniform spheres is more subtle yet well studied. In contrast, disordered photonic networks found in many beetles are among the most complex natural photonic architectures, and the fundamental relationships between their structure and color remain unclear. Here, we use an inverse method to identify the structural features encoded in the reflectance spectrum. By comparing the spectrum of an unknown system with a database of simulated spectra from computer-generated photonic networks, we infer its structural properties. Applying this approach to both simulated networks and the biophotonic network responsible for the blue coloration of the weevil Pachyrhynchus congestus mirabilis, we identify rings and pores as the key local scattering motifs. Their characteristic sizes govern the spectral position of the reflectance peak, whereas short-range disorder controls its width. The inverse method reveals clear spectral signatures of short-range order, whereas the influence of hyperuniformity and primitive similarity appears comparatively weak. This suggests that blue structural coloration is governed by local scattering mechanisms rather than photonic band-gap effects. We propose an analogy to an inverse photonic glass, in which pores and rings act as correlated local resonators. This perspective provides new design principles for bio-inspired structural-color materials.

</details>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [174] [PhysElite: How Far Are LLMs from Solving Olympiad-Level Physics Problems?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25097)
*Ruoran Xu,Wending Gao,Liyunfeng Chen,Aixin Shi,Haoyu Cheng,Zixiang Fang,Yiqiang Zou,Qiufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 PhysElite，一个大规模双语多模态奥林匹克级别物理推理基准，包含 11,586 道例题及其视觉图示、逐步中英双语解题过程和最终答案。对 18 个开源与闭源多模态大语言模型进行评测，最强模型准确率仅为 33.7%，并进一步开展步骤级过程评估以诊断推理链条中的失败环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理基准数据集存在两大不足：缺乏高难度题目，以及未能全面覆盖视觉形式、知识点和逐步解题过程，导致模型在现有数据集上的表现可能无法真实反映其解决复杂物理问题的能力。为弥补这一缺口，需要构建反映专家级物理推理难度与广度的新基准。

Method: 构建 PhysElite 基准数据集，包含 11,586 道奥林匹克级别（Olympiad-level）物理题目，每道题配有多模态视觉图示、逐步中英双语解题推导（step-by-step bilingual solution derivation）及最终答案。在此基础上对 18 个开源和闭源多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）进行答案准确率基准测试，并对推理链进行步骤级过程评估（step-level process evaluation）。

Result: 在 18 个被评测的多模态大语言模型中，表现最强的模型也仅达到 33.7% 的答案准确率，说明现有模型在奥林匹克级物理推理任务上仍存在显著局限。作者还通过步骤级评估识别了模型在推理链条中的具体失败模式。

Conclusion: PhysElite 为评估多模态大语言模型在复杂物理推理上的能力提供了一个更具挑战性和代表性的基准，同时揭示了当前最强模型在该任务上的明显不足。数据集已公开发布，可供后续研究使用。

Abstract: Understanding how (multimodal) large language models perform on physics problems requires benchmarks that reflect the difficulty and breadth of expert-level physical reasoning. Existing physics benchmarks remain limited in the following two important ways: (1) short of high-difficulty datasets, and (2) lack of comprehensive coverage of visual forms, knowledge points, and step-by-step solution processes. As a result, model performance on current datasets may not be fully representative of their ability to solve complex physics problems. To address these issues, we present PhysElite, a large-scale bilingual multimodal benchmark for Olympiad-level physics reasoning. PhysElite contains 11,586 Olympiad-tier problems. For each problem, we provide corresponding visual diagrams, step-by-step bilingual Chinese-English solution derivations, and the final answer. We benchmark 18 open-source and closed-source MLLMs, and find that even the strongest model reaches only 33.7% answer accuracy. We additionally conduct step-level process evaluation to diagnose where models fail in the reasoning chain. Our datasets are released at https://huggingface.co/datasets/physelite/PhysElite.

</details>


### [175] [SwarmWorld: Stigmergic technological evolution in societies of language-model agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26081)
*Subhadeep Pal,Fiona Y. Wang,Markus J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SwarmWorld框架，展示由初始同质的LLM代理（language-model agents）在无需预设角色或配方的情况下，通过共享空间环境自组织形成演化中的技术社会。代理在分离认知与后果的机制下探索环境、构建持久人工制品并编写可执行控制器，最终形成比独立搜索基线更广泛且更具韧性的技术组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多数多代理系统依赖直接对话、预定义角色或集中式工作流，尚不清楚去中心化代理能否在不依赖这些约束的条件下自主构建功能性技术并超越独立搜索的性能。本文旨在探索语言模型代理能否通过共享环境的协调机制涌现集体智能。

Method: 方法基于SwarmWorld模拟环境：初始同质的LLM代理在空间环境中探索、处理资源、测试材料、构建持久人工制品，并编写由确定性模拟器评估的可执行控制器。系统将认知与后果分离——代理在固定的动作和材料模式（schemas）内提出架构与控制器，由模拟世界决定功能。代理在代理移除后在未见过扰动下接受评估。研究还比较了显式文化机制与仅依赖物理stigmergy的条件。

Result: 共享社会开发出比强best-of-N独立搜索基线更广泛、更具韧性的技术组合，但独立搜索在最强人工制品上仍具竞争力。代理分化为探索、构建、维护和协调行为，并随世界成熟发生转变。技术通过协作构建、可执行代码继承及持久代理-人工制品网络累积，多数重用始于物理观察而非交流。

Conclusion: 显式文化机制能放大协作与组织，但其功能收益取决于结果与时间尺度。仅凭物理stigmergy即可支撑有能力的社会，而交互驱动的是持久的技术生态而非普遍更优秀的个体发明。

Abstract: Collective intelligence can emerge when individuals coordinate through a shared environment, allowing local actions to accumulate into durable social organization. Language-model agents offer a new substrate for this process, yet most multi-agent systems rely on direct conversation, predefined roles, or centralized workflows. It remains unclear whether decentralized agents can build functional technologies and outperform independent search. Here, initially homogeneous LLM agents in SwarmWorld self-organize without assigned roles or recipes into evolving technological societies. Agents explore a spatial environment, process resources, test materials, construct persistent artifacts, and write executable controllers evaluated by a deterministic simulator under unseen disturbances after the agents are removed. SwarmWorld splits cognition from consequence: agents propose architectures and controllers within fixed action and material schemas, while the simulated world determines function. Shared societies develop broader, more resilient technological portfolios than a strong best-of-N isolated-search baseline, although isolated search remains competitive for the strongest artifact. Agents differentiate into exploration, construction, maintenance, and coordination behaviors, transitioning as the world matures. Technologies accumulate through collaborative construction, executable inheritance, and persistent agent-artifact networks, with most reuse beginning through physical observation rather than communication. Explicit cultural mechanisms amplify collaboration and organization, but functional benefits depend on outcome and timescale. Physical stigmergy alone supports capable societies, while interaction drives persistent technological ecologies rather than universally superior individual inventions.

</details>

### [176] [Schrödinger Bridges over Kinetic Swarming Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25281)
*Asmaa Eldesoukey,Md Zulfiqur Haider,Italo Napolitano,Yongxin Chen,Abhishek Halder*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了受随机扰动影响的惯性群体（inertial swarm）在有限时间范围内的最小能量集体操控问题，基于薛定谔桥（Schrödinger bridge）理论，针对Cucker–Smale对齐和Morse吸引–排斥两种交互作用模型，推导了具有时间对称结构的非线性耦合最优性系统，并提出了嵌套不动点算法进行数值求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生物启发群体（bio-inspired swarm）中，仅靠交互作用往往无法在预设时间范围内将群体引导至期望的聚合构型，而这类需求在靶向治疗、集体运输和紧急疏散等应用中至关重要。因此需要引入额外的控制机制，实现有限时间内的群体定向操控。

Method: 基于薛定谔桥理论，将未受控的交互随机动力学视为先验模型，最优控制即为实现指定端点分布所需的最小能量修正漂移（corrective drift）。针对由Cucker–Smale对齐或Morse吸引–排斥交互驱动的多智能体系统均值场表示，推导了具有类经典薛定谔桥时间对称结构的非线性耦合最优性系统，并提出嵌套不动点（nested fixed-point）方案进行数值求解。

Result: 数值算例表明，所获得的最优控制（修正漂移）能够根据交互作用对操控任务有利还是对抗，动态地利用或抵消群体间的交互力。

Conclusion: 该形式化框架将薛定谔桥理论与交互式群体动力学相结合，为在随机扰动和交互作用下实现群体状态分布的有限时间精确操控提供了一条可行的理论路径。

Abstract: Paradigmatic interaction models explain how collective behaviors can emerge in complex systems from interactions among the constituent agents. In bio-inspired swarms, however, interactions alone may not suffice to bring the population to a desired aggregate configuration within a prescribed time horizon, as needed in applications ranging from targeted therapy to collective transport and emergency evacuation. In the present work, we consider finite-horizon minimum-energy collective steering for inertial swarms that are subject to stochastic disturbances. We focus on the mean-field representations of these multi-agent systems driven by Cucker--Smale alignment or Morse attraction--repulsion interactions. Our objective is to steer the swarm between prescribed endpoint distributions using a state-feedback control, where the endpoint specifications can be full phase-space distributions (positions and velocities) or position marginals alone. Our formalism is rooted in the theory of Schrödinger bridges, which has inspired contemporary developments spanning statistical inference, biological modeling, stochastic control, and generative learning. Within the bridges framework, the uncontrolled interacting stochastic dynamics are viewed as a prior model, and the optimal control as the minimum-energy corrective drift needed to realize the prescribed distributions. We derive nonlinear, coupled necessary optimality systems with a time-symmetric structure reminiscent of classical Schrödinger bridges, and propose nested fixed-point schemes to numerically solve them. Numerical examples show that the obtained optimal control (corrective drift) can dynamically exploit or counteract the interaction forces, depending on whether the latter are favorable or adversarial to the steering task.

</details>

# cond-mat.supr-con [[Back]](#toc)

### [177] [Even/Odd-parity STS spectra induced by quantum well mirror symmetry breaking in iron-based superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24906)
*Xiuqing Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了块体FeSe、单层FeSe和KCa₂Fe₄As₄F₂的超导扫描隧道谱（STS），提出量子阱（quantum well）机制统一解释镜像对称破缺、棋盘状子晶格有序、库珀对（Cooper pairing）、费米子-玻色子对偶性（fermion-boson duality）及半Bogoliubov态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定超导扫描隧道谱是否由晶体结构唯一决定，是凝聚态物理（condensed matter physics）中的根本性挑战。作者旨在寻找一个能够统一解释不同类型铁基超导体STS谱的普适机制。

Method: 系统研究三种体系——块体FeSe单晶、单层FeSe和KCa₂Fe₄As₄F₂，解析出一、二、三阶棋盘状量子阱结构，分别对应实验观测到的一、二、三对超导相干峰。提出普适的能隙标度律Δ(T,ξ)=η(T)/ξ²，其中η(T)为温度相关的前因子，ξ为量子阱深度。

Result: 在块体FeSe中，量子阱促进实空间库珀配对，形成简并反铁磁棋盘状子晶格，产生玻色型偶宇称STS响应；单层FeSe中镜像对称破缺抑制库珀配对，诱导非简并铁磁子晶格二分，产生费米型奇宇称STS谱。对KCa₂Fe₄As₄F₂预测的能隙对±6.2 meV、±5.6 meV和±4.2 meV与实验结果±6.2 meV、±5.4 meV和±4.4 meV高度吻合。

Conclusion: 量子阱机制统一解释了镜像对称破缺、棋盘子晶格有序、库珀配对、费米子-玻色子对偶性和半Bogoliubov态，为建立统一的高温超导理论提供了新见解。

Abstract: Determining whether superconducting scanning tunneling spectroscopy (STS) is uniquely dictated by crystal structure constitutes a fundamental challenge in condensed matter physics. Here, we systematically investigate bulk FeSe single crystals, monolayer FeSe, and $\mathrm{KCa_2Fe_4As_4F_2}$. We resolve one-, two-, and three-order checkerboard quantum-well structures that perfectly match the experimentally observed one, two, and three pairs of superconducting coherence peaks. In bulk FeSe, quantum wells promote real-space Cooper pairing and form degenerate antiferromagnetic checkerboard sublattices, yielding bosonic even-parity STS responses. In monolayer FeSe, mirror symmetry breaking suppresses Cooper pairing and induces nondegenerate ferromagnetic sublattice dichotomy, producing fermionic odd-parity STS spectra. We establish a universal gap scaling law $Δ(T, ξ) = η(T)/ξ^2$, where $η(T)$ is a temperature-dependent prefactor and $ξ$ denotes quantum-well depth that governs the number and magnitude of superconducting gaps. For $\mathrm{KCa_2Fe_4As_4F_2}$, our predicted gap pairs of $\pm6.2$ meV, $\pm5.6$ meV, and $\pm4.2$ meV are in excellent agreement with experimental results of $\pm6.2$ meV, $\pm5.4$ meV, and $\pm4.4$ meV. This quantum-well mechanism unifies mirror symmetry breaking, checkerboard sublattice ordering, Cooper pairing, fermion-boson duality, and half-Bogoliubov states for STS interpretation, offering new insights toward a unified high-$T_\text{c}$ superconductivity theory.

</details>


### [178] [Probing bulk superconductivity in centrosymmetric Te-doped PtBi$_2$ single crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25093)
*Kilian Srowik,Pablo Pedrazzini,Soumen Ash,Oksana Kvitnitskaya,Andrii Kuibarov,Susmita Changdar,Oleksandr Suvorov,Volodymyr Bezguba,Alexander Kordyuk,Rafał Kurleto,Dawid Wutke,Reza Firouzmandi,Robert Kluge,Swarnamayee Mishra,Alexander Mistonov,Saicharan Aswartham,Jochen Geck,Sergey Borisenko,Laura T. Corredor,Bernd Büchner*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了Te掺杂的γ-PtBi₂单晶（名义组分PtBi₁.₉₆Te₀.₀₄），确认其恢复了中心对称（centrosymmetric）的P\bar{3}m1结构，并在该结构中观察到临界温度约2.4 K的体超导电性，排除了非中心对称拓扑超导的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: γ-PtBi₂被提出为拓扑i波超导体，其物理实现的关键前提是P31m三角结构中缺少反演对称性（inversion symmetry）。然而，已有报道指出少量Te掺杂（约2%）即可使结构恢复中心对称性P\bar{3}m1，因此需要实验验证Te掺杂后样品的结构与超导性质。

Method: 合成了Te掺杂的PtBi_{2-x}Te_x样品，通过单晶X射线衍射（XRD）确认晶体结构；利用磁化强度（magnetization）和比热（specific heat）测量表征体超导电性；通过电阻（resistance）和点接触（point contact）实验进一步验证超导；使用角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）确认表面终止和费米弧（Fermi arcs）的存在性。

Result: XRD确认样品为具有中心对称性的P\bar{3}m1结构；超导体积分数约100%，临界温度T_c≈2.4 K；上临界场分别为面内H^{∥}_{c2}(0)≈6.3 kOe和面外H^{⊥}_{c2}(0)≈4.7 kOe；点接触测量显示T_c轻微提升至约3 K；ARPES显示单一表面终止且无费米弧，进一步佐证中心对称结构。

Conclusion: Te掺杂使PtBi₂恢复了中心对称的P\bar{3}m1结构，在该体系中观察到的超导电性属于中心对称体材料性质，不满足此前提出的拓扑i波超导所要求的非中心对称条件。

Abstract: The Weyl semimetal $γ$-PtBi$_2$ has been shown to be one of the most promising novel materials, recently proposed as a topological i-wave superconductor. A crucial requirement for observing this physics is the absence of inversion symmetry in its trigonal $P31m$ crystal structure. Centrosymmetry has been reported to be readily restored in the $P\overline{3}m1$ structure upon electron doping, partially substituting Bi with as little as 2% Te. In this work, we synthesized Te-doped PtBi$_{2-x}$Te$_{x}$ samples and thoroughly investigated the bulk superconductivity of selected single crystals with nominal composition PtBi$_{1.96}$Te$_{0.04}$, exhibiting the highest superconducting volume fraction of $\sim100$%. Single crystal XRD measurements confirm the centrosymmetric $P\overline{3}m1$ structure in our samples, whereas bulk superconductivity with a critical temperature of $T_\mathrm{c} \approx 2.4\,$K was observed in magnetization and specific heat measurements. The upper critical fields for two different orientations were determined as $H^{\parallel}_{\mathrm{c2}}(0)\approx 6.3\,$kOe for in-plane and $H^{\perp}_{\mathrm{c2}}(0)\approx 4.7\,$kOe for out-of-plane magnetic fields. Robust superconductivity was also found in resistance and point contact measurements, where the latter showed a slight enhancement of the critical temperature up to $T_\mathrm{c} \sim 3\,$K. Finally, ARPES measurements corroborate the centrosymmetric structure of our samples, showing a single surface termination and the absence of Fermi arcs.

</details>


### [179] [Grain Boundary Engineering Effect on Vortex Matter in Superconducting Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25268)
*Qun Wang,Ting Chen,Ya-Xun He,Xing-Jian Liu,Jian-Wen Sun,Kang-Hong Yin,Fang-Ting Lin,Shi-Xun Cao,Jun-Yi Ge*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本研究通过调控溅射压力（sputtering pressure）来调节NbTiN多晶超导薄膜中晶界（grain boundary）的耦合强度，并利用低温磁力显微镜（cryogenic magnetic force microscopy）等手段可视化其涡旋（vortex）组织行为，建立了沉积参数、晶界连接性与宏观弱连接输运及微观涡旋排列之间的直接联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多晶超导薄膜中的晶界对涡旋钉扎（vortex pinning）和超导电性具有双重作用，既能钉扎涡旋又可能通过类似约瑟夫森（Josephson）的弱连接效应降低超导性能；因此需要理解如何调控晶界耦合以优化涡旋钉扎性能。

Method: 通过改变NbTiN薄膜的溅射压力（5 mTorr与7 mTorr）来调节晶界耦合；使用电阻随场两步转变谱、晶粒取向分析、临界电流密度（critical current density）测量、第二磁峰（second magnetization peak）以及δl型钉扎响应等手段表征宏观性能；并利用低温磁力显微镜直接观察涡旋的空间分布。

Result: 5 mTorr薄膜呈现分散的晶粒取向、场下两步电阻转变及团簇状（cluster-like）涡旋排布，显示晶间弱连接行为；7 mTorr薄膜则形成(111)织构、单步转变、更高的临界电流密度、出现第二磁峰和δl型钉扎响应，并展现出更均匀、局部有序度更高的涡旋分布。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了由沉积条件调控的晶界连接性、宏观弱连接输运与微观涡旋组织三者之间的关联，为在多晶超导薄膜中工程化调控涡旋钉扎提供了实用途径。

Abstract: Grain boundaries (GBs) in polycrystalline superconducting films act as a double-edged sword: they can pin vortices or degrade superconductivity through Josephson-like weak-link coupling. Here, we demonstrate that sputtering pressure tunes GB coupling in NbTiN films and visualize its consequences for vortex matter. The 5 mTorr film exhibits dispersed grain orientations and a two-step resistive transition under field, signaling intergranular weak-link behavior. In contrast, the 7 mTorr film develops a (111) texture, a single-step transition, higher critical current density, a second magnetization peak, and a δl-type pinning response consistent with improved GB coupling. Cryogenic magnetic force microscopy reveals a spatially heterogeneous, cluster-like vortex configuration in the 5 mTorr film, whereas the 7 mTorr film hosts a more uniform distribution with enhanced local order. These results establish a connection between deposition-controlled GB connectivity, macroscopic weak-link transport, and microscopic vortex organization, providing a practical route to tailor vortex pinning in polycrystalline superconducting films.

</details>


### [180] [Distinct Surface and Bulk Superconductivity in the Kagome Superconductor SrSn$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25271)
*Qun Zhu,Yong-Wei Wang,Ji-Hai Zhang,Qiang-Jun Cheng,Chen-Yu Hu,Jun-Zhong Wang,Qi-Kun Xue,Xu-Cun Ma,Can-Li Song*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究通过可调隧道结电阻的扫描隧道谱学（scanning tunneling spectroscopy），在kagome超导体SrSn₃外延薄膜中区分出表面与体相两个独立的超导通道，二者在超导能隙、上临界场和涡核电子态上具有显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有非平庸电子结构的量子材料中，表面与体相超导电性可能具有根本不同的性质，但二者叠加的光谱特征往往阻碍对每个超导通道的直接实验观测。

Method: 采用扫描隧道谱学方法，通过调控隧道结电阻（tunneling junction resistance）来选择性探测表面与体相超导通道的谱学响应。

Result: 表面超导通道具有厚度无关的超导能隙和增强的上临界场，而体相通道具有更大的超导能隙（随膜厚减小而降低）和低得多的上临界场。磁涡核内仅在表面超导通道观察到稳健的非劈裂零偏压电导峰（zero-bias conductance peak），而体相通道始终伴随显著的零偏压抑制。

Conclusion: 研究表明涡核电子结构对底层超导通道具有敏感性，为拓扑量子材料中涡旋束缚态提供了新见解。

Abstract: Surface and bulk superconductivity may possess fundamentally different superconducting properties in quantum materials with nontrivial electronic structures, yet their superimposed spectroscopic signatures often prevent direct experimental access to each superconducting channel. Here we reveal, in epitaxial films of the kagome superconductor SrSn$_3$, distinct surface and bulk superconducting channels with markedly different superconducting gaps, upper critical fields, and vortex-core electronic states by tuning the tunneling junction resistance in scanning tunneling spectroscopy. The surface superconductivity is characterized by a thickness-independent superconducting gap and an enhanced upper critical field, whereas the bulk superconducting channel exhibits a larger superconducting gap that decreases with reducing film thickness and a much lower upper critical field. Within magnetic vortex cores, robust non-split zero-bias conductance peaks are observed exclusively in the surface superconducting channel, while pronounced zero-bias suppression is consistently associated with the bulk superconducting channel. These findings demonstrate that the vortex-core electronic structure depends sensitively on the underlying superconducting channel, providing new insight into vortex-bound states in topological quantum materials.

</details>


### [181] [Nearly Isotropic Quantum-Critical Transport in Single-Crystal CeNiC2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25616)
*Hanming Ma,Jun Gouchi,Dilip Bhoi,Toru Shigeoka,Bosen Wang,J. -G. Cheng,Yoshiya Uwatoko*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文报道了高质量CeNiC₂单晶各晶轴方向的压力依赖电阻率测量，在临界压力P_c≈9.5-10 GPa附近观察到沿所有晶轴近乎T线性的正常态电阻率、显著增强的剩余电阻率以及窄超导穹顶。各向同性演化表明该量子临界输运机制与简单的低维自旋涨落图像不符，价态涨落可能是与超导穹顶相关的临界散射的主要来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前人在多晶CeNiC₂中报道了压力诱导的超导性和T线性电阻率，但取向平均掩盖了临界散射的方向性特征，因此需要通过高质量单晶沿各晶轴的系统测量来解析其各向异性。

Method: 对高质量CeNiC₂单晶沿每个晶体学轴方向施加电流，测量压力依赖的电阻率；同时在不同磁场（沿a和c方向施加、最高9T）下系统测量I∥b方向的输运性质。

Result: 单晶具有显著更低的剩余电阻率和略高的最大超导起始温度T_c；在临界压力附近，所有晶轴的正常态电阻率均趋于T线性，剩余电阻率强烈增强，超导呈窄穹顶形态；I∥b方向的T线性正常态在高达9T磁场下几乎不变，上临界场较大且各向异性适中。

Conclusion: 三个轴向的共同演化确立了近乎各向同性的量子临界输运区域，与简单低维自旋涨落（spin-fluctuation）图像不符，暗示价态涨落（valence fluctuations）是伴随超导穹顶的临界散射的主要来源。

Abstract: Pressure-induced superconductivity and $T$-linear resistivity have been reported in polycrystalline CeNiC$_2$, but orientational averaging has left the directional character of the critical scattering unresolved. We report pressure-dependent resistivity of high-quality single crystals for current along each crystallographic axis. These crystals have substantially lower residual resistivity and a slightly higher maximum onset $T_c$ than the polycrystalline sample, placing superconductivity in a cleaner transport regime. Near $P_c \approx 9.5-10$ GPa, the normal-state resistivity becomes nearly $T$-linear along every axis, the fitted residual resistivity is strongly enhanced, and superconductivity forms a narrow dome. For $I \parallel b$, the $T$-linear normal state remains nearly unchanged in magnetic fields up to 9 T applied along $a$ and $c$; the upper critical field is large and only moderately anisotropic. The common evolution along all three axes establishes a nearly isotropic quantum-critical transport regime, inconsistent with a simple low-dimensional spin-fluctuation picture and implicates valence fluctuations as the leading source of critical scattering associated with the superconducting dome.

</details>


### [182] [Fluxtube Bouquets and Type-1.5 Clustering in Superfluid Neutron Star Cores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25943)
*Adarsh Karekkat,Gabriele Montefusco,Marco Antonelli*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究了中子星外核中耦合的中子超流体与质子超导体构成的介观构型，采用分量Ginzburg-Landau自由能描述凝聚体，通过准周期边界条件和约束相位印刻计算最小化自由能。研究发现局部吸引耦合下涡旋-磁通管重叠能量上有利，多个质子磁通管可围绕中子涡旋结合形成"磁通管花束"结构，并确认了在零拖曳效应（entrainment）情况下type-1.5类机制仍然存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解中子星外核中中子超流体与质子超导体共存时的介观构型及其相互作用机制，这对于揭示中子星内部的耗散耦合和输运过程具有重要意义。此前研究尚未系统探讨局部耦合下涡旋与磁通管之间的聚集行为，以及type-1.5类机制在该体系中的存在性。

Method: 采用包含局部耦合项（neglecting genuine phase-gradient entrainment）的双分量Ginzburg-Landau自由能描述凝聚体，在二维空间中使用准周期边界条件（quasi-periodic boundary conditions）和约束相位印刻计算（constrained phase-imprinting calculations）对自由能进行最小化，用以研究涡旋-磁通管（vortex-fluxtube）和磁通管-磁通管（fluxtube-fluxtube）的相互作用。

Result: 在自由能呈局部吸引耦合时，涡旋-磁通管重叠在能量上更为有利，多个已存在的质子磁通管可以围绕单个中子涡旋结合，形成有限尺寸的涡旋中心聚集结构（称为"磁通管花束"）。这种花束构型可能变得非常致密，使得单个涡旋能够有效容纳多个磁通量子。同时，研究确认了type-1.5类（type-1.5-like）机制的存在，且该机制在零拖曳效应情况下仍然成立；在该机制下，磁通管-磁通管相互作用在短距离表现为排斥、在中等距离表现为吸引，导致自组装团簇的形成，而单个磁通管仍保持拓扑独立性。

Conclusion: 揭示了中子星外核中涡旋与磁通管之间复杂的聚集行为及其背后的相互竞争机制（短程排斥与中程吸引），并讨论了这些结构对中子星内部耗散耦合（dissipative coupling）和输运（transport）过程的可能影响。

Abstract: We study mesoscopic configurations of a neutron superfluid coupled to a proton superconductor in the outer core of a neutron star. The condensates are described by a two-component Ginzburg-Landau free energy with local couplings, neglecting genuine phase-gradient entrainment. In two spatial dimensions, we minimize the free energy using quasi-periodic boundary conditions and constrained phase-imprinting calculations to study vortex-fluxtube and fluxtube-fluxtube interactions. We find that, for locally attractive couplings in the free energy, vortex-fluxtube overlap is energetically favoured and several pre-existing proton fluxtubes can bind around a neutron vortex, forming finite vortex-centred aggregates that we call fluxtube bouquets. These bouquet configurations may become so dense that a vortex can effectively accommodate several quanta of magnetic flux. We also confirm the possible presence of a type-1.5-like regime and find that it survives in the zero-entrainment regime considered here. In this type-1.5 regime, the fluxtube-fluxtube interaction is repulsive at short distances and attractive at intermediate distances, leading to self-assembled clusters while the individual fluxtubes remain topologically distinct. Possible implications for dissipative coupling and transport in neutron stars are discussed.

</details>


### [183] [Symmetry Origins of the Field-Free Superconducting Diode Effect in the Kagome Superconductor CsV$_3$Sb$_5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26022)
*Xin-Jie Liu,Shengbiao Sun,Ke-Fan Song,Jia-Peng Peng,Xilin Feng,Lang Xiao,Tong Liu,Qilin Han,Ya-Qing Bie,Ning Kang,Xiaosong Wu,Yanfei Wu,Shouguo Wang,Kam Tuen Law,Shuo Wang,Dapeng Yu,Ben-Chuan Lin*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过实验研究表明，未经过处理的纯净CsV₃Sb₅器件本身不表现出无场超导二极管效应（field-free superconducting diode effect），但表面氧化或不对称刻蚀可激活大幅度的非互易超流响应。该响应具有随机性，其极性与大小随扫描过程变化，反映了亚稳态的时间反演对称破缺（TRS breaking）畴构型；而通过Ti掺杂抑制长程电荷密度波（CDW）有序后，超导二极管效应消失，表明超导态的时间反演对称破缺与CDW相关有序存在联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无场超导二极管效应同时要求反演对称破缺（inversion-symmetry breaking）与内部时间反演对称破缺，因而是探测超导体中隐藏有序的灵敏探针。然而，呈中心对称晶格的笼目（kagome）超导体AV₃Sb₅原则上不应出现该效应；同时，此前在CsV₃Sb₅超导区观察到的时间反演对称破缺究竟源于超导本身还是继承自CDW有序，这一问题仍悬而未决，亟需实验厘清。

Method: 制备纯净及经表面氧化或不对称刻蚀处理的CsV₃Sb₅器件，通过测量无场条件下的超导二极管响应及其磁场依赖行为，系统考察其极性和大小随扫描过程的变化；同时利用Ti掺杂抑制长程CDW有序，对比研究超导二极管效应的存续情况，以判定超导态时间反演对称破缺的来源。

Result: 纯净CsV₃Sb₅器件不表现出内在的无场超导二极管效应，而表面氧化或不对称刻蚀可激活显著的非互易超流行为；该响应具有随机性，极性和大小随扫描变化，暗示存在亚稳态的时间反演对称破缺畴构型。小面外磁场能够稳定超导二极管响应，支持磁场对畴的选择作用；Ti掺杂抑制长程CDW有序后，超导二极管效应消失。

Conclusion: 实验结果确立了CsV₃Sb₅中无场超导二极管效应的对称性要求，揭示其具有随机畴控制（stochastic domain-controlled）的特征，并将超导态的时间反演对称破缺与CDW相关有序联系起来，为理解该体系中的隐藏有序提供了关键实验证据。

Abstract: Field-free superconducting diode effects require both inversion-symmetry breaking and an internal time-reversal-symmetry (TRS) breaking field, making them sensitive probes of hidden order in superconductors. In centrosymmetric kagome AV$_3$Sb$_5$, the inversion symmetry generally should generally preclude the observation of the superconducting diode effect. Furthermore, though TRS breaking has been reported in the superconducting regime of CsV$_3$Sb$_5$, whether it is generated by superconductivity or inherited from charge-density-wave (CDW) order remains unresolved. Here we show that pristine CsV$_3$Sb$_5$ devices exhibit no intrinsic field-free superconducting diode effect, whereas surface oxidation or asymmetric etching activates a large nonreciprocal supercurrent. Moreover, the response is stochastic, with sweep-dependent polarity and magnitude, indicating metastable TRS-breaking domain configurations. Small out-of-plane magnetic fields stabilize the superconducting diode response, consistent with field selection of such domains. Finally, when long-range CDW order is suppressed by Ti doping, the SDE disappears. Our results establish the symmetry requirements for the field-free SDE in CsV$_3$Sb$_5$, reveal its stochastic domain-controlled character, and link superconducting-state TRS breaking to CDW-related order.

</details>


### [184] [Electrical manipulation of oxygen stoichiometry in multiterminal YBa$_2$Cu$_3$O$_{7-δ}$ junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26045)
*Daniel Stoffels,Caio C. Quaglio-Gomes,Nicolas Lejeune,Emile Fourneau,Pedro Schio,Huidong Li,Lourdes Fabrega,Anna Palau,Maycon Motta,Alejandro V. Silhanek*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在YBa₂Cu₃O₇₋δ Y形三端器件中，通过电流极性选择性地控制单个端口的氧空位迁移，利用光学反射率变化直接可视化氧耗尽传播前沿，并通过多种表征手段建立氧含量与超导转变温度（Tc）之间的定量联系，为超导多端器件的局部后加工调控开辟了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部操控氧化学计量比（oxygen stoichiometry）可调控复杂氧化物（complex oxides）的电子性质，但在多端高温超导结中，通过氧再分布选择性修改单个载流支路尚未被探索，该研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在Y形三端YBa₂Cu₃O₇₋δ器件上施加特定极性的电流以驱动氧空位迁移；通过光学反射率（optical reflectivity）变化直接可视化氧耗尽传播前沿，并结合开尔文探针力显微镜（Kelvin Probe Force Microscopy）和扫描激光显微镜（Scanning Laser Microscopy）分别探测功函数变化与超导转变的空间分辨演变；采用有限元建模（finite-element modeling）和纳米探针X射线衍射（nanoprobe X-ray diffraction）对空位再分布进行定性复现。

Result: 氧迁移过程高度定向，取决于施加电流的极性，可在Y形器件的中心节点处创建汇聚或发散两种传播前沿；电阻变化在分钟时间尺度上弛豫，由空位浓度梯度驱动；扫描激光显微镜揭示的Tc对比提供了氧含量的定量标尺，可实现对氧再分布的直接可视化。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了在超导多端器件制备完成后局部调控其性能的后加工途径，适用于纳米低温管（nanocryotrons）、yTron及可调弱连接（tunable weak links）等器件。

Abstract: Local manipulation of oxygen stoichiometry offers a route to control the electronic properties of complex oxides, yet the selective modification of individual current-carrying branches through oxygen redistribution remains unexplored in multiterminal high-temperature superconducting junctions. In a YBa$_2$Cu$_3$O$_{7-δ}$ Y-shaped three-terminal device, we demonstrate the possibility to electrically control oxygen vacancy migration on a hand-picked terminal while largely preserving the other two. Oxygen-depleted propagating fronts are directly visualized by the resulting change in optical reflectivity and they are linked to the evolution of the electrical response. The process is highly directional and determined by the polarity of the applied current, allowing for the creation of either a converging or a diverging propagating front from the central node of the Y-shaped device. The associated changes in resistance exhibit relaxation on a timescale of minutes, driven by the vacancy concentration gradient. Effects of oxygen migration are also mapped by Kelvin Probe Force Microscopy and Scanning Laser Microscopy, which probe work-function changes and spatially resolved variations in the superconducting transition, respectively. Notably, the Tc contrast revealed by the latter provides a quantitative handle on the underlying oxygen content, enabling direct visualization of oxygen redistribution. Finite-element modeling and nanoprobe X-ray diffraction qualitatively reproduce the observed vacancy redistribution. These results establish a post-fabrication route to locally tune properties of superconducting multiterminal devices such as nanocryotrons, yTron, and tunable weak links.

</details>
