### [185] [Laplace surface of eccentric orbits around Kerr black hole and black-hole effects on Lidov-Kozai cycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25353)
*Haonan Quan,Xing Wei*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文研究了克尔黑洞（Kerr black hole）加伴星系统中偏心轨道的拉普拉斯面（Laplace surface），分析了相对论效应与伴星潮汐效应的平衡条件，并进一步研究黑洞效应对利道夫-科扎伊循环（Lidov-Kozai cycles）的影响，最终将该机制应用于解释银河系中心S星团（S-cluster）近各向同性的轨道取向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强引力场环境中天体轨道动力学行为，特别是相对论效应与外部伴星潮汐效应共同作用下的轨道演化规律，并利用这些规律解释观测到的银河系中心恒星轨道分布特征。

Method: 作者围绕克尔黑洞与伴星共存系统，考虑两种相对论效应（近心点进动apsidal precession和伦斯-瑟林进动Lense-Thirring precession）与伴星潮汐效应的平衡，通过求解长期摄动方程（secular equations）研究拉普拉斯面的平衡解及其稳定性，并分析黑洞效应对利道夫-科扎伊循环的影响。

Result: 发现平衡解的两个分支仅存在于测试粒子轨道倾角的一个狭窄范围内；当伴星倾斜角（obliquity）接近90度时系统稳定。利道夫-科扎伊循环频率关于离黑洞距离呈非单调变化，并在广义相对论近心点进动与伴星潮汐进动之间的转变区域达到最小值；黑洞效应还会抑制循环振幅。

Conclusion: 该机制能够很好地解释银河系中心S星团近似各向同性的轨道取向分布，表明黑洞相对论效应与伴星潮汐效应共同作用可有效重塑恒星轨道结构。

Abstract: We study the Laplace surface of eccentric orbits around a Kerr black hole with a companion star. The two relativistic effects (periapsis apsidal precession and Lense-Thirring precession) balance companion's tidal effect. By solving the secular equations, we find that the two branches of equilibrium solutions exist only in a narrow range of orbital inclinations of test particle, and that the stability exists when companion's obliquity approaches 90 degree. We then study the black-hole effects on Lidov-Kozai cycles. In addition to the black-hole suppression on cycle amplitude, we find that the cycle frequency is non-monotonic about distance from black hole and reaches its minimum in the transition between GR apsidal precession and companions's tidal precession. Finally we use this mechanism to well interpret the nearly isotropic orientation of S-cluster in the Galactic center.

</details>


### [186] [Revealing Galactic dust beneath the cosmic infrared background anisotropies with Wavelet Phase Harmonics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25617)
*Srijita Sinha,Tuhin Ghosh,Erwan Allys,François Boulanger,Jean-Marc Delouis*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种基于小波相位谐波统计量（Wavelet Phase Harmonics, WPH）的成分分离算法，用于从宇宙红外背景（CIB）各向异性中提取银河系尘埃发射，并在Planck 353 GHz频段的三个高银纬区域成功实现了尘埃信号与CIB的分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银河系尘埃发射与宇宙红外背景各向异性具有相似的光谱特性，这使得两者难以区分，因此需要开发一种有效的成分分离算法来提取尘埃信号。

Method: 利用WPH统计量开发成分分离算法，应用于Planck 353 GHz频段的三个高银纬天区（覆盖不同中性氢柱密度范围），并与利用尘埃-HI线性相关关系的标准模板拟合方法进行性能对比。

Result: 在三个天区均成功恢复了尘埃信号，且尘埃与CIB各向异性成分之间无明显泄漏；低信噪比区域的算法性能通过了与模板拟合方法的对比验证；在中等和高信噪比区域，分离出的尘埃信号图在保持与HI柱密度图统计相关性的同时，成功去除了污染。

Conclusion: 基于WPH的成分分离算法能够有效提取银河系尘埃发射，并可用于分析尘埃发射相对于氢柱密度随角尺度变化的空间特性。

Abstract: Separating Galactic dust emission from cosmic infrared background (CIB) anisotropies is challenging because both the emission components have similar spectral properties. The primary objective of this work is to develop a component-separation algorithm to extract the Galactic dust emission from CIB anisotropies dominated sky regions using Wavelet Phase Harmonics statistics (WPH). We apply our algorithm to the \textit{Planck} $353\,\mathrm{GHz}$ frequency band over three sky regions spanning a range of neutral hydrogen ($\text{HI}$) column densities at high Galactic latitudes. In all three regions, we successfully recover the dust signal without significant leakage between the dust and CIB anisotropy components. We use the low signal-to-noise ($\mathrm{S/N}$) region to validate the component-separation algorithm and compare its performance with the standard template-fit approach that uses the linear dust-$\text{HI}$ correlation relation. For intermediate and high $\mathrm{S/N}$ regions, we separate the dust signal map from the contamination while preserving the statistical correlation with $\text{HI}$ column density map. Dust maps are used to analyze the relationship between dust and $\text{HI}$ emissions. We characterize the spatial variations in dust emission relative to the hydrogen column density as a function of the angular scale.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.quant-gas'></div>

# cond-mat.quant-gas [[Back]](#toc)

### [187] [Dynamical development of long-range spatial coherence in non-equilibrium bosonic condensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25407)
*Bianca Rae Fabricante,Dąbrówka Biegańska,Paolo Comaron,Mateusz Król,Matthias Wurdack,Maciej Pieczarka,Mark Steger,David W. Snoke,Kenneth West,Daniele Sanvitto,Dario Ballarini,Dimitrios Trypogeorgos,Loren N. Pfeiffer,Andrew G. Truscott,Marzena Szymańska,Elena A. Ostrovskaya,Eliezer Estrecho*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本研究通过时间分辨实验和数值模拟，揭示了激子极化激元（exciton-polariton）凝聚体在非平衡驱动-耗散条件下形成过程中的空间相干性（spatial coherence）演化机制，发现其经历两个不同阶段：早期相互作用驱动的暂态相干振荡阶段，以及随后覆盖整个系统的空间均匀高相干稳态阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色凝聚（bosonic condensation）中自发相干性的建立是其核心特征之一，然而虽然稳态凝聚体的空间相干性已被充分理解，凝聚体形成过程中相干性的时间演化在很大程度上尚未被探索。这一空白构成了本研究的核心动机。

Method: 研究同时采用时间分辨实验（time-resolved experiments）与数值建模（numerical modeling）两种方法，系统考察非平衡、驱动-耗散型激子极化激元凝聚体的动力学形成过程，并比较自由流动与受限两种系统中经历淬火（quench，即凝聚体从两种不同初始条件快速生长）时的相干性演化行为。

Result: 研究发现空间相干性的建立包含两个截然不同的阶段：早期阶段由相互作用驱动，表现为空间相干性的瞬时振荡；随后进入稳态区域，其特征为空间均匀且高度相干的态，该相干性覆盖整个系统尺寸并随时间持续。上述两个阶段在自由流动和受限的激子极化激元系统中均出现。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解非平衡玻色凝聚体中长程空间相干性（long-range spatial coherence）的建立过程提供了深入洞察，揭示了相互作用驱动的暂态演化与稳态相干性形成之间的内在关联。

Abstract: Development of spontaneous coherence is one of the hallmarks of bosonic condensation in a variety of physical systems, such as cold atoms, confined photons, and hybrid light-matter quasiparticles like exciton polaritons in semiconductors. While spatial coherence is well understood once a steady-state condensate has been established, its temporal evolution as the condensate forms is largely unexplored. Here, we explore the dynamical formation of a non-equilibrium, driven-dissipative exciton-polariton condensate through both time-resolved experiments and numerical modeling. Our study reveals that the spatial coherence is established through two distinct stages. The early-time stage is interaction-driven and features transient oscillations in spatial coherence. This stage is followed by a steady-state regime characterized by a spatially-uniform high degree of coherence that extends over the entire size of the system and persists over time. These stages of spatial coherence development occur in both free flowing and confined exciton-polariton systems that undergo a quench - rapid growth of the condensate starting from two different initial settings. Our study offers a deep insight into the process by which long-range spatial coherence is established in a non-equilibrium bosonic condensate.

</details>


### [188] [Mass-anisotropy driven stripe pattern formation and directional modulational instability in polariton condensate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25825)
*Hari Sadhan Ghosh,Soumyadeep Halder,Subrata Das,Sonjoy Majumder*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了质量各向异性（mass anisotropy）对驱动-耗散型极化激元凝聚体中调制不稳定性（modulational instability）、条纹图案形成（stripe pattern formation）及其集体动力学的影响，发现通过控制有效质量比可在非均匀泵浦条件下操控图案的方向选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期实验在电控有效质量与各向异性质量分布的实现方面取得进展，为研究质量各向异性如何影响非平衡极化激元凝聚体的动力学行为提供了新契机。

Method: 对均匀泵浦系统进行Bogoliubov分析，考察质量各向异性对不稳定模式色散关系的影响；并通过对非共振高斯激光束泵浦的凝聚体进行数值模拟，研究质量比高于临界值时条纹图案的形成与演化。

Result: 质量各向异性不改变调制不稳定性判据，但可能重塑不稳定模式的色散；沿较大有效质量方向的最不稳定波长随质量比增大而减小，引入各向异性控制的图案选择长度尺度。当泵浦宽度与该波长可比时，凝聚体沿大质量方向发展出条纹图案，伴随粒子数涨落与质心振荡；泵浦宽度进一步增大时条纹畸变产生空间不规则性，但仍保持显著的方向偏好，不同于各向同性情形的完全无序图案。

Conclusion: 质量各向异性被确认为非平衡极化激元凝聚体中方向性调制不稳定性和图案形成的关键控制参数。

Abstract: Motivated by recent experimental advances toward electrical control of the effective mass and the realization of anisotropic mass distributions, we investigate the impact of mass anisotropy on driven-dissipative polariton condensates, focusing on modulational instability, pattern formation, and the collective dynamics of the emergent patterns. We show that unequal effective masses along the two orthogonal directions induce stripe pattern formation in an exciton-polariton condensate pumped by a non-resonant Gaussian laser beam above a critical mass ratio. A Bogoliubov analysis of the homogeneously pumped system reveals that mass anisotropy leaves the modulational-instability criteria unchanged but reshapes the unstable-mode dispersion by reducing the most unstable wave- length along the direction of larger effective mass with increasing mass ratio. This introduces an anisotropy-controlled length scale for pattern selection. When the pump width becomes comparable to the most unstable wavelength, the condensate becomes modulationally unstable and develops stripe patterns along the direction of larger effective mass, accompanied by particle-number fluctua- tions and center-of-mass oscillations along the same direction. As the pump width increases further, the stripes become distorted and develop spatial irregularities during the time evolution, indicative of modulational instability. Nevertheless, the anisotropic mass distribution preserves a pronounced directional preference, in contrast to the fully disordered patterns observed in the isotropic-mass case. Our results identify mass anisotropy as a key control parameter for directional modulational instability and pattern formation in nonequilibrium polariton condensates.

</details>


### [189] [Exact analytical spectrum, eigenstates, and quantum geometry of the quarter-flux Harper-Hofstadter model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26099)
*Isaac Tesfaye,André Eckardt*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该论文为四能带Harper-Hofstadter模型（四分之一磁通）提供了能谱与量子几何张量（QGT）的闭式表达式。通过揭示模型的子晶格对称性，作者将Bloch哈密顿量变为反对角块结构，由此解析导出能谱和本征态，并进一步给出包括Berry曲率和量子度量在内的完整QGT闭式形式，最终评估了分数陈绝缘体稳定性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子几何（quantum geometry）已成为原子物理和凝聚态物理中的指导性原则，决定了Bloch带的拓扑响应及其承载的相关相稳定性。然而，除两带模型之外，能谱和量子几何的闭式解非常稀少，因此需要为更复杂的多带模型提供解析框架。

Method: 作者首先证明该模型具有子晶格对称性（sublattice symmetry），使Bloch哈密顿量呈反对角块结构，从而解析导出能谱和本征态。在此基础上，推导了子晶格对称系统中量子几何张量的一般表达式，并将其分解为各子晶格扇区的贡献，进而获得该模型所有能带的完整QGT闭式解。

Result: 论文得到了四分之一磁通Harper-Hofstadter模型的能谱与完整量子几何张量的闭式表达式，覆盖所有能带的Berry曲率和量子度量；并解析地评估了分数陈绝缘体（fractional Chern insulator）稳定性判据，量化表明该模型最低能带是接近理想的陈带（Chern band）。

Conclusion: 该工作为Harper-Hofstadter模型在四分之一磁通下的量子几何提供了完整的解析描述，验证了该模型最低能带几乎理想的陈带性质，为其在超冷原子、光子与超导电路等实验平台上的分数陈绝缘体研究提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Quantum geometry has emerged as a guiding principle across atomic and condensed-matter physics, shaping the topological responses of Bloch bands and the stability of the correlated phases they host. Beyond two-band models, however, closed-form expressions for both the spectrum and the quantum geometry are rare. Here we provide such expressions for a paradigmatic four-band model that has recently been realized experimentally with ultracold atoms, photons and in superconducting circuits: the Harper-Hofstadter model at quarter flux, describing charged particles on a two-dimensional square lattice subjected to a uniform magnetic field. We achieve this by first showing that the model possesses a sublattice symmetry, which renders its Bloch Hamiltonian anti-block-diagonal allowing us to derive the spectrum and the eigenstates analytically. From that we also obtain closed-form expressions for the full quantum geometric tensor (QGT), including both the Berry curvature and the quantum metric, for all the bands of the model. For this purpose we first derive a general expression for the QGT for sublattice-symmetric systems in terms of contributions from the individual sublattice sectors. Finally, we evaluate fractional-Chern-insulator stability criteria analytically and quantify the lowest band of the quarter-flux Harper-Hofstadter model to be a nearly ideal Chern band.

</details>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [190] [Topology of low-dimensional generalized Ricci solitons and string backgrounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25829)
*Jeffrey Streets*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文基于Perelman和Wei等人已有的思想与方法，证明了紧致广义Ricci孤子（generalized Ricci soliton, GRS）具有正的Yamabe不变量，并基于Bakry-Émery Ricci曲率的分裂定理推导出针对GRS的Cheeger-Gromoll型分裂定理。以此为基础，文章确定了低维GRS的微分同胚分类，并系统刻画了各类弦论背景（如Bismut-Hermitian-Einstein流形、强挠率G2与Spin(7)-流形）上的拓扑约束，证明多数情况下这些结构与其经典特殊全纯结构与不可能共存在同一流形上。最后，借助Kollár关于Seifert纤维化五维流形特征的推广，文章在自然约束下确定了BHE三维流形的拓扑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义Ricci孤子（generalized Ricci soliton）在微分几何与弦论中具有重要地位，但对其整体拓扑性质的系统刻画仍不充分。本文旨在借助既有分裂定理与几何分析工具，建立GRS紧致情形下的拓扑分类，并探讨弦论背景下若干特殊几何结构的共存限制，从而为理解这些几何对象的全局结构提供统一框架。

Method: 本文借鉴Akutagawa针对Perelman工作的思想，证明紧致GRS具有正的Yamabe不变量。作者利用Wei关于Bakry-Émery Ricci曲率的分裂定理（splitting theorem）作为推论，获得GRS的Cheeger-Gromoll型分裂结果，进而对低维GRS进行分类。在弦论背景部分，作者通过拓扑约束对Bismut-Hermitian-Einstein（BHE）、强挠率G2与强挠率Spin(7)-流形逐一分析，并借助Kollár关于Seifert纤维化五维流形特征的扩展来确定BHE三维流形的拓扑。

Result: 主要结果包括：紧致广义Ricci孤子具有正Yamabe不变量；低维GRS微分同胚于S³/Γ或S³×S¹/Γ；弦论背景下各类特殊几何结构（BHE、强挠率G2、强挠率Spin(7)-流形）在大多数情况下与其经典特殊全纯结构对应的流形不能共存；在自然约束下，BHE三维流形的拓扑得到完整确定。

Conclusion: 本文建立了紧致广义Ricci孤子拓扑性质的一般性结论，并系统揭示了弦论背景下若干几何结构的拓扑不相容性。这些结果不仅深化了对GRS整体结构及其分类问题的理解，也为弦论中特殊几何背景的流形选择提供了明确的拓扑限制。

Abstract: Adapting ideas of \cite{akutagawa2007perelman}, we show that compact generalized Ricci solitons (GRS) have positive Yamabe invariant. We observe a Cheeger-Gromoll-type splitting theorem for GRS as a corollary of the splitting theorem for Bakry-Émery Ricci curvature in \cite{wei2009comparison}. Using this we show that low dimensional GRS are diffeomorphic to $S^3 / Γ$ or $S^3 \times S^1 / Γ$. We determine various topological constraints on string backgrounds (Bismut-Hermitian-Einstein (BHE), strong torsion $G_2$, strong torsion $\mathrm{Spin}(7)$-manifolds) and show in most cases that they cannot exist on the same manifolds as their classical special holonomy counterparts. Finally we determine the topology of BHE threefolds under natural constraints, relying on an extension of parts of Kollar's characterization of Seifert fibered $5$-manifolds over complex orbifolds \cite{kollar2006circle}.

</details>
